`src-tauri/src/app.rs`:

```rs
//! What the overlay should be doing, decided from what happened. Pure: it
//! takes events and returns commands for the platform thread, so every rule
//! here is tested without a screen.

use crate::core::center::{centred, Area};
use crate::core::crosshair::{draw, to_svg, Crosshair};
use crate::core::focus::{self, FocusState, WindowInfo};
use crate::core::launchers::{game_at, InstalledGame};
use crate::core::raster::{rasterize, slab};
use crate::platform::{Command, Event, MouseButton};
use crate::store::{AimMode, Game, Settings};

/// The settings window's title; Scorpio's other windows (the tray's) never
/// count as being in front.
pub const SETTINGS_TITLE: &str = "Scorpio";

/// Space around the crosshair on the unlocked slab, at least.
const SLAB_PAD: u32 = 40;
const SLAB_MIN: u32 = 132;

/// The display the crosshair is centred on, physical pixels.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Display {
    pub x: i32,
    pub y: i32,
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
}

impl Default for Display {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Display { x: 0, y: 0, width: 1920, height: 1080 }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Hotkey {
    Lock,
    Center,
    Hide,
    Next,
    Previous,
    AddGame,
}

impl Hotkey {
    pub fn from_id(id: &str) -> Option<Hotkey> {
        Some(match id {
            "lock" => Hotkey::Lock,
            "center" => Hotkey::Center,
            "hide" => Hotkey::Hide,
            "next" => Hotkey::Next,
            "previous" => Hotkey::Previous,
            "addGame" => Hotkey::AddGame,
            _ => return None,
        })
    }
}

/// What a step changed besides the overlay.
#[derive(Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct Effects {
    pub commands: Vec<Command>,
    /// The settings changed and should be saved and shown.
    pub settings_changed: bool,
    /// Something the settings window shows changed (focus, lock, hidden).
    pub status_changed: bool,
}

/// What the overlay currently shows, to send a command only on a change.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
enum Shown {
    Hidden,
    Crosshair { crosshair: Crosshair, display: Display, offset: (i32, i32), unlocked: bool },
}

pub struct App {
    pub settings: Settings,
    pub installed: Vec<InstalledGame>,
    pub own_pid: u32,
    pub display: Display,
    pub focus: FocusState,
    pub front: Option<WindowInfo>,
    /// The last window in front that was not Scorpio's, for "add this game".
    pub last_outside: Option<WindowInfo>,
    pub locked: bool,
    pub hidden: bool,
    aiming: bool,
    shown: Shown,
    listening: Option<Vec<MouseButton>>,
    /// The window in front changed since the overlay was last raised.
    raise: bool,
    capture_hidden: Option<bool>,
    saved_index: Option<usize>,
}

impl App {
    pub fn new(settings: Settings, own_pid: u32) -> App {
        App {
            settings,
            installed: Vec::new(),
            own_pid,
            display: Display::default(),
            focus: FocusState { visible: false, game: None },
            front: None,
            last_outside: None,
            locked: true,
            hidden: false,
            aiming: false,
            shown: Shown::Hidden,
            listening: None,
            raise: false,
            capture_hidden: None,
            saved_index: None,
        }
    }

    /// The crosshair for what is in front: the game's own, else the main one.
    pub fn active_crosshair(&self) -> &Crosshair {
        self.focus
            .game
            .and_then(|index| self.settings.games.get(index))
            .and_then(|game| game.crosshair.as_ref())
            .unwrap_or(&self.settings.crosshair)
    }

    fn active_crosshair_mut(&mut self) -> &mut Crosshair {
        let game = self.focus.game.filter(|index| self.settings.games.get(*index).is_some_and(|game| game.crosshair.is_some()));
        match game {
            Some(index) => self.settings.games[index].crosshair.as_mut().expect("checked above"),
            None => &mut self.settings.crosshair,
        }
    }

    fn detected(&self, window: &WindowInfo) -> bool {
        let detect = &self.settings.detect;
        (detect.fullscreen && focus::fullscreen_key(window).is_some())
            || (detect.launchers && !window.path.is_empty() && game_at(&window.path, &self.installed).is_some())
    }

    fn refocus(&mut self) {
        let saved: Vec<_> = self.settings.games.iter().map(|game| game.fingerprint.clone()).collect();
        let hidden = FocusState { visible: false, game: None };
        let next = match self.front.clone() {
            // The settings window shows the crosshair so it can be tuned, for
            // the game last in front, worked out again so a removed game
            // never leaves a stale index behind.
            Some(window) if window.pid == self.own_pid && window.title == SETTINGS_TITLE => {
                let behind = self.last_outside.clone();
                let base = focus::next_state(&hidden, behind.as_ref(), self.own_pid, &saved, |w| self.detected(w));
                FocusState { visible: true, game: base.game }
            }
            // The tray menu and other windows of Scorpio's own change nothing.
            Some(window) if window.pid == self.own_pid => return,
            front => focus::next_state(&self.focus, front.as_ref(), self.own_pid, &saved, |w| self.detected(w)),
        };
        if !next.visible || next.game != self.focus.game {
            self.aiming = false;
        }
        self.focus = next;
    }

    fn should_show(&self) -> bool {
        !self.locked || (self.focus.visible && !self.hidden && !(self.settings.aim.hide && self.aiming))
    }

    pub fn event(&mut self, event: Event) -> Effects {
        let mut effects = Effects::default();
        match event {
            Event::Foreground(front) => {
                if let Some(window) = &front {
                    if window.pid != self.own_pid {
                        self.last_outside = Some(window.clone());
                    }
                }
                self.front = front;
                self.raise = true;
                self.refocus();
                effects.status_changed = true;
            }
            Event::Mouse { button, down } => {
                if self.settings.aim.hide && button == self.settings.aim.button {
                    match self.settings.aim.mode {
                        AimMode::Hold => self.aiming = down,
                        AimMode::Toggle if down => self.aiming = !self.aiming,
                        AimMode::Toggle => {}
                    }
                }
            }
            Event::Key { .. } => {}
            Event::Moved { x, y } => {
                // A drag cut short by locking reports the slab's corner; the
                // crosshair has already gone back to its own size.
                if !self.locked {
                    let size = self.slab_size();
                    let (cx, cy) = self.display_centre_corner(size);
                    self.settings.offset = (x - cx, y - cy);
                    self.settings = std::mem::take(&mut self.settings).validated();
                    effects.settings_changed = true;
                }
            }
            Event::CenterClicked => {
                self.settings.offset = (0, 0);
                effects.settings_changed = true;
            }
        }
        self.sync(&mut effects);
        effects
    }

    pub fn hotkey(&mut self, key: Hotkey) -> Effects {
        let mut effects = Effects::default();
        match key {
            Hotkey::Lock => {
                self.locked = !self.locked;
                effects.status_changed = true;
            }
            Hotkey::Center => {
                self.settings.offset = (0, 0);
                effects.settings_changed = true;
            }
            Hotkey::Hide => {
                self.hidden = !self.hidden;
                effects.status_changed = true;
            }
            Hotkey::Next | Hotkey::Previous => {
                let count = self.settings.saved.len();
                if count > 0 {
                    let index = match (self.saved_index, key) {
                        (None, Hotkey::Next) => 0,
                        (None, _) => count - 1,
                        (Some(i), Hotkey::Next) => (i + 1) % count,
                        (Some(i), _) => (i + count - 1) % count,
                    };
                    self.saved_index = Some(index);
                    let chosen = self.settings.saved[index].crosshair.clone();
                    *self.active_crosshair_mut() = chosen;
                    effects.settings_changed = true;
                }
            }
            Hotkey::AddGame => {
                if self.add_game_in_front() {
                    effects.settings_changed = true;
                    effects.status_changed = true;
                }
            }
        }
        self.sync(&mut effects);
        effects
    }

    /// Adds the window in front as a game; never Scorpio's own window, and
    /// never one already added. True when one was added.
    pub fn add_game_in_front(&mut self) -> bool {
        let target = match &self.front {
            Some(front) if front.pid != self.own_pid => front.clone(),
            _ => return false,
        };
        self.add_game(&target)
    }

    pub fn add_game(&mut self, window: &WindowInfo) -> bool {
        if !focus::identifiable(window) {
            return false;
        }
        let saved: Vec<_> = self.settings.games.iter().map(|game| game.fingerprint.clone()).collect();
        if focus::find_game(&saved, window).is_some() {
            return false;
        }
        let name = if window.name.is_empty() { window.title.clone() } else { window.name.clone() };
        self.settings.games.push(Game { name, fingerprint: focus::fingerprint(window), crosshair: None });
        self.settings = std::mem::take(&mut self.settings).validated();
        self.refocus();
        true
    }

    /// New settings from the settings window.
    pub fn replace_settings(&mut self, settings: Settings) -> Effects {
        let mut effects = Effects { settings_changed: true, ..Effects::default() };
        self.settings = settings.validated();
        self.saved_index = None;
        self.refocus();
        self.sync(&mut effects);
        effects
    }

    pub fn set_display(&mut self, display: Display) -> Effects {
        let mut effects = Effects::default();
        self.display = display;
        self.sync(&mut effects);
        effects
    }

    pub fn set_installed(&mut self, installed: Vec<InstalledGame>) -> Effects {
        let mut effects = Effects { status_changed: true, ..Effects::default() };
        self.installed = installed;
        self.refocus();
        self.sync(&mut effects);
        effects
    }

    pub fn set_locked(&mut self, locked: bool) -> Effects {
        if self.locked == locked {
            return Effects::default();
        }
        self.hotkey(Hotkey::Lock)
    }

    /// The monitor of the game (or of the window behind the settings), else
    /// the primary display.
    pub fn target_display(&self) -> Display {
        let window = match &self.front {
            Some(window) if window.pid == self.own_pid => self.last_outside.as_ref(),
            front => front.as_ref(),
        };
        window
            .and_then(|window| window.monitor)
            .filter(|[_, _, width, height]| *width > 0 && *height > 0)
            .map(|[x, y, width, height]| Display { x, y, width: width as u32, height: height as u32 })
            .unwrap_or(self.display)
    }

    fn crosshair_size(&self) -> u32 {
        draw(self.active_crosshair(), self.target_display().height).size
    }

    /// The unlocked slab is square and keeps the crosshair's parity, so the
    /// crosshair sits on the same pixels locked and unlocked.
    fn slab_size(&self) -> u32 {
        let size = self.crosshair_size();
        if self.locked {
            return size;
        }
        let mut slab = (size + 2 * SLAB_PAD).max(SLAB_MIN);
        if (slab - size) % 2 == 1 {
            slab += 1;
        }
        slab
    }

    /// Top-left corner that centres a square of `size` on the display.
    fn display_centre_corner(&self, size: u32) -> (i32, i32) {
        let d = self.target_display();
        let area = Area { x: d.x as f64, y: d.y as f64, width: d.width as f64, height: d.height as f64 };
        let placed = centred(area, size, size, 1.0);
        (placed.x, placed.y)
    }

    /// Sends what changed since the last step.
    fn sync(&mut self, effects: &mut Effects) {
        let want = if self.should_show() {
            Shown::Crosshair {
                crosshair: self.active_crosshair().clone(),
                display: self.target_display(),
                offset: self.settings.offset,
                unlocked: !self.locked,
            }
        } else {
            Shown::Hidden
        };
        // A Show raises the overlay too; otherwise raise it when the window
        // in front changed and the crosshair stays.
        let raise = std::mem::take(&mut self.raise);
        if want != self.shown {
            effects.commands.push(self.render(&want));
            self.shown = want;
        } else if raise && self.shown != Shown::Hidden {
            effects.commands.push(Command::Raise);
        }

        if self.capture_hidden != Some(self.settings.hide_from_capture) {
            effects.commands.push(Command::ExcludeFromCapture(self.settings.hide_from_capture));
            self.capture_hidden = Some(self.settings.hide_from_capture);
        }

        let listen = if self.settings.aim.hide && self.focus.visible && self.locked { vec![self.settings.aim.button] } else { Vec::new() };
        if self.listening.as_ref() != Some(&listen) {
            effects.commands.push(Command::ListenTo { mouse: listen.clone(), keys: Vec::new() });
            self.listening = Some(listen);
        }


    }

    fn render(&self, shown: &Shown) -> Command {
        let Shown::Crosshair { crosshair, display, offset, unlocked } = shown else {
            return Command::Hide;
        };
        let bitmap = rasterize(&draw(crosshair, display.height), 1.0);
        let (bitmap, button) = if *unlocked {
            let size = self.slab_size();
            let (bitmap, button) = slab(&bitmap, size, size);
            (bitmap, Some(button))
        } else {
            (bitmap, None)
        };
        let (x, y) = self.display_centre_corner(bitmap.width);
        Command::Show { bitmap, x: x + offset.0, y: y + offset.1, unlocked: *unlocked, button }
    }

    /// What a stream should show, for the OBS page: the crosshair as drawn
    /// on the game's screen (never the unlocked slab), or nothing.
    pub fn stream_view(&self) -> StreamView {
        match &self.shown {
            Shown::Crosshair { crosshair, display, offset, .. } => StreamView {
                visible: true,
                svg: to_svg(&draw(crosshair, display.height)),
                offset: [offset.0, offset.1],
                height: display.height,
            },
            Shown::Hidden => StreamView { visible: false, svg: String::new(), offset: [0, 0], height: self.target_display().height },
        }
    }

    /// Plain words for the settings window's status line.
    pub fn status(&self) -> Status {
        let game = self.focus.game.and_then(|index| self.settings.games.get(index)).map(|game| game.name.clone());
        let front = self.front.as_ref().filter(|window| window.pid != self.own_pid).cloned().or_else(|| self.last_outside.clone());
        Status { visible: self.focus.visible, game, front, locked: self.locked, hidden: self.hidden }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, serde::Serialize)]
pub struct StreamView {
    pub visible: bool,
    pub svg: String,
    pub offset: [i32; 2],
    pub height: u32,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, serde::Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Status {
    pub visible: bool,
    pub game: Option<String>,
    pub front: Option<WindowInfo>,
    pub locked: bool,
    pub hidden: bool,
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const OWN: u32 = 7;

    fn window(pid: u32, name: &str, fullscreen: bool) -> WindowInfo {
        WindowInfo {
            pid,
            name: name.into(),
            path: format!("c:\\games\\{name}.exe"),
            title: name.to_uppercase(),
            window_class: format!("{name}-class"),
            fullscreen,
            monitor: None,
        }
    }

    fn app() -> App {
        let mut settings = Settings::default();
        settings.detect.fullscreen = false;
        settings.detect.launchers = false;
        settings.games.push(Game { name: "Game".into(), fingerprint: focus::fingerprint(&window(1, "game", false)), crosshair: None });
        let mut app = App::new(settings, OWN);
        app.capture_hidden = Some(false);
        app.listening = Some(Vec::new());
        app
    }

    fn shows(effects: &Effects) -> Option<bool> {
        effects.commands.iter().rev().find_map(|command| match command {
            Command::Show { .. } => Some(true),
            Command::Hide => Some(false),
            _ => None,
        })
    }

    #[test]
    fn shows_only_while_an_added_game_is_in_front() {
        let mut app = app();
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false))))), Some(true));
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(2, "browser", false))))), Some(false));
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false))))), Some(true));
    }

    #[test]
    fn nothing_is_sent_when_nothing_changes() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false))));
        assert_eq!(app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false)))).commands, vec![Command::Raise], "only raised again");
    }

    #[test]
    fn own_window_keeps_the_crosshair_and_add_game_ignores_it() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false))));
        let effects = app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(WindowInfo { title: SETTINGS_TITLE.into(), ..window(OWN, "scorpio", false) })));
        assert_eq!(shows(&effects), None);
        assert!(app.focus.visible);
        assert!(!app.add_game_in_front());
        assert_eq!(app.settings.games.len(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn add_game_adds_the_window_in_front_once() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(3, "other", false))));
        assert!(!app.focus.visible);
        let effects = app.hotkey(Hotkey::AddGame);
        assert!(effects.settings_changed);
        assert_eq!(shows(&effects), Some(true));
        assert_eq!(app.settings.games.len(), 2);
        assert!(!app.add_game_in_front());
    }

    #[test]
    fn fullscreen_detection_follows_the_setting() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(4, "unknown", true))));
        assert!(!app.focus.visible);
        let mut settings = app.settings.clone();
        settings.detect.fullscreen = true;
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.replace_settings(settings)), Some(true));
    }

    #[test]
    fn game_crosshair_replaces_the_main_one() {
        let mut app = app();
        let red = Crosshair { color: [255, 0, 0], ..Crosshair::default() };
        app.settings.games[0].crosshair = Some(red.clone());
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false))));
        assert_eq!(app.active_crosshair(), &red);
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(2, "browser", false))));
        assert_eq!(app.active_crosshair(), &app.settings.crosshair);
    }

    #[test]
    fn aim_hold_and_toggle() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.settings.aim.hide = true;
        let effects = app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false))));
        assert!(effects.commands.contains(&Command::ListenTo { mouse: vec![MouseButton::Right], keys: vec![] }));
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Mouse { button: MouseButton::Right, down: true })), Some(false));
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Mouse { button: MouseButton::Right, down: false })), Some(true));
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Mouse { button: MouseButton::Left, down: true })), None);

        app.settings.aim.mode = AimMode::Toggle;
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Mouse { button: MouseButton::Right, down: true })), Some(false));
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Mouse { button: MouseButton::Right, down: false })), None);
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Mouse { button: MouseButton::Right, down: true })), Some(true));
    }

    #[test]
    fn nothing_is_listened_to_outside_a_game() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.settings.aim.hide = true;
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false))));
        let effects = app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(2, "browser", false))));
        assert!(effects.commands.contains(&Command::ListenTo { mouse: vec![], keys: vec![] }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn hide_hotkey_and_unlock() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false))));
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.hotkey(Hotkey::Hide)), Some(false));
        let effects = app.hotkey(Hotkey::Lock);
        let Some(Command::Show { unlocked, button, .. }) = effects.commands.first() else { panic!("unlocked shows the slab") };
        assert!(*unlocked && button.is_some());
        app.hotkey(Hotkey::Lock);
        assert!(app.locked);
    }

    #[test]
    fn locked_crosshair_is_exactly_centred_and_slab_keeps_its_centre() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.set_display(Display { x: 1920, y: 0, width: 2560, height: 1440 });
        let locked = app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false))));
        let Some(Command::Show { bitmap, x, y, .. }) = locked.commands.first() else { panic!() };
        assert_eq!(2 * x + bitmap.width as i32, 2 * 1920 + 2560);
        assert_eq!(2 * y + bitmap.height as i32, 1440);
        let (cx, cy) = (2 * x + bitmap.width as i32, 2 * y + bitmap.height as i32);

        let unlocked = app.hotkey(Hotkey::Lock);
        let Some(Command::Show { bitmap, x, y, .. }) = unlocked.commands.first() else { panic!() };
        assert_eq!((2 * x + bitmap.width as i32, 2 * y + bitmap.height as i32), (cx, cy));
    }

    #[test]
    fn dragging_sets_the_offset_and_centre_button_resets_it() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false))));
        let effects = app.hotkey(Hotkey::Lock);
        let Some(Command::Show { x, y, .. }) = effects.commands.first().cloned() else { panic!() };
        let moved = app.event(Event::Moved { x: x + 5, y: y - 3 });
        assert!(moved.settings_changed);
        assert_eq!(app.settings.offset, (5, -3));
        app.event(Event::CenterClicked);
        assert_eq!(app.settings.offset, (0, 0));
    }

    #[test]
    fn next_and_previous_cycle_saved_crosshairs() {
        let mut app = app();
        for gap in [1, 2, 3] {
            app.settings.saved.push(crate::store::Saved { name: format!("{gap}"), crosshair: Crosshair { gap, ..Crosshair::default() } });
        }
        app.hotkey(Hotkey::Next);
        assert_eq!(app.settings.crosshair.gap, 1);
        app.hotkey(Hotkey::Previous);
        assert_eq!(app.settings.crosshair.gap, 3);
        app.hotkey(Hotkey::Next);
        assert_eq!(app.settings.crosshair.gap, 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn unidentifiable_window_is_not_added() {
        let mut app = app();
        let nameless = WindowInfo { pid: 9, title: "Untitled".into(), ..WindowInfo::default() };
        assert!(!app.add_game(&nameless));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_desktop_hides_and_closing_settings_over_it_stays_hidden() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false))));
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Foreground(None))), Some(false));
        let settings = WindowInfo { title: SETTINGS_TITLE.into(), ..window(OWN, "scorpio", false) };
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(settings)))), Some(true));
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Foreground(None))), Some(false));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_tray_menu_changes_nothing() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(2, "browser", false))));
        let tray = WindowInfo { title: String::new(), ..window(OWN, "scorpio", false) };
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(tray)))), None);
        assert!(!app.focus.visible);
    }

    #[test]
    fn removing_a_game_while_tuning_keeps_the_right_game() {
        let mut app = app();
        for name in ["b", "c"] {
            app.settings.games.push(Game { name: name.into(), fingerprint: focus::fingerprint(&window(5, name, false)), crosshair: None });
        }
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(5, "b", false))));
        assert_eq!(app.focus.game, Some(1));
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(WindowInfo { title: SETTINGS_TITLE.into(), ..window(OWN, "scorpio", false) })));
        let mut settings = app.settings.clone();
        settings.games.remove(0);
        app.replace_settings(settings);
        assert_eq!(app.focus.game, Some(0), "b moved to index 0");
        assert_eq!(app.settings.games[0].name, "b");
    }

    #[test]
    fn aiming_resets_when_the_crosshair_goes_away() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.settings.aim.hide = true;
        app.settings.detect.fullscreen = true;
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(4, "x", true))));
        app.event(Event::Mouse { button: MouseButton::Right, down: true });
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(2, "browser", false))));
        assert_eq!(shows(&app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(4, "x", true))))), Some(true));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_move_after_locking_is_ignored() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false))));
        app.hotkey(Hotkey::Lock);
        app.hotkey(Hotkey::Lock);
        assert!(!app.event(Event::Moved { x: 1, y: 1 }).settings_changed);
        assert_eq!(app.settings.offset, (0, 0));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_crosshair_is_centred_on_the_games_monitor() {
        let mut app = app();
        app.set_display(Display { x: 0, y: 0, width: 1920, height: 1080 });
        let on_second = WindowInfo { monitor: Some([1920, 0, 2560, 1440]), ..window(1, "game", false) };
        let effects = app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(on_second)));
        let Some(Command::Show { bitmap, x, y, .. }) = effects.commands.first() else { panic!() };
        assert_eq!(2 * x + bitmap.width as i32, 2 * 1920 + 2560);
        assert_eq!(2 * y + bitmap.height as i32, 1440);
        let settings = WindowInfo { title: SETTINGS_TITLE.into(), monitor: Some([0, 0, 1920, 1080]), ..window(OWN, "scorpio", false) };
        assert!(app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(settings))).commands.iter().all(|c| !matches!(c, Command::Show { .. })), "tuning keeps it on the game's monitor");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_stream_follows_the_overlay() {
        let mut app = app();
        assert!(!app.stream_view().visible);
        app.event(Event::Foreground(Some(window(1, "game", false))));
        let view = app.stream_view();
        assert!(view.visible && view.svg.starts_with("<svg"));
        app.hotkey(Hotkey::Hide);
        assert!(!app.stream_view().visible);
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/src/core/center.rs`:

```rs
//! Where a window goes so its middle sits on the display's middle, between the
//! two centre pixels where a game draws its own crosshair.
//!
//! Windows are placed in whole logical pixels; at 125% or 150% scaling each axis also tries one and two
//! logical pixels smaller and keeps the size whose middle lands closest.

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Area {
    pub x: f64,
    pub y: f64,
    pub width: f64,
    pub height: f64,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Placed {
    pub x: i32,
    pub y: i32,
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
    /// How far the middle is off, in physical pixels.
    pub miss: f64,
}

pub fn centred(area: Area, width: u32, height: u32, scale: f64) -> Placed {
    let x = fit_axis(area.x, area.width, width, scale);
    let y = fit_axis(area.y, area.height, height, scale);
    Placed { x: x.position, y: y.position, width: x.size, height: y.size, miss: x.miss.max(y.miss) }
}

struct Fit {
    position: i32,
    size: u32,
    miss: f64,
}

/// JavaScript's Math.round, which the reference placement was tuned with.
fn js_round(value: f64) -> f64 {
    (value + 0.5).floor()
}

fn fit_axis(start: f64, length: f64, size: u32, scale: f64) -> Fit {
    let middle = (js_round(start * scale) * 2.0 + js_round(length * scale)) / 2.0;
    [size, size.saturating_sub(1), size.saturating_sub(2)]
        .map(|candidate| {
            let span = js_round(f64::from(candidate) * scale);
            let position = js_round((middle - span / 2.0) / scale);
            let miss = (position * scale + span / 2.0 - middle).abs();
            Fit { position: position as i32, size: candidate, miss }
        })
        .into_iter()
        .reduce(|best, fit| if fit.miss < best.miss - 1e-9 { fit } else { best })
        .expect("three candidates")
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn at_100_percent_the_middle_is_the_displays_middle_exactly() {
        let areas = [(0.0, 0.0, 2560.0, 1440.0), (1920.0, 1080.0, 1920.0, 1080.0), (-1920.0, 0.0, 1920.0, 1080.0)];
        for (x, y, width, height) in areas {
            let b = centred(Area { x, y, width, height }, 201, 125, 1.0);
            let middle = (f64::from(b.x) + f64::from(b.width) / 2.0, f64::from(b.y) + f64::from(b.height) / 2.0);
            assert_eq!((middle, b.miss), ((x + width / 2.0, y + height / 2.0), 0.0), "{b:?}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn at_125_150_and_175_percent_within_half_a_pixel() {
        for scale in [1.25_f64, 1.5, 1.75] {
            let area = Area { x: 0.0, y: 0.0, width: (2560.0 / scale).round(), height: (1440.0 / scale).round() };
            let b = centred(area, 160, 160, scale);
            assert!(b.miss <= 0.5, "{scale}: {b:?}");
            assert!(b.width >= 158 && b.height >= 158, "{scale}: {b:?}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn at_200_percent_exact() {
        let b = centred(Area { x: 0.0, y: 0.0, width: 1920.0, height: 1080.0 }, 160, 160, 2.0);
        assert_eq!((b.x, b.y, b.miss), (880, 460, 0.0));
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/src/core/crosshair.rs`:

```rs
//! Counter-Strike 2's crosshair: its settings, its share codes, and its shapes
//! in physical pixels for the player's screen.
//!
//! Share code byte layouts are the ones documented by akiver/csgo-sharecode (doc/code_layout.md, MIT).

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// The static styles Scorpio offers; a dynamic style in an imported code
/// becomes the static style it rests as (0, 2, 5 -> 4; 1 -> 3; 7 -> 9).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Shape {
    Cross,
    Circle,
    Dot,
    Square,
    Quad,
}

/// (CS2 style id, display name, shape), in the order the UI lists them.
pub const STYLES: [(u8, &str, Shape); 5] = [
    (4, "Static Cross", Shape::Cross),
    (3, "Static Circle", Shape::Circle),
    (6, "Dot Only", Shape::Dot),
    (8, "Static Square", Shape::Square),
    (9, "Static Quad", Shape::Quad),
];

/// Values a share code carries that an overlay does not draw; kept so a code
/// survives a round trip.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default, rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Extra {
    pub follow_recoil: bool,
    pub spread_limit: u8,
    pub split_distance: u8,
    pub inner_split_alpha: f32,
    pub outer_split_alpha: f32,
    pub split_size_ratio: f32,
    pub scope_dot_scale: f32,
    pub scope_dot_uses_color: bool,
}

/// The settings, in CS2's units. `normalized()` clamps every field into range.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default, rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Crosshair {
    pub style: u8,
    pub length: u8,
    /// Half steps (Scorpio's own precision); share codes carry it rounded.
    pub thickness: f32,
    pub gap: i16,
    pub dot: bool,
    pub t_shape: bool,
    pub color: [u8; 3],
    pub alpha: u8,
    /// 0 none, 1 full, 2 top and left edges only.
    pub outline: u8,
    /// Scorpio's own: CS2 draws outlines 1 px wide. Not in share codes.
    pub outline_width: u8,
    pub outline_color: [u8; 3],
    pub outline_alpha: u8,
    /// The screen height the sizes were made at.
    pub made_for: u16,
    pub extra: Extra,
}

impl Default for Extra {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Extra {
            follow_recoil: false,
            spread_limit: 255,
            split_distance: 7,
            inner_split_alpha: 1.0,
            outer_split_alpha: 0.4,
            split_size_ratio: 0.3,
            scope_dot_scale: 1.0,
            scope_dot_uses_color: false,
        }
    }
}

impl Default for Crosshair {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Crosshair {
            style: 4,
            length: 8,
            thickness: 2.0,
            gap: 4,
            dot: false,
            t_shape: false,
            color: [50, 250, 50],
            alpha: 255,
            outline: 0,
            outline_width: 1,
            outline_color: [0, 0, 0],
            outline_alpha: 255,
            made_for: 1080,
            extra: Extra::default(),
        }
    }
}

impl Crosshair {
    /// Every field clamped into range; style set to a static style.
    pub fn normalized(&self) -> Crosshair {
        let e = &self.extra;
        Crosshair {
            style: resting_style(self.style),
            thickness: halves(self.thickness, 0.0, 32.0, 2.0),
            gap: self.gap.clamp(-3840, 3840),
            outline: self.outline.min(2),
            outline_width: self.outline_width.clamp(1, 6),
            made_for: self.made_for.clamp(240, 8640),
            extra: Extra {
                split_distance: e.split_distance.min(127),
                inner_split_alpha: hundredths(e.inner_split_alpha, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0),
                outer_split_alpha: hundredths(e.outer_split_alpha, 0.3, 1.0, 0.4),
                split_size_ratio: hundredths(e.split_size_ratio, 0.0, 1.0, 0.3),
                scope_dot_scale: hundredths(e.scope_dot_scale, 0.1, 2.0, 1.0),
                ..e.clone()
            },
            ..self.clone()
        }
    }

    pub fn shape(&self) -> Shape {
        let style = resting_style(self.style);
        STYLES.iter().find(|entry| entry.0 == style).map_or(Shape::Cross, |entry| entry.2)
    }
}

/// A dynamic style id becomes the static one it rests as.
pub fn resting_style(style: u8) -> u8 {
    match style {
        _ if STYLES.iter().any(|entry| entry.0 == style) => style,
        1 => 3,
        7 => 9,
        _ => 4,
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Decoded {
    /// "cs2-v1", "legacy-v1", "legacy-v3" or "legacy-v4".
    pub format: &'static str,
    /// The style the code had before a dynamic one was set to rest.
    pub code_style: u8,
    pub crosshair: Crosshair,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct NotACode;

/// JavaScript's Math.round: halves go up, also below zero.
fn js_round(value: f64) -> f64 {
    (value + 0.5).floor()
}

fn within(value: f32, min: f32, max: f32, fallback: f32) -> f64 {
    f64::from(if value.is_finite() { value.clamp(min, max) } else { fallback })
}

fn halves(value: f32, min: f32, max: f32, fallback: f32) -> f32 {
    (js_round(within(value, min, max, fallback) * 2.0) / 2.0) as f32
}

fn hundredths(value: f32, min: f32, max: f32, fallback: f32) -> f32 {
    (js_round(within(value, min, max, fallback) * 100.0) / 100.0) as f32
}

/* Share codes: a number in base 57 written least significant digit first; its
bytes are big-endian and byte 0 is the sum of the rest, mod 256. */

const ALPHABET: &[u8; 57] = b"ABCDEFGHJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefhijkmnopqrstuvwxyz23456789";

fn digit(c: u8) -> Option<u32> {
    ALPHABET.iter().position(|&a| a == c).map(|i| i as u32)
}

fn to_bytes(digits: &[u8], size: usize) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut bytes = vec![0u8; size];
    for &c in digits.iter().rev() {
        let mut carry = digit(c)?;
        for byte in bytes.iter_mut().rev() {
            let t = u32::from(*byte) * 57 + carry;
            *byte = t as u8;
            carry = t >> 8;
        }
        if carry != 0 {
            return None;
        }
    }
    Some(bytes)
}

fn to_digits(bytes: &[u8], count: usize) -> String {
    let mut value = bytes.to_vec();
    (0..count)
        .map(|_| {
            let mut rest = 0u32;
            for byte in value.iter_mut() {
                let t = (rest << 8) | u32::from(*byte);
                *byte = (t / 57) as u8;
                rest = t % 57;
            }
            ALPHABET[rest as usize] as char
        })
        .collect()
}

fn sum_ok(bytes: &[u8]) -> bool {
    bytes[0] == bytes[1..].iter().fold(0u8, |total, &b| total.wrapping_add(b))
}

fn uint32(bytes: &[u8], at: usize) -> u32 {
    u32::from_le_bytes([bytes[at], bytes[at + 1], bytes[at + 2], bytes[at + 3]])
}

fn bits(value: u32, from: u32, count: u32) -> u32 {
    (value >> from) & ((1 << count) - 1)
}

/// Round half to even, as CS:GO did when it scaled sizes.
fn round_even(value: f64) -> f64 {
    let floor = value.floor();
    if (value - floor - 0.5).abs() < 1e-9 {
        floor + floor.rem_euclid(2.0)
    } else {
        value.round()
    }
}

/// cs2-v1, CS2 since 30/09/2026: 32 bytes behind "CS".
fn read_cs2(b: &[u8]) -> Crosshair {
    let field = uint32(b, 18);
    Crosshair {
        made_for: u16::from_le_bytes([b[2], b[3]]),
        style: b[4] & 0x1f,
        dot: b[4] & 0x40 != 0,
        t_shape: b[4] & 0x80 != 0,
        color: [b[5], b[6], b[7]],
        alpha: b[8],
        outline_color: [b[9], b[10], b[11]],
        outline_alpha: b[12],
        thickness: f32::from(b[13] & 0x3f),
        outline: b[13] >> 6,
        gap: i16::from_le_bytes([b[14], b[15]]),
        length: b[16],
        extra: Extra {
            follow_recoil: b[4] & 0x20 != 0,
            spread_limit: b[17],
            split_distance: bits(field, 0, 7) as u8,
            inner_split_alpha: bits(field, 7, 7) as f32 / 100.0,
            outer_split_alpha: (bits(field, 14, 7) + 30) as f32 / 100.0,
            split_size_ratio: bits(field, 21, 7) as f32 / 100.0,
            scope_dot_uses_color: bits(field, 28, 1) == 1,
            scope_dot_scale: (f32::from(b[22]) + 10.0) / 100.0,
        },
        ..Crosshair::default()
    }
}

/// legacy-v3 (23/09/2026) and legacy-v4 (24/09/2026): pixels at `made_for`.
fn read_pixel_era(b: &[u8], version: u8) -> Crosshair {
    let field = uint32(b, 10);
    Crosshair {
        style: b[2] & 0x0f,
        dot: b[2] & 0x40 != 0,
        t_shape: b[2] & 0x80 != 0,
        outline: if version == 3 { u8::from(b[2] & 0x20 != 0) } else { bits(field, 28, 2) as u8 },
        color: [b[3], b[4], b[5]],
        alpha: b[6],
        gap: i16::from(b[7]),
        length: b[8],
        thickness: bits(field, 23, 5) as f32,
        made_for: u16::from_le_bytes([b[14], b[15]]),
        extra: Extra {
            follow_recoil: b[2] & 0x10 != 0,
            spread_limit: b[9],
            split_distance: bits(field, 0, 7) as u8,
            inner_split_alpha: bits(field, 7, 5) as f32 * 0.05,
            outer_split_alpha: 0.3 + bits(field, 12, 4) as f32 * 0.05,
            split_size_ratio: bits(field, 16, 7) as f32 / 100.0,
            ..Extra::default()
        },
        ..Crosshair::default()
    }
}

/// legacy-v1, CS:GO and CS2 before 23/09/2026: sizes in tenths, drawn by the
/// old renderer. Placed at `screen_height` with the community's reading of that
/// renderer: length and thickness scale by height / 480, the gap is 4 + gap.
fn read_tenths(b: &[u8], screen_height: u32) -> Crosshair {
    let scale = f64::from(screen_height) / 480.0;
    let scaled = |tenths: u8| round_even(f64::from(tenths) / 10.0 * scale);
    Crosshair {
        style: (b[13] & 0x0e) >> 1,
        dot: b[13] & 0x10 != 0,
        t_shape: b[13] & 0x80 != 0,
        color: [b[4], b[5], b[6]],
        alpha: if b[13] & 0x40 != 0 { b[7] } else { 255 },
        outline: u8::from(b[10] & 0x08 != 0),
        outline_width: (b[3] / 2 + b[3] % 2).max(1),
        gap: (4.0 + f64::from(b[2] as i8) / 10.0).trunc() as i16,
        length: scaled(b[14]) as u8,
        thickness: scaled(b[12]).max(1.0) as f32,
        made_for: screen_height.min(u32::from(u16::MAX)) as u16,
        extra: Extra {
            follow_recoil: b[8] & 0x80 != 0,
            split_distance: b[8] & 0x07,
            inner_split_alpha: f32::from(b[10] >> 4) / 10.0,
            outer_split_alpha: (f32::from(b[11] & 0x0f) / 10.0).max(0.3),
            split_size_ratio: f32::from(b[11] >> 4) / 10.0,
            ..Extra::default()
        },
        ..Crosshair::default()
    }
}

/// The 44 digits of a cs2-v1 code.
fn cs2_digits(code: &str) -> Option<&[u8]> {
    let digits = code.strip_prefix("CS")?.as_bytes();
    let ok = !code.starts_with("CSGO") && digits.len() == 44 && digits.iter().all(|&c| digit(c).is_some());
    ok.then_some(digits)
}

/// The 25 digits of a CSGO-xxxxx-xxxxx-xxxxx-xxxxx-xxxxx code (dashes optional).
fn legacy_digits(code: &str) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut rest = code.strip_prefix("CSGO")?.as_bytes();
    let mut digits = Vec::with_capacity(25);
    for _ in 0..5 {
        rest = rest.strip_prefix(b"-").unwrap_or(rest);
        let group = rest.get(..5)?;
        if !group.iter().all(|&c| digit(c).is_some()) {
            return None;
        }
        digits.extend_from_slice(group);
        rest = &rest[5..];
    }
    rest.is_empty().then_some(digits)
}

fn read_raw(code: &str, screen_height: u32) -> Option<(&'static str, Crosshair)> {
    if let Some(digits) = cs2_digits(code) {
        let bytes = to_bytes(digits, 32)?;
        return (sum_ok(&bytes) && bytes[1] == 1).then(|| ("cs2-v1", read_cs2(&bytes)));
    }
    let bytes = to_bytes(&legacy_digits(code)?, 18)?;
    match bytes[1] {
        _ if !sum_ok(&bytes) => None,
        1 => Some(("legacy-v1", read_tenths(&bytes, screen_height))),
        3 => Some(("legacy-v3", read_pixel_era(&bytes, 3))),
        4 => Some(("legacy-v4", read_pixel_era(&bytes, 4))),
        _ => None,
    }
}

/// Reads any CS2 or CS:GO crosshair code. `screen_height` places an old
/// (legacy-v1, sizes in tenths) code at the player's resolution.
pub fn read_code(code: &str, screen_height: u32) -> Result<Decoded, NotACode> {
    let (format, raw) = read_raw(code.trim(), screen_height).ok_or(NotACode)?;
    Ok(Decoded { format, code_style: raw.style, crosshair: raw.normalized() })
}

/// The cs2-v1 code CS2 imports today.
pub fn write_code(crosshair: &Crosshair) -> String {
    let s = crosshair.normalized();
    let e = &s.extra;
    let percent = |value: f32| js_round(f64::from(value) * 100.0) as u32;
    let field = u32::from(e.split_distance)
        | percent(e.inner_split_alpha) << 7
        | (percent(e.outer_split_alpha) - 30) << 14
        | percent(e.split_size_ratio) << 21
        | u32::from(e.scope_dot_uses_color) << 28;
    let flags = if e.follow_recoil { 0x20 } else { 0 } | if s.dot { 0x40 } else { 0 } | if s.t_shape { 0x80 } else { 0 };
    let mut bytes = [0u8; 32];
    bytes[1] = 1;
    bytes[2..4].copy_from_slice(&s.made_for.to_le_bytes());
    bytes[4] = s.style | flags;
    bytes[5..8].copy_from_slice(&s.color);
    bytes[8] = s.alpha;
    bytes[9..12].copy_from_slice(&s.outline_color);
    bytes[12] = s.outline_alpha;
    // CS2 keeps whole pixels; a half step rounds
    bytes[13] = js_round(f64::from(s.thickness)) as u8 | s.outline << 6;
    bytes[14..16].copy_from_slice(&s.gap.to_le_bytes());
    bytes[16] = s.length;
    bytes[17] = e.spread_limit;
    bytes[18..22].copy_from_slice(&field.to_le_bytes());
    bytes[22] = (percent(e.scope_dot_scale) - 10) as u8;
    bytes[0] = bytes[1..].iter().fold(0u8, |total, &b| total.wrapping_add(b));
    format!("CS{}", to_digits(&bytes, 44))
}

/* Drawing */

/// Sizes in physical pixels on a screen `screen_height` pixels tall.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Pixels {
    pub length: i32,
    /// Quarter pixels; the fraction is drawn as a soft far edge.
    pub thickness: f32,
    pub gap: i32,
    pub outline: i32,
}

/// CS2 scales every size by the screen's height over the height it was made at.
pub fn pixels(crosshair: &Crosshair, screen_height: u32) -> Pixels {
    let s = crosshair.normalized();
    let k = f64::from(screen_height) / f64::from(s.made_for);
    Pixels {
        length: js_round(f64::from(s.length) * k) as i32,
        thickness: (js_round(f64::from(s.thickness) * k * 4.0) / 4.0) as f32,
        gap: js_round(f64::from(s.gap) * k) as i32,
        outline: (js_round(f64::from(s.outline_width) * k) as i32).max(1),
    }
}

/// One filled piece of the drawing, in physical pixels; colour is straight
/// (not premultiplied) RGBA.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Piece {
    Rect { x: f32, y: f32, w: f32, h: f32, rgba: [u8; 4] },
    Ring { cx: f32, cy: f32, r: f32, width: f32, rgba: [u8; 4] },
}

/// A square `size` pixels wide whose centre is the crosshair's centre (between
/// the two middle pixels). Outline pieces come first.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Drawing {
    pub size: u32,
    pub pieces: Vec<Piece>,
    pub pixels: Pixels,
}

/// x, y, w, h
type Bar = [f32; 4];

fn push(bars: &mut Vec<Bar>, x: f32, y: f32, w: f32, h: f32) {
    if w > 0.0 && h > 0.0 {
        bars.push([x, y, w, h]);
    }
}

/// The four bars beyond the gap; the T shape drops the top one.
fn cross_bars(bars: &mut Vec<Bar>, from: f32, base: f32, t: f32, p: &Pixels, t_shape: bool) {
    let (gap, length) = (p.gap as f32, p.length as f32);
    push(bars, from + base + gap, from, length, t);
    push(bars, from - gap - length, from, length, t);
    push(bars, from, from + base + gap, t, length);
    if !t_shape {
        push(bars, from, from - gap - length, t, length);
    }
}

/// A frame around the gap; the quad keeps only its corners. Its sides are
/// whole pixels, and it is placed from its own width so it stays centred
/// like a cross of that width.
fn frame_bars(bars: &mut Vec<Bar>, c: f32, t: f32, p: &Pixels, quad: bool) {
    let gap = p.gap.max(0) as f32;
    let w = t.round().max(1.0);
    let from = c - (w / 2.0).floor();
    let inner = from - gap;
    let span = w + 2.0 * gap;
    let outer = span + 2.0 * w;
    let (left, top, right, bottom) = (inner - w, inner - w, inner + span, inner + span);
    if !quad {
        push(bars, left, top, outer, w);
        push(bars, left, bottom, outer, w);
        push(bars, left, inner, w, span);
        push(bars, right, inner, w, span);
        return;
    }
    let arm = outer.min((2.0 * w).max(p.length as f32));
    let far = left + outer - arm;
    for (x, y) in [(left, top), (far, top), (left, bottom), (far, bottom)] {
        push(bars, x, y, arm, w);
    }
    for (x, y) in [(left, top), (right, top), (left, far), (right, far)] {
        push(bars, x, y, w, arm);
    }
}

fn outline_of(bars: &[Bar], mode: u8, o: f32) -> Vec<Bar> {
    let mut edges = Vec::new();
    for &[x, y, w, h] in bars {
        match mode {
            1 => edges.push([x - o, y - o, w + 2.0 * o, h + 2.0 * o]),
            2 => edges.extend([[x - o, y - o, w + o, o], [x - o, y, o, h]]),
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    edges
}

pub fn draw(crosshair: &Crosshair, screen_height: u32) -> Drawing {
    let s = crosshair.normalized();
    let p = pixels(&s, screen_height);
    let shape = s.shape();
    let t = if shape == Shape::Dot { p.thickness.max(1.0) } else { p.thickness };
    // Bars start on whole pixels; a fractional thickness adds a soft far edge
    let base = t.floor();
    let reach = p.gap.max(0) + p.length + t.ceil() as i32 + p.outline + 2;
    let size = 2 * (reach + 2);
    let c = (size / 2) as f32;
    let from = c - (base / 2.0).floor();
    let mut bars = Vec::new();
    match shape {
        Shape::Cross if t > 0.0 => cross_bars(&mut bars, from, base, t, &p, s.t_shape),
        Shape::Square | Shape::Quad if t > 0.0 => frame_bars(&mut bars, c, t, &p, shape == Shape::Quad),
        _ => {}
    }
    if s.dot || shape == Shape::Dot {
        let d = t.max(1.0);
        push(&mut bars, from, from, d, d);
    }
    let edges = outline_of(&bars, s.outline, p.outline as f32);
    let [r, g, b] = s.color;
    let [or, og, ob] = s.outline_color;
    let rect = |rgba: [u8; 4]| move |[x, y, w, h]: Bar| Piece::Rect { x, y, w, h, rgba };
    let mut pieces: Vec<Piece> = edges.into_iter().map(rect([or, og, ob, s.outline_alpha])).collect();
    pieces.extend(bars.into_iter().map(rect([r, g, b, s.alpha])));
    if shape == Shape::Circle && t > 0.0 {
        let radius = (p.gap as f32 + p.length as f32 / 2.0).max(1.0);
        // A ring has no top or left edge, so both outline modes draw it whole.
        if s.outline != 0 {
            let width = t + 2.0 * p.outline as f32;
            pieces.insert(0, Piece::Ring { cx: c, cy: c, r: radius, width, rgba: [or, og, ob, s.outline_alpha] });
        }
        pieces.push(Piece::Ring { cx: c, cy: c, r: radius, width: t, rgba: [r, g, b, s.alpha] });
    }
    Drawing { size: size as u32, pieces, pixels: p }
}

fn css([r, g, b, a]: [u8; 4]) -> String {
    format!("rgba({r},{g},{b},{:.3})", f32::from(a) / 255.0)
}

/// The same drawing as SVG, for previews in the settings window.
pub fn to_svg(drawing: &Drawing) -> String {
    let size = drawing.size;
    let mut svg = format!(r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="{size}" height="{size}" viewBox="0 0 {size} {size}">"#);
    // Consecutive rects of one colour share a group, so the outline comes first
    let mut group: Option<[u8; 4]> = None;
    for piece in &drawing.pieces {
        match *piece {
            Piece::Rect { x, y, w, h, rgba } => {
                if group != Some(rgba) {
                    if group.is_some() {
                        svg.push_str("</g>");
                    }
                    svg.push_str(&format!(r#"<g fill="{}">"#, css(rgba)));
                    group = Some(rgba);
                }
                svg.push_str(&format!(r#"<rect x="{x}" y="{y}" width="{w}" height="{h}"/>"#));
            }
            Piece::Ring { cx, cy, r, width, rgba } => {
                if group.take().is_some() {
                    svg.push_str("</g>");
                }
                svg.push_str(&format!(r#"<circle cx="{cx}" cy="{cy}" r="{r}" fill="none" stroke="{}" stroke-width="{width}"/>"#, css(rgba)));
            }
        }
    }
    if group.is_some() {
        svg.push_str("</g>");
    }
    svg.push_str("</svg>");
    svg
}
```
