`src-tauri/src/core/focus.rs`:

```rs
//! Deciding whether the window in front is a game.

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// What the platform reports about a top-level window. `path` may be empty
/// when the system won't say (never worked around by opening the process).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default, rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct WindowInfo {
    pub pid: u32,
    pub name: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub window_class: String,
    pub fullscreen: bool,
    /// The monitor the window is on: x, y, width, height in physical pixels.
    pub monitor: Option<[i32; 4]>,
}

/// The four checks of a saved game.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default, rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Fingerprint {
    pub name: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub window_class: String,
}

/// Each check: Some(true/false), or None when either side is empty.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Verdict {
    pub name: Option<bool>,
    pub path: Option<bool>,
    pub title: Option<bool>,
    pub window_class: Option<bool>,
    pub agree: u8,
    pub matches: bool,
}

pub const AGREE: u8 = 2;

pub fn fingerprint(window: &WindowInfo) -> Fingerprint {
    Fingerprint {
        name: window.name.trim().to_owned(),
        path: window.path.trim().to_owned(),
        title: window.title.trim().to_owned(),
        window_class: window.window_class.trim().to_owned(),
    }
}

fn program_name(value: &str) -> String {
    let name = value.trim().to_lowercase();
    name.strip_suffix(".exe").map_or(name.clone(), str::to_owned)
}

fn same_path(a: &str, b: &str) -> bool {
    let norm = |p: &str| p.trim().to_lowercase().replace('/', "\\");
    norm(a) == norm(b)
}

/// None when either side has nothing to compare.
fn check(saved: &str, seen: &str, same: impl Fn(&str, &str) -> bool) -> Option<bool> {
    (!saved.trim().is_empty() && !seen.trim().is_empty()).then(|| same(saved, seen))
}

/// Names and paths compare without case (and paths without slash style);
/// titles and classes exactly. Two agreeing checks make a match.
pub fn compare(saved: &Fingerprint, window: &WindowInfo) -> Verdict {
    let exact = |a: &str, b: &str| a.trim() == b.trim();
    let name = check(&saved.name, &window.name, |a, b| program_name(a) == program_name(b));
    let path = check(&saved.path, &window.path, same_path);
    let title = check(&saved.title, &window.title, exact);
    let window_class = check(&saved.window_class, &window.window_class, exact);
    let agree = [name, path, title, window_class].iter().filter(|c| **c == Some(true)).count() as u8;
    Verdict { name, path, title, window_class, agree, matches: agree >= AGREE }
}

/// A program can be told apart only by its name or its executable.
pub fn identifiable(window: &WindowInfo) -> bool {
    !window.name.trim().is_empty() || !window.path.trim().is_empty()
}

/// Index of the saved fingerprint this window is, the one with most agreeing checks.
pub fn find_game(saved: &[Fingerprint], window: &WindowInfo) -> Option<(usize, Verdict)> {
    let mut best: Option<(usize, Verdict)> = None;
    for (index, game) in saved.iter().enumerate() {
        let verdict = compare(game, window);
        if verdict.matches && best.as_ref().map_or(true, |(_, b)| verdict.agree > b.agree) {
            best = Some((index, verdict));
        }
    }
    best
}

/// Window classes of the desktop and the shell, which can fill a monitor too.
const SHELL_CLASSES: [&str; 7] = ["progman", "workerw", "shell_traywnd", "windows.ui.core.corewindow", "desktop_window", "xfdesktop", "nautilus-desktop"];
const SHELL_PROGRAMS: [&str; 3] = ["explorer", "plasmashell", "gnome-shell"];

/// A borderless or fullscreen window counts as a game unless it is the desktop
/// or the shell (Progman, WorkerW, Shell_TrayWnd, explorer, plasmashell,
/// gnome-shell...). Returns the key used to exclude it: "exe:<path or name, lower case>".
pub fn fullscreen_key(window: &WindowInfo) -> Option<String> {
    let class = window.window_class.trim().to_lowercase();
    let shell = SHELL_CLASSES.contains(&class.as_str()) || SHELL_PROGRAMS.contains(&program_name(&window.name).as_str());
    if !window.fullscreen || shell {
        return None;
    }
    let path = window.path.trim();
    let program = if path.is_empty() { window.name.trim() } else { path };
    Some(format!("exe:{}", program.to_lowercase()))
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct FocusState {
    pub visible: bool,
    /// Index into the saved games.
    pub game: Option<usize>,
}

/// After the window in front changes. Nothing in front (the desktop, or no
/// window at all) hides the crosshair. Scorpio's own windows show it and keep
/// the game that was in focus. `detected` says whether an unsaved window
/// counts as a game (launcher install or fullscreen, not excluded).
pub fn next_state(previous: &FocusState, front: Option<&WindowInfo>, own_pid: u32, saved: &[Fingerprint], detected: impl Fn(&WindowInfo) -> bool) -> FocusState {
    let Some(front) = front else {
        return FocusState { visible: false, game: None };
    };
    if front.pid == own_pid {
        return FocusState { visible: true, game: previous.game };
    }
    match find_game(saved, front) {
        Some((index, _)) => FocusState { visible: true, game: Some(index) },
        None => FocusState { visible: detected(front), game: None },
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const OWN: u32 = 4242;

    fn window(pid: u32, name: &str, path: &str, title: &str, class: &str, fullscreen: bool) -> WindowInfo {
        WindowInfo { pid, name: name.into(), path: path.into(), title: title.into(), window_class: class.into(), fullscreen, monitor: None }
    }

    fn cs2() -> WindowInfo {
        window(20, "cs2", r"C:\Steam\steamapps\common\Counter-Strike Global Offensive\game\bin\win64\cs2.exe", "Counter-Strike 2", "SDL_app", true)
    }

    fn finals() -> WindowInfo {
        window(30, "Discovery", r"C:\Steam\steamapps\common\The Finals\Discovery.exe", "THE FINALS", "UnrealWindow", true)
    }

    fn browser() -> WindowInfo {
        window(10, "brave", r"C:\Brave\brave.exe", "New Tab - Brave", "Chrome_WidgetWin_1", false)
    }

    fn games() -> Vec<Fingerprint> {
        vec![fingerprint(&cs2()), fingerprint(&finals())]
    }

    #[test]
    fn two_of_four_checks_make_a_match_empty_checks_do_not_vote() {
        assert_eq!(compare(&fingerprint(&cs2()), &cs2()).agree, 4);
        let moved = WindowInfo { title: "CS2 - 300 fps".into(), path: r"D:\moved\cs2.exe".into(), ..cs2() };
        assert!(compare(&fingerprint(&cs2()), &moved).matches);
        assert!(!compare(&fingerprint(&cs2()), &WindowInfo { window_class: "SDL_app".into(), ..browser() }).matches);
        let bare = WindowInfo { pid: 1, name: "CS2.EXE".into(), path: cs2().path.to_uppercase().replace('\\', "/"), ..WindowInfo::default() };
        let v = compare(&fingerprint(&cs2()), &bare);
        assert_eq!((v.name, v.path, v.title, v.window_class), (Some(true), Some(true), None, None));
    }

    #[test]
    fn titles_and_classes_compare_exactly() {
        let shouting = WindowInfo { title: "COUNTER-STRIKE 2".into(), window_class: "sdl_app".into(), ..cs2() };
        let v = compare(&fingerprint(&cs2()), &shouting);
        assert_eq!((v.title, v.window_class, v.agree), (Some(false), Some(false), 2));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_best_agreeing_saved_game_wins() {
        let twin = Fingerprint { title: "x".into(), window_class: "y".into(), ..fingerprint(&cs2()) };
        let saved = [vec![twin], games()].concat();
        assert_eq!(find_game(&saved, &cs2()).map(|(i, _)| i), Some(1));
        assert_eq!(find_game(&games(), &browser()), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn focus_games_show_it_others_hide_it_scorpio_shows_it_and_keeps_the_game() {
        let saved = games();
        let never = |_: &WindowInfo| false;
        let step = |previous: &FocusState, front: Option<&WindowInfo>| next_state(previous, front, OWN, &saved, never);
        let own = WindowInfo { pid: OWN, name: "Scorpio".into(), ..WindowInfo::default() };

        let mut s = FocusState { visible: true, game: None };
        s = step(&s, Some(&browser()));
        assert_eq!(s, FocusState { visible: false, game: None });
        s = step(&s, Some(&cs2()));
        assert_eq!(s, FocusState { visible: true, game: Some(0) });
        s = step(&s, Some(&own));
        assert_eq!(s, FocusState { visible: true, game: Some(0) }, "settings in front: shown, same game");
        assert!(step(&step(&s, Some(&browser())), Some(&own)).visible, "settings opened over the browser: shown to tune it");
        assert_eq!(step(&s, None), FocusState { visible: false, game: None }, "the desktop or no window hides it");

        let other = WindowInfo { pid: 77, ..browser() };
        assert_eq!(next_state(&s, Some(&other), OWN, &saved, |w| w.pid == 77), FocusState { visible: true, game: None });
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_fullscreen_window_is_a_game_unless_it_is_the_desktop_or_the_shell() {
        assert_eq!(fullscreen_key(&cs2()), Some(format!("exe:{}", cs2().path.to_lowercase())));
        assert_eq!(fullscreen_key(&browser()), None);
        assert_eq!(fullscreen_key(&WindowInfo { name: "explorer".into(), window_class: "Progman".into(), ..cs2() }), None);
        assert_eq!(fullscreen_key(&WindowInfo { name: "plasmashell".into(), window_class: "plasmashell".into(), ..cs2() }), None);
        assert_eq!(fullscreen_key(&WindowInfo { name: "Game.exe".into(), path: String::new(), ..cs2() }), Some("exe:game.exe".into()));
    }

    #[test]
    fn only_a_name_or_a_path_identifies_a_program() {
        assert!(!identifiable(&WindowInfo { pid: 1, name: " ".into(), title: "t".into(), ..WindowInfo::default() }));
        assert!(identifiable(&cs2()));
    }
}

```

`src-tauri/src/core/launchers.rs`:

```rs
//! Games installed through launchers, found in the launchers' own files.
//! Parsers are pure; `scan()` reads the disk (files and, on Windows, `reg.exe query`).

use serde::Serialize;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct InstalledGame {
    /// "Steam", "Epic", "GOG", "Ubisoft", "Xbox", "Riot", "EA", "Battle.net", "Heroic", "Lutris".
    pub source: String,
    pub name: String,
    /// Install folder as a prefix: lower case, '/' separators, trailing '/'.
    pub key: String,
}

/// A folder as a comparable prefix: lower case, one separator style, trailing separator.
pub fn folder_key(folder: &str) -> String {
    let key = folder.trim().replace('\\', "/").trim_end_matches('/').to_lowercase();
    if key.is_empty() { key } else { key + "/" }
}

/// The installed game an executable belongs to: the deepest folder holding it.
pub fn game_at<'a>(executable: &str, games: &'a [InstalledGame]) -> Option<&'a InstalledGame> {
    let file = executable.trim().replace('\\', "/").to_lowercase();
    if file.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    games.iter().filter(|game| !game.key.is_empty() && file.starts_with(&game.key)).max_by_key(|game| game.key.len())
}

/// Every installed game Scorpio can find on this machine.
pub fn scan() -> Vec<InstalledGame> {
    let found = if cfg!(windows) {
        windows_scan()
    } else if cfg!(target_os = "linux") {
        linux_scan()
    } else {
        Vec::new()
    };
    unique(found)
}

/// The first game found for each folder; games without a folder dropped.
fn unique(found: Vec<InstalledGame>) -> Vec<InstalledGame> {
    let mut seen = std::collections::HashSet::new();
    found.into_iter().filter(|game| !game.key.is_empty() && seen.insert(game.key.clone())).collect()
}

fn game(source: &str, name: &str, folder: &str) -> InstalledGame {
    InstalledGame { source: source.to_owned(), name: name.trim().to_owned(), key: folder_key(folder) }
}

fn last_part(folder: &str) -> &str {
    folder.trim_end_matches(['/', '\\']).rsplit(['/', '\\']).next().unwrap_or("")
}

/// Steam installs tools and runtimes as apps too.
const NOT_GAMES: [&str; 14] = [
    "steamworks shared",
    "steamworks common redistributables",
    "wallpaper engine",
    "wallpaper_engine",
    "lossless scaling",
    "borderless gaming",
    "steamvr",
    "steam linux runtime",
    "steam linux runtime - soldier",
    "steam linux runtime - sniper",
    "proton experimental",
    "proton hotfix",
    "proton easyanticheat runtime",
    "proton battleye runtime",
];

fn is_tool(name: &str) -> bool {
    let name = name.trim().to_lowercase();
    let proton_version = name.strip_prefix("proton ").is_some_and(|rest| rest.starts_with(|c: char| c.is_ascii_digit()));
    NOT_GAMES.contains(&name.as_str()) || proton_version
}

/* Parsers */

/// Every value stored under `key` (any case) in a Valve KeyValues text.
fn vdf_values(text: &str, key: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut values = Vec::new();
    let mut pending: Option<String> = None;
    let mut chars = text.chars();
    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
        match c {
            '"' => {
                let token = vdf_string(&mut chars);
                match pending.take() {
                    Some(k) if k.eq_ignore_ascii_case(key) => values.push(token),
                    Some(_) => {}
                    None => pending = Some(token),
                }
            }
            '{' | '}' => pending = None,
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    values
}

/// The rest of a quoted string, with `\\` and `\"` unescaped.
fn vdf_string(chars: &mut std::str::Chars) -> String {
    let mut out = String::new();
    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
        match c {
            '"' => break,
            '\\' => match chars.next() {
                Some(next @ ('\\' | '"')) => out.push(next),
                Some(other) => out.extend(['\\', other]),
                None => out.push('\\'),
            },
            _ => out.push(c),
        }
    }
    out
}

fn steam_libraries(vdf: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    vdf_values(vdf, "path")
}

fn steam_game(acf: &str, library: &str) -> Option<InstalledGame> {
    let name = vdf_values(acf, "name").into_iter().next().unwrap_or_default();
    let dir = vdf_values(acf, "installdir").into_iter().next().unwrap_or_default();
    if dir.is_empty() || is_tool(&name) || is_tool(&dir) {
        return None;
    }
    let shown = if name.is_empty() { &dir } else { &name };
    Some(game("Steam", shown, &format!("{library}/steamapps/common/{dir}")))
}

fn json_str<'a>(value: &'a serde_json::Value, key: &str) -> &'a str {
    value.get(key).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str).unwrap_or("")
}

fn epic_game(text: &str) -> Option<InstalledGame> {
    let item: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(text).ok()?;
    let folder = json_str(&item, "InstallLocation");
    let name = json_str(&item, "DisplayName");
    let categories = item.get("AppCategories").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array);
    let is_game = categories.is_some_and(|list| list.iter().any(|c| c.as_str() == Some("games")));
    if folder.is_empty() || !is_game || is_tool(name) {
        return None;
    }
    Some(game("Epic", if name.is_empty() { last_part(folder) } else { name }, folder))
}

/// `reg query <key> /s /v <value>` output: one folder per subkey.
fn registry_folders(text: &str, value: &str, source: &str) -> Vec<InstalledGame> {
    text.lines()
        .filter_map(|line| registry_value(line, value))
        .filter(|folder| !is_tool(last_part(folder)))
        .map(|folder| game(source, last_part(folder), folder))
        .collect()
}

/// "    <value>    REG_SZ    <data>" -> data.
fn registry_value<'a>(line: &'a str, value: &str) -> Option<&'a str> {
    let rest = line.strip_prefix(char::is_whitespace)?.trim_start();
    let after_name = strip_word(rest, value)?;
    let data = strip_word(after_name, "REG_SZ")?.trim();
    (!data.is_empty()).then_some(data)
}

/// `text` without a leading `word` (any case) and the whitespace after it.
fn strip_word<'a>(text: &'a str, word: &str) -> Option<&'a str> {
    let head = text.get(..word.len())?;
    let rest = &text[word.len()..];
    (head.eq_ignore_ascii_case(word) && rest.starts_with(char::is_whitespace)).then(|| rest.trim_start())
}

/// Battle.net's product.db is protobuf with the install folders as plain text;
/// it also names their parents and its own folders, which are not games.
fn battle_net_games(db: &[u8]) -> Vec<InstalledGame> {
    let mut found: Vec<(String, String)> = Vec::new();
    for folder in printable_paths(db) {
        let key = folder_key(&folder);
        if !key.contains("battle.net") && !key.contains("programdata") && !is_bare_root(&key) && !found.iter().any(|(k, _)| *k == key) {
            found.push((key, last_part(&folder).to_owned()));
        }
    }
    let keys: Vec<String> = found.iter().map(|(k, _)| k.clone()).collect();
    found
        .into_iter()
        .filter(|(key, _)| !keys.iter().any(|other| other != key && other.starts_with(key.as_str())))
        .map(|(key, name)| InstalledGame { source: "Battle.net".to_owned(), name, key })
        .collect()
}

/// A drive, or one of the shared folders on it, that holds many programs.
fn is_bare_root(key: &str) -> bool {
    let rest = match key.as_bytes() {
        [drive, b':', b'/', ..] if drive.is_ascii_alphabetic() => &key[3..],
        _ => return false,
    };
    ["", "program files/", "program files (x86)/", "games/", "users/", "windows/"].contains(&rest)
}

/// Runs like `C:\x...` or `d:/x...`: a drive, a separator, then 2 to 200
/// printable ASCII characters up to the next unprintable byte.
fn printable_paths(db: &[u8]) -> Vec<String> {
    let printable = |b: u8| (0x20..=0x7e).contains(&b);
    let mut paths = Vec::new();
    let mut i = 0;
    while i + 3 <= db.len() {
        let starts = db[i].is_ascii_alphabetic() && db[i + 1] == b':' && matches!(db[i + 2], b'\\' | b'/');
        let end = (i + 3..db.len()).find(|&j| !printable(db[j])).unwrap_or(db.len());
        if starts && (2..=200).contains(&(end - i - 3)) {
            paths.push(String::from_utf8_lossy(&db[i..end]).into_owned());
            i = end;
        } else {
            i += 1;
        }
    }
    paths
}

/// X:\.GamingRoot: "RGBX", a little-endian u32 count, then UTF-16 folder names on that drive.
fn xbox_folders(bytes: &[u8]) -> Vec<String> {
    if bytes.len() < 8 || &bytes[..4] != b"RGBX" {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    let count = u32::from_le_bytes([bytes[4], bytes[5], bytes[6], bytes[7]]) as usize;
    let units: Vec<u16> = bytes[8..].chunks_exact(2).map(|pair| u16::from_le_bytes([pair[0], pair[1]])).collect();
    String::from_utf16_lossy(&units)
        .split('\0')
        .map(str::trim)
        .filter(|name| !name.is_empty())
        .take(count)
        .map(str::to_owned)
        .collect()
}

/// RiotClientInstalls.json: each game's folder is a key of associated_client.
fn riot_games(text: &str) -> Vec<InstalledGame> {
    let Ok(data) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(text) else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    let Some(clients) = data.get("associated_client").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object) else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    clients
        .keys()
        .filter_map(|folder| {
            let parts: Vec<&str> = folder.split(['/', '\\']).filter(|part| !part.is_empty()).collect();
            let last = *parts.last()?;
            let is_channel = last.eq_ignore_ascii_case("live") || last.eq_ignore_ascii_case("pbe");
            let name = if is_channel { parts.len().checked_sub(2).map(|i| parts[i])? } else { last };
            Some(game("Riot", name, folder))
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Heroic (Linux): legendaryConfig/installed.json and gog_store/installed.json.
fn heroic_games(text: &str, source: &str) -> Vec<InstalledGame> {
    let Ok(data) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(text) else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    let entries: Vec<&serde_json::Value> = match (data.get("installed").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array), data.as_object()) {
        (Some(list), _) => list.iter().collect(),
        (None, Some(map)) => map.values().collect(),
        _ => Vec::new(),
    };
    entries
        .into_iter()
        .filter_map(|entry| {
            let folder = json_str(entry, "install_path");
            let name = [json_str(entry, "title"), json_str(entry, "app_name"), last_part(folder)].into_iter().find(|n| !n.is_empty()).unwrap_or("");
            (!folder.is_empty() && !is_tool(name)).then(|| game(source, name, folder))
        })
        .collect()
}

/* Reading the disk: only the launchers' own files, never another process. */

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

fn read_text(file: &Path) -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(file).unwrap_or_default()
}

fn entries(dir: &Path, folders: bool) -> Vec<String> {
    let Ok(listing) = std::fs::read_dir(dir) else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    listing
        .flatten()
        .filter(|entry| entry.file_type().is_ok_and(|kind| kind.is_dir() == folders))
        .filter_map(|entry| entry.file_name().into_string().ok())
        .collect()
}

fn is_app_manifest(name: &str) -> bool {
    let lower = name.to_lowercase();
    let id = lower.strip_prefix("appmanifest_").and_then(|rest| rest.strip_suffix(".acf"));
    id.is_some_and(|id| !id.is_empty() && id.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()))
}

fn steam_from(roots: &[PathBuf]) -> Vec<InstalledGame> {
    let mut libraries: Vec<(String, PathBuf)> = Vec::new();
    for root in roots {
        let listed = steam_libraries(&read_text(&root.join("steamapps").join("libraryfolders.vdf")));
        for library in std::iter::once(root.clone()).chain(listed.into_iter().map(PathBuf::from)) {
            let key = folder_key(&library.to_string_lossy());
            if !libraries.iter().any(|(k, _)| *k == key) {
                libraries.push((key, library));
            }
        }
    }
    libraries
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|(_, library)| {
            let steamapps = library.join("steamapps");
            entries(&steamapps, false)
                .into_iter()
                .filter(|name| is_app_manifest(name))
                .filter_map(move |name| steam_game(&read_text(&steamapps.join(name)), &library.to_string_lossy()))
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        })
        .collect()
}

fn subfolder_games(source: &str, parents: &[PathBuf], skip: &[&str]) -> Vec<InstalledGame> {
    parents
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|parent| {
            entries(parent, true)
                .into_iter()
                .filter(|name| !skip.contains(&name.to_lowercase().as_str()) && !is_tool(name))
                .map(|name| game(source, &name, &parent.join(&name).to_string_lossy()))
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        })
        .collect()
}

#[cfg(windows)]
fn reg_query(args: &[&str]) -> String {
    use std::os::windows::process::CommandExt;
    const CREATE_NO_WINDOW: u32 = 0x0800_0000;
    std::process::Command::new("reg.exe")
        .arg("query")
        .args(args)
        .creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
        .output()
        .map(|out| String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).into_owned())
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

#[cfg(not(windows))]
fn reg_query(_args: &[&str]) -> String {
    String::new()
}

const DRIVES: [char; 6] = ['C', 'D', 'E', 'F', 'G', 'H'];

fn on_drives(folder: &str) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
    DRIVES.iter().map(|drive| PathBuf::from(format!("{drive}:\\{folder}"))).collect()
}

fn windows_scan() -> Vec<InstalledGame> {
    let steam = registry_value_in(&reg_query(&["HKCU\\Software\\Valve\\Steam", "/v", "SteamPath"]), "SteamPath")
        .map_or_else(|| "C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Steam".to_owned(), |path| path.replace('/', "\\"));
    let mut found = steam_from(&[PathBuf::from(steam)]);

    let epic = Path::new("C:\\ProgramData\\Epic\\EpicGamesLauncher\\Data\\Manifests");
    let items = entries(epic, false).into_iter().filter(|name| name.to_lowercase().ends_with(".item"));
    found.extend(items.filter_map(|name| epic_game(&read_text(&epic.join(name)))));

    let gog = reg_query(&["HKLM\\SOFTWARE\\WOW6432Node\\GOG.com\\Games", "/s", "/v", "path"]);
    found.extend(registry_folders(&gog, "path", "GOG"));
    let ubisoft = reg_query(&["HKLM\\SOFTWARE\\WOW6432Node\\Ubisoft\\Launcher\\Installs", "/s", "/v", "InstallDir"]);
    found.extend(registry_folders(&ubisoft, "InstallDir", "Ubisoft"));

    let xbox: Vec<PathBuf> = DRIVES
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|drive| {
            let root = format!("{drive}:\\");
            let listed = xbox_folders(&std::fs::read(format!("{root}.GamingRoot")).unwrap_or_default());
            listed.into_iter().map(move |name| Path::new(&root).join(name))
        })
        .collect();
    found.extend(subfolder_games("Xbox", &xbox, &["gamesave"]));

    found.extend(riot_games(&read_text(Path::new("C:\\ProgramData\\Riot Games\\RiotClientInstalls.json"))));
    found.extend(subfolder_games("Riot", &on_drives("Riot Games"), &["riot client"]));

    let mut ea = vec![PathBuf::from("C:\\Program Files\\EA Games"), PathBuf::from("C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Origin Games")];
    ea.extend(on_drives("EA Games"));
    found.extend(subfolder_games("EA", &ea, &[]));

    found.extend(battle_net_games(&std::fs::read("C:\\ProgramData\\Battle.net\\Agent\\product.db").unwrap_or_default()));
    found
}

/// The first `name` value in `reg query` output.
fn registry_value_in(text: &str, name: &str) -> Option<String> {
    text.lines().find_map(|line| registry_value(line, name)).map(str::to_owned)
}

fn linux_scan() -> Vec<InstalledGame> {
    let Some(home) = std::env::var_os("HOME").map(PathBuf::from) else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    let mut found = steam_from(&[
        home.join(".steam/steam"),
        home.join(".local/share/Steam"),
        home.join(".var/app/com.valvesoftware.Steam/.local/share/Steam"),
    ]);
    let heroic = home.join(".config/heroic");
    found.extend(heroic_games(&read_text(&heroic.join("legendaryConfig/legendary/installed.json")), "Heroic"));
    found.extend(heroic_games(&read_text(&heroic.join("gog_store/installed.json")), "Heroic"));
    // Lutris keeps its games under ~/Games by default
    found.extend(subfolder_games("Lutris", &[home.join("Games")], &[]));
    found
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn installed(source: &str, name: &str, key: &str) -> InstalledGame {
        InstalledGame { source: source.into(), name: name.into(), key: key.into() }
    }

    fn names(games: &[InstalledGame]) -> Vec<&str> {
        games.iter().map(|g| g.name.as_str()).collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn steam_libraries_then_games_from_app_manifests_tools_left_out() {
        let vdf = r#""0" { "path"  "C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Steam" } "1" { "path" "/home/me/.local/share/Steam" }"#;
        assert_eq!(steam_libraries(vdf), [r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam", "/home/me/.local/share/Steam"]);

        let acf = |name: &str, dir: &str| format!(r#""AppState" {{ "name" "{name}" "installdir" "{dir}" }}"#);
        assert_eq!(steam_game(&acf("THE FINALS", "The Finals"), r"C:\Steam"), Some(installed("Steam", "THE FINALS", "c:/steam/steamapps/common/the finals/")));
        assert_eq!(steam_game(&acf("Proton 9.0", "Proton 9.0"), "/s"), None);
        assert_eq!(steam_game(&acf("Steam Linux Runtime - Sniper", "SteamLinuxRuntime_sniper"), "/s"), None);
        assert_eq!(steam_game(&acf("Wallpaper Engine", "wallpaper_engine"), "/s"), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_value_that_reads_like_a_key_is_not_one() {
        let acf = r#""AppState" { "name" "path" "installdir" "Odd" "UserConfig" { "path" "x" } }"#;
        assert_eq!(vdf_values(acf, "path"), ["x"]);
        assert_eq!(vdf_values(acf, "INSTALLDIR"), ["Odd"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn epic_counts_only_games_gog_and_ubisoft_come_from_the_registry() {
        let fortnite = r#"{"DisplayName":"Fortnite","InstallLocation":"C:\\Epic\\Fortnite","AppCategories":["games"]}"#;
        assert_eq!(epic_game(fortnite).map(|g| g.name), Some("Fortnite".into()));
        assert_eq!(epic_game(r#"{"DisplayName":"Unreal Engine","InstallLocation":"C:\\UE","AppCategories":["engines"]}"#), None);
        assert_eq!(epic_game("{"), None);
        let reg = "    path    REG_SZ    C:\\GOG Games\\Witcher 3\r\n";
        assert_eq!(registry_folders(reg, "path", "GOG"), [installed("GOG", "Witcher 3", "c:/gog games/witcher 3/")]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn registry_output_is_read_line_by_line() {
        let text = "\r\nHKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE\\SOFTWARE\\WOW6432Node\\Ubisoft\\Launcher\\Installs\\635\r\n    InstallDir    REG_SZ    D:/Ubisoft/Far Cry 5/\r\n\r\nEnd of search: 1 match(es) found.\r\n";
        assert_eq!(registry_folders(text, "installdir", "Ubisoft"), [installed("Ubisoft", "Far Cry 5", "d:/ubisoft/far cry 5/")]);
        assert_eq!(registry_value_in("    SteamPath    REG_SZ    c:/program files (x86)/steam\r\n", "SteamPath").as_deref(), Some("c:/program files (x86)/steam"));
        assert_eq!(registry_folders("    pathology    REG_SZ    C:\\x\r\n", "path", "GOG"), []);
    }

    #[test]
    fn battle_net_keeps_install_folders_and_drops_their_parents_and_its_own() {
        let db = b"\x02C:/Program Files (x86)\x03C:/Program Files (x86)/StarCraft II\x04C:/ProgramData/Battle.net/Agent\x05D:/Games\x06D:/Games/Overwatch\x07";
        let found = battle_net_games(db);
        assert_eq!(names(&found), ["StarCraft II", "Overwatch"]);
        assert_eq!(found[0].source, "Battle.net");
        assert_eq!(game_at(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Google\Chrome\chrome.exe", &found), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn xbox_folders_from_gaming_root_riot_games_from_its_install_list() {
        let root: Vec<u8> = (0..56).step_by(2).map(|i| u8::from_str_radix(&"5247425801000000580062006f007800470061006d00650073000000"[i..i + 2], 16).unwrap()).collect();
        assert_eq!(xbox_folders(&root), ["XboxGames"]);
        assert!(xbox_folders(b"nope").is_empty());

        let riot = r#"{"associated_client":{"D:/Riot Games/VALORANT/live/":"x","C:\\Riot Games\\League of Legends":"y"}}"#;
        let found = riot_games(riot);
        let mut riot_names = names(&found);
        riot_names.sort_unstable();
        assert_eq!(riot_names, ["League of Legends", "VALORANT"]);
        assert!(riot_games("[").is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn heroic_lists_its_installed_games_on_linux() {
        let legendary = r#"{"Fortnite":{"title":"Fortnite","install_path":"/home/me/Games/Heroic/Fortnite"}}"#;
        let gog = r#"{"installed":[{"appName":"1","install_path":"/home/me/Games/Heroic/Cyberpunk 2077","title":"Cyberpunk 2077"}]}"#;
        assert_eq!(heroic_games(legendary, "Heroic"), [installed("Heroic", "Fortnite", "/home/me/games/heroic/fortnite/")]);
        assert_eq!(heroic_games(gog, "Heroic")[0].name, "Cyberpunk 2077");
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_executable_belongs_to_the_deepest_folder_that_holds_it_never_a_sibling() {
        let games = [
            installed("Steam", "Deadlock", "c:/steam/steamapps/common/deadlock/"),
            installed("X", "Outer", "d:/games/"),
            installed("Y", "Inner", "d:/games/inner/"),
        ];
        assert_eq!(game_at(r"C:\Steam\steamapps\common\Deadlock\bin\deadlock.exe", &games).map(|g| g.name.as_str()), Some("Deadlock"));
        assert_eq!(game_at("D:/Games/Inner/x.exe", &games).map(|g| g.name.as_str()), Some("Inner"));
        assert_eq!(game_at(r"C:\Steam\steamapps\common\Deadlock-other\x.exe", &games), None);
        assert_eq!(game_at(r"C:\Steam\steam.exe", &games), None);
        assert_eq!(game_at("  ", &games), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn folder_keys_and_duplicates() {
        assert_eq!(folder_key(r" C:\Games\Foo\\ "), "c:/games/foo/");
        assert_eq!(folder_key(""), "");
        let found = vec![installed("Steam", "A", "c:/a/"), installed("Epic", "A again", "c:/a/"), installed("EA", "none", ""), installed("EA", "B", "c:/b/")];
        assert_eq!(names(&unique(found)), ["A", "B"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn steam_and_subfolders_are_read_from_disk() {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("scorpio-launchers-{}", std::process::id()));
        let steamapps = root.join("Steam").join("steamapps");
        let second = root.join("Library2");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&steamapps).unwrap();
        std::fs::create_dir_all(second.join("steamapps")).unwrap();
        std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("Games").join("Celeste")).unwrap();
        std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("Games").join("Proton 8.0")).unwrap();
        let library = second.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "\\\\");
        std::fs::write(steamapps.join("libraryfolders.vdf"), format!(r#""libraryfolders" {{ "0" {{ "path" "{library}" }} }}"#)).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(steamapps.join("appmanifest_730.acf"), r#""AppState" { "name" "Counter-Strike 2" "installdir" "Counter-Strike Global Offensive" }"#).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(steamapps.join("appmanifest_x.acf"), r#""AppState" { "name" "Not a manifest" "installdir" "x" }"#).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(second.join("steamapps").join("appmanifest_1.acf"), r#""AppState" { "name" "Deadlock" "installdir" "Deadlock" }"#).unwrap();

        let mut steam = names(&steam_from(&[root.join("Steam")])).into_iter().map(str::to_owned).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        steam.sort();
        let lutris = subfolder_games("Lutris", &[root.join("Games")], &[]);
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(steam, ["Counter-Strike 2", "Deadlock"]);
        assert_eq!(names(&lutris), ["Celeste"]);
        assert!(lutris[0].key.ends_with("/games/celeste/"));
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/src/core/raster.rs`:

```rs
//! Drawings -> premultiplied BGRA bitmaps the overlay windows show as they are.

use super::crosshair::{Drawing, Piece};

/// Premultiplied BGRA, top row first, `width * 4` bytes per row: the layout
/// Windows' `UpdateLayeredWindow` and an X11 ARGB visual both take.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Bitmap {
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
    pub data: Vec<u8>,
}

impl Bitmap {
    pub fn transparent(width: u32, height: u32) -> Bitmap {
        Bitmap { width, height, data: vec![0; (width * height * 4) as usize] }
    }
}

/// Paints the pieces in order with "source over", each rect edge covering a
/// pixel by its exact fractional area, rings anti-aliased by distance.
/// `opacity` (0..1) scales the whole result.
pub fn rasterize(drawing: &Drawing, opacity: f32) -> Bitmap {
    let mut canvas = Canvas::new(drawing.size, drawing.size);
    for piece in &drawing.pieces {
        match *piece {
            Piece::Rect { x, y, w, h, rgba } => canvas.rect(x, y, w, h, straight(rgba)),
            Piece::Ring { cx, cy, r, width, rgba } => canvas.ring(cx, cy, r, width, straight(rgba)),
        }
    }
    canvas.into_bitmap(opacity)
}

/// What the crosshair window shows while unlocked: a stone slab held by iron
/// cramps, the crosshair centred on it, and a centre button. Returns the bitmap
/// and the button's rectangle (x, y, w, h) so the platform can hit-test it.
pub fn slab(crosshair: &Bitmap, width: u32, height: u32) -> (Bitmap, (i32, i32, i32, i32)) {
    let (w, h) = (width as f32, height as f32);
    let mut canvas = Canvas::new(width, height);
    canvas.rect(0.0, 0.0, w, h, STONE);
    canvas.edge(0.0, 0.0, w, h, MARBLE_EDGE);
    canvas.image((width as i64 - crosshair.width as i64) / 2, (height as i64 - crosshair.height as i64) / 2, crosshair);
    for x in [(w / 4.0).floor() - CRAMP.0 / 2.0, (w * 3.0 / 4.0).floor() - CRAMP.0 / 2.0] {
        canvas.cramp(x, 0.0);
        canvas.cramp(x, h - CRAMP.1);
    }
    let button = (((w - BUTTON) / 2.0).floor(), h - BUTTON - BUTTON_MARGIN);
    canvas.button(button.0, button.1);
    (canvas.into_bitmap(1.0), (button.0 as i32, button.1 as i32, BUTTON as i32, BUTTON as i32))
}

/// Straight RGBA, each channel 0..1.
type Colour = [f32; 4];

const STONE: Colour = [11.0 / 255.0, 10.0 / 255.0, 9.0 / 255.0, 0.62];
const MARBLE_EDGE: Colour = [235.0 / 255.0, 229.0 / 255.0, 218.0 / 255.0, 0.22];
const MARBLE: Colour = [235.0 / 255.0, 229.0 / 255.0, 218.0 / 255.0, 1.0];
const IRON: Colour = [0x4a as f32 / 255.0, 0x3d as f32 / 255.0, 0x2c as f32 / 255.0, 1.0];
const RIVET: Colour = [0xa0 as f32 / 255.0, 0x8a as f32 / 255.0, 0x63 as f32 / 255.0, 1.0];
const BUTTON_FILL: Colour = [6.0 / 255.0, 6.0 / 255.0, 5.0 / 255.0, 1.0];
/// Width and height of an iron cramp.
const CRAMP: (f32, f32) = (24.0, 7.0);
const RIVET_SIZE: f32 = 3.0;
const BUTTON: f32 = 26.0;
const BUTTON_MARGIN: f32 = 10.0;

fn straight([r, g, b, a]: [u8; 4]) -> Colour {
    [r, g, b, a].map(|channel| f32::from(channel) / 255.0)
}

/// How much of [a0, a1] lies inside [b0, b1].
fn overlap(a0: f32, a1: f32, b0: f32, b1: f32) -> f32 {
    (a1.min(b1) - a0.max(b0)).max(0.0)
}

/// Premultiplied RGBA, each channel 0..1, top row first.
struct Canvas {
    width: u32,
    height: u32,
    pixels: Vec<Colour>,
}

impl Canvas {
    fn new(width: u32, height: u32) -> Canvas {
        Canvas { width, height, pixels: vec![[0.0; 4]; (width * height) as usize] }
    }

    /// Source over, with the source already premultiplied.
    fn blend(&mut self, x: i64, y: i64, source: Colour) {
        if x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= i64::from(self.width) || y >= i64::from(self.height) {
            return;
        }
        let pixel = &mut self.pixels[(y as usize) * self.width as usize + x as usize];
        let keep = 1.0 - source[3];
        for (out, value) in pixel.iter_mut().zip(source) {
            *out = value + *out * keep;
        }
    }

    fn paint(&mut self, x: i64, y: i64, [r, g, b, a]: Colour, coverage: f32) {
        let alpha = a * coverage;
        if alpha > 0.0 {
            self.blend(x, y, [r * alpha, g * alpha, b * alpha, alpha]);
        }
    }

    /// Each pixel covered by the exact fraction of its area inside the rect.
    fn rect(&mut self, x: f32, y: f32, w: f32, h: f32, colour: Colour) {
        let columns = (x.floor().max(0.0) as i64)..(x + w).ceil().min(self.width as f32) as i64;
        let rows = (y.floor().max(0.0) as i64)..(y + h).ceil().min(self.height as f32) as i64;
        for py in rows {
            let cover_y = overlap(py as f32, py as f32 + 1.0, y, y + h);
            for px in columns.clone() {
                let cover_x = overlap(px as f32, px as f32 + 1.0, x, x + w);
                self.paint(px, py, colour, cover_x * cover_y);
            }
        }
    }

    /// Coverage from each pixel centre's distance to the circle: the share of a
    /// one-pixel span across the ring's line that falls on the line.
    fn ring(&mut self, cx: f32, cy: f32, r: f32, width: f32, colour: Colour) {
        let reach = r + width / 2.0 + 1.0;
        let (x0, x1) = ((cx - reach).floor().max(0.0) as i64, (cx + reach).ceil().min(self.width as f32) as i64);
        let (y0, y1) = ((cy - reach).floor().max(0.0) as i64, (cy + reach).ceil().min(self.height as f32) as i64);
        for py in y0..y1 {
            for px in x0..x1 {
                let d = (px as f32 + 0.5 - cx).hypot(py as f32 + 0.5 - cy) - r;
                self.paint(px, py, colour, overlap(d - 0.5, d + 0.5, -width / 2.0, width / 2.0));
            }
        }
    }

    /// A one-pixel line just inside the rect's border.
    fn edge(&mut self, x: f32, y: f32, w: f32, h: f32, colour: Colour) {
        self.rect(x, y, w, 1.0, colour);
        self.rect(x, y + h - 1.0, w, 1.0, colour);
        self.rect(x, y + 1.0, 1.0, h - 2.0, colour);
        self.rect(x + w - 1.0, y + 1.0, 1.0, h - 2.0, colour);
    }

    fn cramp(&mut self, x: f32, y: f32) {
        let (w, h) = CRAMP;
        self.rect(x, y, w, h, IRON);
        let top = y + ((h - RIVET_SIZE) / 2.0).floor();
        self.rect(x + RIVET_SIZE, top, RIVET_SIZE, RIVET_SIZE, RIVET);
        self.rect(x + w - 2.0 * RIVET_SIZE, top, RIVET_SIZE, RIVET_SIZE, RIVET);
    }

    /// The centre button: a dark square with a small marble cross in it.
    fn button(&mut self, x: f32, y: f32) {
        self.rect(x, y, BUTTON, BUTTON, BUTTON_FILL);
        self.edge(x, y, BUTTON, BUTTON, MARBLE_EDGE);
        let (c, gap, arm, t) = (BUTTON / 2.0, 2.0, 5.0, 2.0);
        let (cx, cy) = (x + c, y + c);
        self.rect(cx + gap, cy - t / 2.0, arm, t, MARBLE);
        self.rect(cx - gap - arm, cy - t / 2.0, arm, t, MARBLE);
        self.rect(cx - t / 2.0, cy + gap, t, arm, MARBLE);
        self.rect(cx - t / 2.0, cy - gap - arm, t, arm, MARBLE);
    }

    /// Lays a premultiplied BGRA bitmap over the canvas.
    fn image(&mut self, x: i64, y: i64, bitmap: &Bitmap) {
        for (i, bgra) in bitmap.data.chunks_exact(4).enumerate() {
            let (px, py) = ((i as u32 % bitmap.width) as i64, (i as u32 / bitmap.width) as i64);
            let [b, g, r, a] = [bgra[0], bgra[1], bgra[2], bgra[3]].map(|v| f32::from(v) / 255.0);
            if a > 0.0 {
                self.blend(x + px, y + py, [r, g, b, a]);
            }
        }
    }

    fn into_bitmap(self, opacity: f32) -> Bitmap {
        let opacity = opacity.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        let byte = |value: f32| (value * opacity * 255.0).round().clamp(0.0, 255.0) as u8;
        let data = self.pixels.iter().flat_map(|&[r, g, b, a]| [byte(b), byte(g), byte(r), byte(a)]).collect();
        Bitmap { width: self.width, height: self.height, data }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const WHITE: [u8; 4] = [255, 255, 255, 255];

    fn drawing(size: u32, pieces: Vec<Piece>) -> Drawing {
        let pixels = crate::core::crosshair::Pixels { length: 0, thickness: 0.0, gap: 0, outline: 1 };
        Drawing { size, pieces, pixels }
    }

    fn rect(x: f32, y: f32, w: f32, h: f32, rgba: [u8; 4]) -> Piece {
        Piece::Rect { x, y, w, h, rgba }
    }

    fn at(bitmap: &Bitmap, x: u32, y: u32) -> [u8; 4] {
        let i = ((y * bitmap.width + x) * 4) as usize;
        [bitmap.data[i], bitmap.data[i + 1], bitmap.data[i + 2], bitmap.data[i + 3]]
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_one_and_a_half_pixel_bar_paints_its_last_row_at_half() {
        let bitmap = rasterize(&drawing(6, vec![rect(1.0, 1.0, 3.0, 1.5, WHITE)]), 1.0);
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 2, 1), [255; 4]);
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 2, 2), [128; 4], "0.5 of 255 rounds to 128, premultiplied");
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 2, 3), [0; 4]);
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 0, 1), [0; 4]);
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 4, 1), [0; 4]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_fractional_left_edge_covers_by_area() {
        let bitmap = rasterize(&drawing(4, vec![rect(0.25, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0, WHITE)]), 1.0);
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 0, 0)[3], 191, "0.75 of 255");
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 1, 0)[3], 64, "0.25 of 255");
    }

    #[test]
    fn bytes_are_premultiplied_bgra_and_opacity_scales_them() {
        let red = rasterize(&drawing(2, vec![rect(0.0, 0.0, 2.0, 2.0, [255, 0, 0, 128])]), 1.0);
        assert_eq!(at(&red, 1, 1), [0, 0, 128, 128]);
        let faded = rasterize(&drawing(2, vec![rect(0.0, 0.0, 2.0, 2.0, WHITE)]), 0.5);
        assert_eq!(at(&faded, 0, 0), [128; 4]);
        assert_eq!(rasterize(&drawing(2, vec![rect(0.0, 0.0, 2.0, 2.0, WHITE)]), 0.0).data, vec![0; 16]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn later_pieces_go_over_earlier_ones() {
        let half_black = [0, 0, 0, 128];
        let half_white = [255, 255, 255, 128];
        let bitmap = rasterize(&drawing(1, vec![rect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0, half_black), rect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0, half_white)]), 1.0);
        // alpha 0.502 + 0.502 * 0.498; colour only from the white
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 0, 0), [128, 128, 128, 192]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn rects_outside_the_bitmap_are_clipped() {
        let bitmap = rasterize(&drawing(2, vec![rect(-5.0, -5.0, 6.0, 6.0, WHITE), rect(1.5, 1.0, 9.0, 9.0, WHITE)]), 1.0);
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 0, 0), [255; 4]);
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 1, 1), [128; 4]);
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 1, 0), [0; 4]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_ring_is_anti_aliased_by_distance_from_its_circle() {
        let ring = Piece::Ring { cx: 10.5, cy: 10.5, r: 5.0, width: 2.0, rgba: WHITE };
        let bitmap = rasterize(&drawing(21, vec![ring]), 1.0);
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 10, 15)[3], 255, "on the circle");
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 13, 14)[3], 255, "3-4-5: on the circle too");
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 10, 16)[3], 128, "one pixel out: half on the 2 px line");
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 10, 17)[3], 0);
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 10, 10)[3], 0, "the middle is empty");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_whole_crosshair_rasterizes_to_its_drawing_size() {
        let drawing = crate::core::crosshair::draw(&crate::core::crosshair::Crosshair::default(), 1080);
        let bitmap = rasterize(&drawing, 1.0);
        assert_eq!((bitmap.width, bitmap.height, bitmap.data.len()), (drawing.size, drawing.size, (drawing.size * drawing.size * 4) as usize));
        let c = drawing.size / 2;
        // Default: 2 px thick, gap 4 -> the right bar starts at c - 1 + 2 + 4
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, c + 5, c), [50, 250, 50, 255]);
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, c + 4, c), [0; 4]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_slab_has_stone_an_edge_cramps_the_crosshair_and_a_button() {
        let crosshair = Bitmap { width: 10, height: 10, data: [255u8; 4].repeat(100) };
        let (bitmap, button) = slab(&crosshair, 200, 120);
        assert_eq!((bitmap.width, bitmap.height), (200, 120));
        assert_eq!(button, (87, 84, 26, 26), "bottom centre");

        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 5, 60), [6, 6, 7, 158], "stone, premultiplied at 0.62");
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 0, 60)[3], 179, "the marble edge over the stone: 0.62 + 0.22 * 0.38");
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 100, 60), [255; 4], "the crosshair in the middle");
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 40, 1), [0x2c, 0x3d, 0x4a, 255], "a top cramp at a quarter of the width");
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 42, 3), [0x63, 0x8a, 0xa0, 255], "its left rivet");
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, 140, 116), [0x2c, 0x3d, 0x4a, 255], "a bottom cramp at three quarters");

        let (x, y) = (button.0 as u32, button.1 as u32);
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, x + 3, y + 3), [5, 6, 6, 255], "button fill");
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, x, y)[3], 255, "a sharp corner, not cut away");
        assert_eq!(at(&bitmap, x + 16, y + 12), [218, 229, 235, 255], "the marble glyph's right arm");
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/src/desktop_entry.rs`:

```rs
//! On Linux, an AppImage is just a file: nothing tells the desktop it is
//! there, so the app menu and its search can't find Scorpio. When run as an
//! AppImage, Scorpio writes its own desktop entry and icon into the user's
//! data folder (the .deb package installs its own entry instead).

use std::fs;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

const ICON: &[u8] = include_bytes!("../icons/128x128@2x.png");

pub fn install() {
    let Some(appimage) = std::env::var_os("APPIMAGE").map(PathBuf::from) else { return };
    let Some(data) = data_home() else { return };
    if let Err(error) = write(&data, &appimage) {
        log::warn!("desktop entry not written: {error}");
    }
}

fn data_home() -> Option<PathBuf> {
    std::env::var_os("XDG_DATA_HOME")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .filter(|p| p.is_absolute())
        .or_else(|| std::env::var_os("HOME").map(|home| Path::new(&home).join(".local/share")))
}

/// The entry, pointing at the AppImage wherever it is now. Written only when
/// it changed, so a normal start touches nothing.
fn entry(appimage: &Path) -> String {
    let exec = appimage.display().to_string().replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('"', "\\\"");
    format!(
        "[Desktop Entry]\nType=Application\nName=Scorpio\nGenericName=Crosshair Overlay\n\
         Comment=A CS2-style crosshair that shows only while your game is in front\n\
         Exec=\"{exec}\"\nIcon=scorpio\nTerminal=false\nCategories=Utility;Game;\n\
         Keywords=crosshair;overlay;cs2;counter-strike;reticle;aim;scorpio;\nStartupWMClass=scorpio\n"
    )
}

fn write(data: &Path, appimage: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    let desktop = data.join("applications/scorpio.desktop");
    let icon = data.join("icons/hicolor/256x256/apps/scorpio.png");
    let text = entry(appimage);
    if fs::read_to_string(&desktop).ok().as_deref() != Some(text.as_str()) {
        fs::create_dir_all(desktop.parent().expect("has a parent"))?;
        fs::write(&desktop, text)?;
    }
    if fs::metadata(&icon).map(|m| m.len()).ok() != Some(ICON.len() as u64) {
        fs::create_dir_all(icon.parent().expect("has a parent"))?;
        fs::write(&icon, ICON)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn writes_a_searchable_entry_once_and_follows_a_moved_appimage() {
        let data = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("scorpio-desktop-{}", std::process::id()));
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&data);
        write(&data, Path::new("/home/p/Apps/Scorpio.AppImage")).unwrap();
        let text = fs::read_to_string(data.join("applications/scorpio.desktop")).unwrap();
        assert!(text.contains("Exec=\"/home/p/Apps/Scorpio.AppImage\"") && text.contains("Keywords=crosshair;"));
        assert!(data.join("icons/hicolor/256x256/apps/scorpio.png").exists());
        write(&data, Path::new("/home/p/Games/Scorpio.AppImage")).unwrap();
        assert!(fs::read_to_string(data.join("applications/scorpio.desktop")).unwrap().contains("/home/p/Games/"));
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/src/lib.rs`:

```rs
pub mod app;
pub mod core;
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
mod desktop_entry;
pub mod platform;
pub mod obs;
pub mod store;
pub mod update;

use app::{App, Display, Effects, Hotkey, Status};
use core::crosshair::{self, Crosshair};
use core::focus::WindowInfo;
use platform::{Command, Platform};
use serde::Serialize;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::mpsc::channel;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use store::Settings;
use tauri::menu::{Menu, MenuItem};
use tauri::tray::{MouseButton, MouseButtonState, TrayIconBuilder, TrayIconEvent};
use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter, Manager, RunEvent, WebviewUrl, WebviewWindowBuilder};
use tauri_plugin_autostart::ManagerExt;
use tauri_plugin_global_shortcut::{GlobalShortcutExt, Shortcut, ShortcutState};

pub const SETTINGS_WINDOW: &str = "settings";

struct Shared {
    app: Mutex<App>,
    platform: Platform,
    path: PathBuf,
    /// Registered shortcut id -> what it does.
    hotkeys: Mutex<HashMap<u32, Hotkey>>,
    lock_item: MenuItem<tauri::Wry>,
    /// Held from reading the settings to writing them, so two threads saving
    /// at once can't write an older copy last.
    saving: Mutex<()>,
    obs: obs::Obs,
}

fn shared(handle: &AppHandle) -> tauri::State<'_, Shared> {
    handle.state::<Shared>()
}

/// Carries out what an app step decided: platform commands, saving, telling
/// the settings window.
fn apply(handle: &AppHandle, effects: Effects) {
    let state = shared(handle);
    let overlay_changed = !effects.commands.is_empty();
    for command in effects.commands {
        state.platform.send(command);
    }
    if overlay_changed || effects.settings_changed {
        let view = state.app.lock().unwrap().stream_view();
        state.obs.publish(serde_json::to_string(&view).unwrap_or_default());
    }
    if effects.settings_changed {
        let settings = persist(handle);
        let _ = handle.emit_to(SETTINGS_WINDOW, "settings", &settings);
    }
    if effects.status_changed || effects.settings_changed {
        let status = state.app.lock().unwrap().status();
        let _ = state.lock_item.set_text(if status.locked { "Unlock position" } else { "Lock position" });
        let _ = handle.emit_to(SETTINGS_WINDOW, "status", &status);
    }
}

/// Writes the current settings and returns what was written.
fn persist(handle: &AppHandle) -> Settings {
    let state = shared(handle);
    let _saving = state.saving.lock().unwrap();
    let settings = state.app.lock().unwrap().settings.clone();
    if let Err(error) = store::save(&state.path, &settings) {
        log::error!("could not save settings: {error}");
    }
    settings
}

fn primary_display(handle: &AppHandle) -> Option<Display> {
    let monitor = handle.primary_monitor().ok().flatten()?;
    let (position, size) = (monitor.position(), monitor.size());
    Some(Display { x: position.x, y: position.y, width: size.width, height: size.height })
}

/// Registers the player's shortcuts; returns the ones the system refused
/// (taken by another program, or not a valid key).
fn register_hotkeys(handle: &AppHandle, settings: &Settings) -> Vec<String> {
    let state = shared(handle);
    let shortcuts = handle.global_shortcut();
    let _ = shortcuts.unregister_all();
    // Built without the lock: registering waits on the main thread, and the
    // shortcut handler takes this lock there.
    let mut map = HashMap::new();
    let mut refused = Vec::new();
    for (id, accelerator) in settings.keys.all() {
        if accelerator.trim().is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        match accelerator.parse::<Shortcut>() {
            Ok(shortcut) if !map.contains_key(&shortcut.id()) && shortcuts.register(shortcut).is_ok() => {
                map.insert(shortcut.id(), Hotkey::from_id(id).expect("known id"));
            }
            _ => refused.push(id.to_string()),
        }
    }
    *state.hotkeys.lock().unwrap() = map;
    refused
}

fn sync_autostart(handle: &AppHandle, wanted: bool) {
    let launcher = handle.autolaunch();
    if launcher.is_enabled().unwrap_or(false) != wanted {
        let result = if wanted { launcher.enable() } else { launcher.disable() };
        if let Err(error) = result {
            log::warn!("autostart: {error}");
        }
    }
}

/// The settings window exists only while it is open: closing it destroys
/// the web view, so nothing of it stays in memory during play.
fn open_settings(handle: &AppHandle) {
    if let Some(window) = handle.get_webview_window(SETTINGS_WINDOW) {
        let _ = window.unminimize();
        let _ = window.show();
        let _ = window.set_focus();
        return;
    }
    let built = WebviewWindowBuilder::new(handle, SETTINGS_WINDOW, WebviewUrl::App("index.html".into()))
        .title(app::SETTINGS_TITLE)
        .inner_size(980.0, 700.0)
        .min_inner_size(760.0, 560.0)
        .decorations(false)
        .background_color(tauri::window::Color(11, 10, 9, 255))
        .center()
        .build();
    if let Err(error) = built {
        log::error!("could not open settings: {error}");
    }
}

/// The overlay goes away before Scorpio does (quit or update).
pub fn before_exit(handle: &AppHandle) {
    shared(handle).platform.send(Command::Hide);
}

pub fn settings_snapshot(handle: &AppHandle) -> Settings {
    shared(handle).app.lock().unwrap().settings.clone()
}

/// True while the crosshair is over a game (or a detected window).
pub fn game_in_front(handle: &AppHandle) -> bool {
    shared(handle).app.lock().unwrap().focus.visible
}

fn quit(handle: &AppHandle) {
    shared(handle).platform.send(Command::Quit);
    handle.exit(0);
}

/* Commands for the settings window */

#[derive(Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
struct View {
    settings: Settings,
    status: Status,
    styles: Vec<(u8, &'static str)>,
    display_height: u32,
    installed: usize,
    platform: &'static str,
    version: &'static str,
    update: update::UpdateState,
    obs_address: String,
}

#[tauri::command]
fn view(handle: AppHandle) -> View {
    let update = update::state(&handle);
    let state = shared(&handle);
    let app = state.app.lock().unwrap();
    View {
        settings: app.settings.clone(),
        status: app.status(),
        styles: crosshair::STYLES.iter().map(|(id, name, _)| (*id, *name)).collect(),
        display_height: app.display.height,
        installed: app.installed.len(),
        platform: std::env::consts::OS,
        version: env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"),
        update,
        obs_address: obs::address(),
    }
}

#[derive(Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
struct Saved {
    settings: Settings,
    /// Shortcuts that could not be registered.
    refused: Vec<String>,
    /// Why the OBS source could not start, if it couldn't.
    obs_error: Option<String>,
}

/// Turns the OBS source on or off as the settings say; on failure the
/// setting is turned back off.
fn sync_obs(handle: &AppHandle) -> Option<String> {
    let state = shared(handle);
    let wanted = state.app.lock().unwrap().settings.obs;
    let error = state.obs.set_enabled(wanted).err();
    if error.is_some() {
        state.app.lock().unwrap().settings.obs = false;
    }
    error
}

/// Saves new settings and returns them validated.
#[tauri::command]
fn save_settings(handle: AppHandle, settings: Settings) -> Saved {
    let state = shared(&handle);
    let (effects, keys_changed, settings) = {
        let mut app = state.app.lock().unwrap();
        let keys_changed = app.settings.keys != settings.keys;
        let effects = app.replace_settings(settings);
        (effects, keys_changed, app.settings.clone())
    };
    let refused = if keys_changed { register_hotkeys(&handle, &settings) } else { Vec::new() };
    sync_autostart(&handle, settings.start_with_system);
    let obs_error = sync_obs(&handle);
    // The window already has these settings: saved, but not echoed back.
    let settings = persist(&handle);
    apply(&handle, Effects { settings_changed: false, status_changed: true, ..effects });
    Saved { settings, refused, obs_error }
}

/// The crosshair as SVG, sized for the display it will be drawn on.
#[tauri::command]
fn preview(handle: AppHandle, crosshair: Crosshair) -> String {
    let height = shared(&handle).app.lock().unwrap().display.height;
    crosshair::to_svg(&crosshair::draw(&crosshair.normalized(), height))
}

#[derive(Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
struct ReadCode {
    format: &'static str,
    code_style: u8,
    crosshair: Crosshair,
}

#[tauri::command]
fn read_code(handle: AppHandle, code: String) -> Option<ReadCode> {
    let height = shared(&handle).app.lock().unwrap().display.height;
    crosshair::read_code(&code, height)
        .ok()
        .map(|decoded| ReadCode { format: decoded.format, code_style: decoded.code_style, crosshair: decoded.crosshair })
}

#[tauri::command]
fn write_code(crosshair: Crosshair) -> String {
    crosshair::write_code(&crosshair.normalized())
}

/// Windows open now, for "add a running game". Async so it runs off the
/// main thread.
#[tauri::command]
async fn running_windows(handle: AppHandle) -> Vec<WindowInfo> {
    let state = shared(&handle);
    let own = std::process::id();
    let mut windows: Vec<WindowInfo> = (state.platform.list_windows)().into_iter().filter(|w| w.pid != own).collect();
    windows.sort_by_key(|w| w.name.to_lowercase());
    windows
}

#[tauri::command]
fn add_window(handle: AppHandle, window: WindowInfo) -> bool {
    let state = shared(&handle);
    let added = state.app.lock().unwrap().add_game(&window);
    if added {
        let effects = Effects { settings_changed: true, status_changed: true, ..Effects::default() };
        apply(&handle, effects);
    }
    added
}

#[tauri::command]
fn set_locked(handle: AppHandle, locked: bool) {
    let effects = shared(&handle).app.lock().unwrap().set_locked(locked);
    apply(&handle, effects);
}

#[tauri::command]
fn pro_crosshairs() -> serde_json::Value {
    serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../pro-crosshairs.json")).unwrap_or(serde_json::Value::Null)
}

#[tauri::command]
async fn check_updates(handle: AppHandle) -> update::UpdateState {
    update::check(&handle).await
}

#[tauri::command]
async fn install_update(handle: AppHandle) {
    update::install_now(handle).await;
}

#[tauri::command]
fn quit_app(handle: AppHandle) {
    quit(&handle);
}

fn setup(handle: &AppHandle) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    desktop_entry::install();
    let path = handle.path().app_config_dir()?.join("settings.json");
    let settings = store::load(&path);

    let (events, received) = channel();
    let platform = platform::start(events).map_err(|unsupported| unsupported.0)?;

    let open = MenuItem::with_id(handle, "open", "Settings", true, None::<&str>)?;
    let lock_item = MenuItem::with_id(handle, "lock", "Unlock position", true, None::<&str>)?;
    let quit_item = MenuItem::with_id(handle, "quit", "Quit Scorpio", true, None::<&str>)?;
    let menu = Menu::with_items(handle, &[&open, &lock_item, &quit_item])?;

    let mut app = App::new(settings.clone(), std::process::id());
    if let Some(display) = primary_display(handle) {
        app.display = display;
    }
    handle.manage(Shared { app: Mutex::new(app), platform, path, hotkeys: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()), lock_item, saving: Mutex::new(()), obs: obs::Obs::default() });

    TrayIconBuilder::with_id("scorpio")
        .icon(handle.default_window_icon().cloned().ok_or("no icon")?)
        .tooltip("Scorpio")
        .menu(&menu)
        .show_menu_on_left_click(false)
        .on_tray_icon_event(|tray, event| {
            if let TrayIconEvent::Click { button: MouseButton::Left, button_state: MouseButtonState::Up, .. } = event {
                open_settings(tray.app_handle());
            }
        })
        .on_menu_event(|handle, event| match event.id.as_ref() {
            "open" => open_settings(handle),
            "lock" => {
                let effects = shared(handle).app.lock().unwrap().hotkey(Hotkey::Lock);
                apply(handle, effects);
            }
            "quit" => quit(handle),
            _ => {}
        })
        .build(handle)?;

    handle.manage(update::Updates::default());
    register_hotkeys(handle, &settings);
    sync_autostart(handle, settings.start_with_system);
    update::start(handle);
    if let Some(error) = sync_obs(handle) {
        log::warn!("OBS source not started: {error}");
    }

    // Platform events, one at a time, for as long as Scorpio runs.
    let events_handle = handle.clone();
    std::thread::Builder::new().name("scorpio-events".into()).spawn(move || {
        for event in received {
            let display_check = matches!(event, platform::Event::Foreground(_));
            let display = display_check.then(|| primary_display(&events_handle)).flatten();
            let effects = {
                let state = shared(&events_handle);
                let mut app = state.app.lock().unwrap();
                let mut effects = display.map(|d| app.set_display(d)).unwrap_or_default();
                let step = app.event(event);
                effects.commands.extend(step.commands);
                effects.settings_changed |= step.settings_changed;
                effects.status_changed |= step.status_changed;
                effects
            };
            apply(&events_handle, effects);
            if display_check {
                let (visible, manual) = {
                    let state = shared(&events_handle);
                    let app = state.app.lock().unwrap();
                    (app.focus.visible, app.settings.update_manually)
                };
                update::on_focus(&events_handle, visible, manual);
            }
        }
    })?;

    // Launcher folders are read once, off the main thread.
    let scan_handle = handle.clone();
    std::thread::Builder::new().name("scorpio-launchers".into()).spawn(move || {
        let installed = core::launchers::scan();
        let effects = shared(&scan_handle).app.lock().unwrap().set_installed(installed);
        apply(&scan_handle, effects);
    })?;

    // Show the crosshair state right away (centred, hidden until a game).
    let effects = shared(handle).app.lock().unwrap().set_display(primary_display(handle).unwrap_or_default());
    apply(handle, effects);
    Ok(())
}

pub fn run() {
    // The overlay needs X11; under Wayland the settings window joins it on
    // XWayland so both live on the same display server.
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    if std::env::var_os("WAYLAND_DISPLAY").is_some() && std::env::var_os("DISPLAY").is_some() {
        std::env::set_var("GDK_BACKEND", "x11");
    }

    let shortcuts = tauri_plugin_global_shortcut::Builder::new()
        .with_handler(|handle, shortcut, event| {
            if event.state != ShortcutState::Pressed {
                return;
            }
            let state = shared(handle);
            let hotkey = state.hotkeys.lock().unwrap().get(&shortcut.id()).copied();
            if let Some(hotkey) = hotkey {
                let effects = state.app.lock().unwrap().hotkey(hotkey);
                apply(handle, effects);
            }
        })
        .build();

    tauri::Builder::default()
        // The window library would otherwise register for raw input itself;
        // one process gets one raw input target, and it must be Scorpio's own.
        .device_event_filter(tauri::DeviceEventFilter::Always)
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_single_instance::init(|handle, _args, _cwd| open_settings(handle)))
        .plugin(shortcuts)
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_updater::Builder::new().build())
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_autostart::init(tauri_plugin_autostart::MacosLauncher::LaunchAgent, None))
        .invoke_handler(tauri::generate_handler![
            view,
            save_settings,
            preview,
            read_code,
            write_code,
            running_windows,
            add_window,
            set_locked,
            pro_crosshairs,
            check_updates,
            install_update,
            quit_app
        ])
        .setup(|app| {
            setup(app.handle())?;
            Ok(())
        })
        .build(tauri::generate_context!())
        .expect("Scorpio failed to start")
        .run(|_, event| {
            // Closing the settings window must not end Scorpio.
            if let RunEvent::ExitRequested { api, code: None, .. } = event {
                api.prevent_exit();
            }
        });
}
```

```rs
#![cfg_attr(not(debug_assertions), windows_subsystem = "windows")]

fn main() {
    scorpio_lib::run()
```

`src-tauri/src/obs.rs`:

```rs
//! The crosshair for OBS: a page OBS shows as a Browser source above its game
//! capture, kept in step with the real overlay through server-sent events.
//! OBS's game capture reads the game's own frames, so the overlay window is
//! never in them; this page puts the same crosshair into the stream instead.
//!
//! Only on while the player turns it on. It listens on 127.0.0.1 alone, talks
//! only to OBS on the same computer, and never touches a game.

use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader, Write};
use std::net::{Shutdown, TcpListener, TcpStream};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Condvar, Mutex};
use std::time::Duration;

pub const PORT: u16 = 47117;
const PING: Duration = Duration::from_secs(20);

pub fn address() -> String {
    format!("http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}/obs")
}

/// The latest state as JSON, with a version that grows on every change.
#[derive(Default)]
struct Shared {
    state: Mutex<(u64, String)>,
    changed: Condvar,
    stop: AtomicBool,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Obs {
    running: Mutex<Option<Arc<Shared>>>,
    /// Kept while off, so turning it on serves the current crosshair at once.
    last: Mutex<String>,
}

impl Obs {
    pub fn enabled(&self) -> bool {
        self.running.lock().unwrap().is_some()
    }

    /// Starts or stops the server; an error says why it could not start.
    pub fn set_enabled(&self, on: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
        let mut running = self.running.lock().unwrap();
        match (on, running.is_some()) {
            (true, false) => {
                let listener = TcpListener::bind(("127.0.0.1", PORT))
                    .map_err(|error| format!("Port {PORT} is taken by another program ({error})."))?;
                let shared = Arc::new(Shared::default());
                *shared.state.lock().unwrap() = (1, self.last.lock().unwrap().clone());
                let serving = Arc::clone(&shared);
                std::thread::Builder::new()
                    .name("scorpio-obs".into())
                    .spawn(move || serve(listener, serving))
                    .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
                *running = Some(shared);
            }
            (false, true) => {
                let shared = running.take().expect("checked above");
                shared.stop.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
                shared.changed.notify_all();
                // Wake the accept call so the thread sees the stop.
                let _ = TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", PORT));
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// The crosshair as the stream should show it, as JSON.
    pub fn publish(&self, json: String) {
        {
            let mut last = self.last.lock().unwrap();
            if *last == json {
                return;
            }
            last.clone_from(&json);
        }
        if let Some(shared) = self.running.lock().unwrap().as_ref() {
            let mut state = shared.state.lock().unwrap();
            *state = (state.0 + 1, json);
            shared.changed.notify_all();
        }
    }
}

fn serve(listener: TcpListener, shared: Arc<Shared>) {
    for stream in listener.incoming() {
        if shared.stop.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
            break;
        }
        let Ok(stream) = stream else { continue };
        let client = Arc::clone(&shared);
        let _ = std::thread::Builder::new().name("scorpio-obs-client".into()).spawn(move || answer(stream, client));
    }
}

/// The request's path, from its first line; the rest of the headers are read
/// and ignored.
fn request_path(stream: &TcpStream) -> Option<String> {
    let mut reader = BufReader::new(stream.try_clone().ok()?);
    let mut first = String::new();
    reader.read_line(&mut first).ok()?;
    let mut line = String::new();
    for _ in 0..64 {
        line.clear();
        if reader.read_line(&mut line).ok()? <= 2 {
            break;
        }
    }
    let mut parts = first.split_whitespace();
    (parts.next()? == "GET").then(|| parts.next().map(str::to_string)).flatten()
}

fn answer(mut stream: TcpStream, shared: Arc<Shared>) {
    let _ = stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5)));
    let path = request_path(&stream).unwrap_or_default();
    let result = match path.split('?').next().unwrap_or("") {
        "/obs" | "/" => respond(&mut stream, "200 OK", "text/html; charset=utf-8", PAGE.as_bytes()),
        "/events" => events(&mut stream, &shared),
        _ => respond(&mut stream, "404 Not Found", "text/plain", b"Not found"),
    };
    let _ = result;
    let _ = stream.shutdown(Shutdown::Both);
}

fn respond(stream: &mut TcpStream, status: &str, kind: &str, body: &[u8]) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    write!(stream, "HTTP/1.1 {status}\r\nContent-Type: {kind}\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nCache-Control: no-store\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n", body.len())?;
    stream.write_all(body)
}

/// Server-sent events: the current state now, then every change, with a
/// comment line now and then so a closed connection is noticed.
fn events(stream: &mut TcpStream, shared: &Shared) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    stream.write_all(b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Type: text/event-stream\r\nCache-Control: no-store\r\nConnection: keep-alive\r\n\r\n")?;
    let mut seen = 0;
    loop {
        let message = {
            let mut state = shared.state.lock().unwrap();
            while state.0 == seen && !shared.stop.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
                let (next, timeout) = shared.changed.wait_timeout(state, PING).unwrap();
                state = next;
                if timeout.timed_out() {
                    break;
                }
            }
            if shared.stop.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
                return Ok(());
            }
            if state.0 == seen {
                None
            } else {
                seen = state.0;
                Some(state.1.clone())
            }
        };
        match message {
            Some(json) => write!(stream, "data: {json}\n\n")?,
            None => stream.write_all(b": ping\n\n")?,
        }
        stream.flush()?;
    }
}

/// The Browser source page. Sized by OBS to the canvas; the crosshair is
/// scaled from the game's screen height to the canvas height, so it lands
/// where it does on the player's screen.
const PAGE: &str = r#"<!doctype html>
<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Scorpio crosshair</title>
<style>
html, body { margin: 0; height: 100%; overflow: hidden; background: transparent; }
#c { position: absolute; left: 50%; top: 50%; line-height: 0; }
#c svg { display: block; }
</style></head>
<body><div id="c"></div>
<script>
const box = document.getElementById('c')
let last = null
const draw = () => {
  const s = last
  if (!s || !s.visible || !s.svg) { box.innerHTML = ''; return }
  box.innerHTML = s.svg
  const svg = box.firstElementChild
  const k = innerHeight / s.height
  const w = svg.viewBox.baseVal.width, h = svg.viewBox.baseVal.height
  svg.setAttribute('width', w * k)
  svg.setAttribute('height', h * k)
  svg.style.shapeRendering = Math.abs(k - 1) < 0.001 ? 'crispEdges' : 'auto'
  box.style.transform = `translate(${-w * k / 2 + s.offset[0] * k}px, ${-h * k / 2 + s.offset[1] * k}px)`
}
const connect = () => {
  const source = new EventSource('/events')
  source.onmessage = e => { last = JSON.parse(e.data); draw() }
  source.onerror = () => { source.close(); box.innerHTML = ''; setTimeout(connect, 2000) }
}
addEventListener('resize', draw)
connect()
</script></body></html>
"#;

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::io::Read;

    fn get(path: &str) -> String {
        let mut stream = TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", PORT)).unwrap();
        write!(stream, "GET {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\n\r\n").unwrap();
        stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_millis(600))).unwrap();
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        let mut chunk = [0u8; 4096];
        while let Ok(n) = stream.read(&mut chunk) {
            if n == 0 {
                break;
            }
            out.extend_from_slice(&chunk[..n]);
        }
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&out).into_owned()
    }

    #[test]
    fn serves_the_page_streams_changes_and_stops() {
        let obs = Obs::default();
        obs.publish(r#"{"visible":true}"#.into());
        obs.set_enabled(true).unwrap();
        assert!(get("/obs").contains("EventSource('/events')"));
        assert!(get("/nothing").starts_with("HTTP/1.1 404"));

        let reader = std::thread::spawn(|| get("/events"));
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(150));
        obs.publish(r#"{"visible":false}"#.into());
        let stream = reader.join().unwrap();
        assert!(stream.contains(r#"data: {"visible":true}"#), "{stream}");
        assert!(stream.contains(r#"data: {"visible":false}"#), "{stream}");

        obs.set_enabled(false).unwrap();
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
        assert!(TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", PORT)).is_err(), "the port is closed when turned off");
    }
}
```
