`src-tauri/src/platform/linux/describe.rs`:

```rs
//! Describing a top-level window from window-manager properties, plus the
//! executable read from `/proc` (readlink of `exe`, `cmdline` for Wine). No
//! other process is ever opened or signalled.

use crate::core::focus::WindowInfo;
use x11rb::connection::Connection;
use x11rb::protocol::randr::ConnectionExt as _;
use x11rb::protocol::xproto::{Atom, AtomEnum, ConnectionExt, GetPropertyReply, Window};

x11rb::atom_manager! {
    pub Atoms: AtomsCookie {
        _NET_ACTIVE_WINDOW,
        _NET_CLIENT_LIST,
        _NET_WM_PID,
        _NET_WM_NAME,
        _NET_WM_STATE,
        _NET_WM_STATE_FULLSCREEN,
        UTF8_STRING,
        _SCORPIO_WAKE,
    }
}

fn property(conn: &impl Connection, window: Window, name: Atom, kind: Atom) -> Option<GetPropertyReply> {
    conn.get_property(false, window, name, kind, 0, 4096).ok()?.reply().ok()
}

/// `None` when the window no longer exists.
pub fn describe(conn: &impl Connection, atoms: &Atoms, window: Window) -> Option<WindowInfo> {
    let class = property(conn, window, AtomEnum::WM_CLASS.into(), AtomEnum::STRING.into())?;
    // WM_CLASS is "instance\0class\0".
    let window_class = class.value.split(|&b| b == 0).nth(1).map(lossy).unwrap_or_default();
    let pid = property(conn, window, atoms._NET_WM_PID, AtomEnum::CARDINAL.into())
        .and_then(|r| r.value32()?.next())
        .unwrap_or(0);
    let title = property(conn, window, atoms._NET_WM_NAME, atoms.UTF8_STRING)
        .filter(|r| !r.value.is_empty())
        .or_else(|| property(conn, window, AtomEnum::WM_NAME.into(), AtomEnum::ANY.into()))
        .map(|r| lossy(&r.value))
        .unwrap_or_default();
    let fullscreen = property(conn, window, atoms._NET_WM_STATE, AtomEnum::ATOM.into())
        .and_then(|r| r.value32().map(|mut states| states.any(|s| s == atoms._NET_WM_STATE_FULLSCREEN)))
        .unwrap_or(false);
    let path = if pid == 0 { String::new() } else { process_path(pid) };
    let monitor = monitor_of(conn, window);
    Some(WindowInfo { pid, name: file_stem(&path), path, title, window_class, fullscreen, monitor })
}

/// The RandR monitor holding the window's centre, else the primary one.
fn monitor_of(conn: &impl Connection, window: Window) -> Option<[i32; 4]> {
    let geometry = conn.get_geometry(window).ok()?.reply().ok()?;
    let origin = conn.translate_coordinates(window, geometry.root, 0, 0).ok()?.reply().ok()?;
    let cx = i32::from(origin.dst_x) + i32::from(geometry.width) / 2;
    let cy = i32::from(origin.dst_y) + i32::from(geometry.height) / 2;
    let monitors = conn.randr_get_monitors(geometry.root, true).ok()?.reply().ok()?.monitors;
    let rect = |m: &x11rb::protocol::randr::MonitorInfo| [i32::from(m.x), i32::from(m.y), i32::from(m.width), i32::from(m.height)];
    let holds = |m: &&x11rb::protocol::randr::MonitorInfo| {
        let [x, y, w, h] = rect(m);
        (x..x + w).contains(&cx) && (y..y + h).contains(&cy)
    };
    monitors.iter().find(holds).or_else(|| monitors.iter().find(|m| m.primary)).map(rect)
}

/// The window manager's list of managed top-level windows, minus our own.
pub fn list(conn: &impl Connection, atoms: &Atoms, root: Window) -> Vec<WindowInfo> {
    let own = std::process::id();
    let Some(reply) = property(conn, root, atoms._NET_CLIENT_LIST, AtomEnum::WINDOW.into()) else { return Vec::new() };
    let Some(windows) = reply.value32() else { return Vec::new() };
    windows.filter_map(|w| describe(conn, atoms, w)).filter(|info| info.pid != own).collect()
}

pub fn active_window(conn: &impl Connection, atoms: &Atoms, root: Window) -> Option<Window> {
    property(conn, root, atoms._NET_ACTIVE_WINDOW, AtomEnum::WINDOW.into())?.value32()?.next().filter(|&w| w != 0)
}

fn lossy(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    String::from_utf8_lossy(bytes).into_owned()
}

fn process_path(pid: u32) -> String {
    let Ok(exe) = std::fs::read_link(format!("/proc/{pid}/exe")) else { return String::new() };
    let exe = exe.to_string_lossy();
    let cmdline = if is_wine(&exe) { std::fs::read(format!("/proc/{pid}/cmdline")).unwrap_or_default() } else { Vec::new() };
    exe_path(&exe, &cmdline)
}

// Covers wine, wine64, wine-preloader and wine64-preloader (Proton included).
fn is_wine(exe: &str) -> bool {
    exe.rsplit('/').next().is_some_and(|file| file.starts_with("wine"))
}

/// The game's own path: for Wine the `.exe` it was started with, since `exe`
/// only names the Wine loader.
fn exe_path(exe: &str, cmdline: &[u8]) -> String {
    if !is_wine(exe) {
        return exe.to_string();
    }
    cmdline
        .split(|&b| b == 0)
        .map(String::from_utf8_lossy)
        .find(|arg| arg.to_ascii_lowercase().ends_with(".exe"))
        .map(|arg| match arg.get(..3) {
            Some(drive) if drive.eq_ignore_ascii_case("z:\\") => format!("/{}", arg[3..].replace('\\', "/")),
            _ => arg.into_owned(),
        })
        .unwrap_or_else(|| exe.to_string())
}

fn file_stem(path: &str) -> String {
    let file = path.rsplit(['/', '\\']).next().unwrap_or("");
    match file.rfind('.') {
        Some(dot) if dot > 0 => file[..dot].to_string(),
        _ => file.to_string(),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn native_binaries_keep_their_path() {
        assert_eq!(exe_path("/usr/bin/xterm", b""), "/usr/bin/xterm");
        assert_eq!(file_stem("/usr/bin/xterm"), "xterm");
    }

    #[test]
    fn wine_takes_the_exe_from_cmdline() {
        let cmd = b"Z:\\home\\me\\Games\\cs2.exe\0-novid\0";
        assert_eq!(exe_path("/opt/proton/bin/wine64-preloader", cmd), "/home/me/Games/cs2.exe");
        let cmd = b"C:\\Program Files\\Game\\Game.EXE\0";
        assert_eq!(exe_path("/usr/bin/wine64", cmd), "C:\\Program Files\\Game\\Game.EXE");
        assert_eq!(file_stem("C:\\Program Files\\Game\\Game.EXE"), "Game");
        assert_eq!(exe_path("/usr/bin/wineserver", b"wineserver\0"), "/usr/bin/wineserver");
    }

    #[test]
    fn stems() {
        assert_eq!(file_stem(""), "");
        assert_eq!(file_stem("/a/.hidden"), ".hidden");
        assert_eq!(file_stem("/a/b.tar.gz"), "b.tar");
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/src/platform/linux/input.rs`:

```rs
//! Aim buttons and hide keys from XInput2 raw events on the root window: the
//! same stream games read. Selected only while the app asks for something.

use super::super::{Event, MouseButton};
use x11rb::connection::Connection;
use x11rb::protocol::xinput::{self, ConnectionExt as _, XIEventMask};
use x11rb::protocol::xproto::{ConnectionExt as _, Keycode, Keysym, Window};
use x11rb::protocol::Event as XEvent;

const ALL_MASTER_DEVICES: u16 = 1;

pub struct Input {
    available: bool,
    mouse: Vec<MouseButton>,
    keys: Vec<String>,
    held_buttons: Vec<MouseButton>,
    held_keys: Vec<(u32, String)>,
    min_keycode: Keycode,
    per_keycode: usize,
    keysyms: Vec<Keysym>,
}

impl Input {
    pub fn new(conn: &impl Connection) -> Input {
        // Raw events reach a root-window listener without a grab from 2.1 on.
        let available = conn
            .xinput_xi_query_version(2, 2)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|c| c.reply().ok())
            .is_some_and(|v| (v.major_version, v.minor_version) >= (2, 1));
        let mut input = Input {
            available,
            mouse: Vec::new(),
            keys: Vec::new(),
            held_buttons: Vec::new(),
            held_keys: Vec::new(),
            min_keycode: 0,
            per_keycode: 1,
            keysyms: Vec::new(),
        };
        input.load_keymap(conn);
        input
    }

    pub fn load_keymap(&mut self, conn: &impl Connection) {
        let setup = conn.setup();
        let (min, max) = (setup.min_keycode, setup.max_keycode);
        if let Some(map) = conn.get_keyboard_mapping(min, max - min + 1).ok().and_then(|c| c.reply().ok()) {
            self.min_keycode = min;
            self.per_keycode = usize::from(map.keysyms_per_keycode.max(1));
            self.keysyms = map.keysyms;
        }
    }

    pub fn listen(&mut self, conn: &impl Connection, root: Window, mouse: Vec<MouseButton>, keys: Vec<String>) {
        let mut mask = XIEventMask::from(0u32);
        if !mouse.is_empty() {
            mask = mask | XIEventMask::RAW_BUTTON_PRESS | XIEventMask::RAW_BUTTON_RELEASE;
        }
        if !keys.is_empty() {
            mask = mask | XIEventMask::RAW_KEY_PRESS | XIEventMask::RAW_KEY_RELEASE;
        }
        self.held_buttons.retain(|b| mouse.contains(b));
        self.held_keys.retain(|(_, name)| keys.contains(name));
        self.mouse = mouse;
        self.keys = keys;
        if !self.available {
            if !(self.mouse.is_empty() && self.keys.is_empty()) {
                log::warn!("XInput 2.1 is missing: aim buttons and hide keys are not available");
            }
            return;
        }
        let masks = [xinput::EventMask { deviceid: ALL_MASTER_DEVICES, mask: vec![mask] }];
        let _ = conn.xinput_xi_select_events(root, &masks);
    }

    /// The change this event makes to what the app listens to, if any.
    pub fn handle(&mut self, event: &XEvent) -> Option<Event> {
        match event {
            XEvent::XinputRawButtonPress(e) => self.button(e.detail, true),
            XEvent::XinputRawButtonRelease(e) => self.button(e.detail, false),
            XEvent::XinputRawKeyPress(e) => self.key(e.detail, true),
            XEvent::XinputRawKeyRelease(e) => self.key(e.detail, false),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    fn button(&mut self, detail: u32, down: bool) -> Option<Event> {
        let button = match detail {
            1 => MouseButton::Left,
            2 => MouseButton::Middle,
            3 => MouseButton::Right,
            8 => MouseButton::Back,
            9 => MouseButton::Forward,
            _ => return None,
        };
        if !self.mouse.contains(&button) || self.held_buttons.contains(&button) == down {
            return None;
        }
        if down {
            self.held_buttons.push(button);
        } else {
            self.held_buttons.retain(|&b| b != button);
        }
        Some(Event::Mouse { button, down })
    }

    fn key(&mut self, keycode: u32, down: bool) -> Option<Event> {
        let index = (keycode.checked_sub(u32::from(self.min_keycode))? as usize) * self.per_keycode;
        let code = key_name(*self.keysyms.get(index)?)?;
        if !self.keys.contains(&code) {
            return None;
        }
        // Left and right Shift share a name: report the first press and the last release.
        let was_held = self.held_keys.iter().any(|(_, name)| *name == code);
        self.held_keys.retain(|&(k, _)| k != keycode);
        if down {
            self.held_keys.push((keycode, code.clone()));
        }
        let is_held = self.held_keys.iter().any(|(_, name)| *name == code);
        (was_held != is_held).then_some(Event::Key { code, down })
    }
}

/// Keysym -> the platform-neutral names keybinds use.
fn key_name(sym: Keysym) -> Option<String> {
    let name = match sym {
        0x30..=0x39 | 0x41..=0x5a => return Some(char::from(sym as u8).to_string()),
        0x61..=0x7a => return Some(char::from(sym as u8 - 0x20).to_string()),
        0xffbe..=0xffd5 => return Some(format!("F{}", sym - 0xffbe + 1)),
        0xffe1 | 0xffe2 => "Shift",
        0xffe3 | 0xffe4 => "Control",
        0xffe9 | 0xffea => "Alt",
        0xffeb | 0xffec => "Super",
        0x20 => "Space",
        0xff0d => "Enter",
        0xff1b => "Escape",
        0xff09 => "Tab",
        0xff08 => "Backspace",
        0xffe5 => "CapsLock",
        0xff51 => "Left",
        0xff52 => "Up",
        0xff53 => "Right",
        0xff54 => "Down",
        0xff63 => "Insert",
        0xffff => "Delete",
        0xff50 => "Home",
        0xff57 => "End",
        0xff55 => "PageUp",
        0xff56 => "PageDown",
        _ => return None,
    };
    Some(name.to_string())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn names() {
        assert_eq!(key_name(0x68).as_deref(), Some("H"));
        assert_eq!(key_name(0xffc2).as_deref(), Some("F5"));
        assert_eq!(key_name(0xffe2).as_deref(), Some("Shift"));
        assert_eq!(key_name(0x1234), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn reports_changes_only() {
        let mut input = Input {
            available: false,
            mouse: vec![MouseButton::Right],
            keys: vec!["Shift".into()],
            held_buttons: Vec::new(),
            held_keys: Vec::new(),
            min_keycode: 8,
            per_keycode: 1,
            keysyms: vec![0xffe1, 0xffe2, 0x61],
        };
        assert_eq!(input.button(3, true), Some(Event::Mouse { button: MouseButton::Right, down: true }));
        assert_eq!(input.button(3, true), None);
        assert_eq!(input.button(1, true), None);
        assert!(input.key(8, true).is_some());
        assert_eq!(input.key(9, true), None);
        assert_eq!(input.key(8, false), None);
        assert_eq!(input.key(9, false), Some(Event::Key { code: "Shift".into(), down: false }));
        assert_eq!(input.key(10, true), None);
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/src/platform/linux/mod.rs`:

```rs
//! X11 (and XWayland). One thread owns the X connection and blocks on it;
//! `wake` reaches it with a ClientMessage sent over a second connection, which
//! also serves `list_windows`.

mod describe;
mod input;
mod overlay;

use super::{Command, Event, Platform, Unsupported};
use crate::core::focus::WindowInfo;
use describe::Atoms;
use input::Input;
use overlay::Overlay;
use std::error::Error;
use std::sync::mpsc::{channel, Receiver, Sender, TryRecvError};
use std::sync::Arc;
use x11rb::connection::Connection;
use x11rb::protocol::randr::ConnectionExt as _;
use x11rb::protocol::xproto::{AtomEnum, ChangeWindowAttributesAux, ClientMessageEvent, ConnectionExt as _, EventMask, Window};
use x11rb::protocol::Event as XEvent;
use x11rb::rust_connection::RustConnection;

struct State {
    conn: RustConnection,
    atoms: Atoms,
    root: Window,
    overlay: Overlay,
    input: Input,
    active: Option<Window>,
    last: Option<Option<WindowInfo>>,
    events: Sender<Event>,
}

pub fn start(events: Sender<Event>) -> Result<Platform, Unsupported> {
    if std::env::var_os("DISPLAY").is_none() {
        return Err(Unsupported("Scorpio needs X11 or XWayland, and DISPLAY is not set (Wayland without XWayland is not supported)".into()));
    }
    let connect = || RustConnection::connect(None).map_err(|e| Unsupported(format!("cannot connect to the X server: {e}")));
    let (conn, screen_num) = connect()?;
    let (helper, _) = connect()?;
    let state = setup(conn, screen_num, events).map_err(|e| Unsupported(format!("X11 setup failed: {e}")))?;
    let (atoms, root, target) = (state.atoms, state.root, state.overlay.window);

    let (commands, receiver) = channel();
    std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("scorpio-x11".into())
        .spawn(move || run(state, receiver))
        .map_err(|e| Unsupported(format!("cannot start the X11 thread: {e}")))?;

    let helper = Arc::new(helper);
    let lister = Arc::clone(&helper);
    Ok(Platform {
        commands,
        wake: Box::new(move || {
            // An empty event mask delivers the event to the window's creator: our thread.
            let message = ClientMessageEvent::new(32, target, atoms._SCORPIO_WAKE, [0u32; 5]);
            let _ = helper.send_event(false, target, EventMask::NO_EVENT, message);
            let _ = helper.flush();
        }),
        list_windows: Box::new(move || describe::list(&*lister, &atoms, root)),
    })
}

fn setup(conn: RustConnection, screen_num: usize, events: Sender<Event>) -> Result<State, Box<dyn Error>> {
    let atoms = Atoms::new(&conn)?.reply()?;
    let root = conn.setup().roots[screen_num].root;
    let overlay = Overlay::create(&conn, screen_num)?;
    let input = Input::new(&conn);
    conn.change_window_attributes(root, &ChangeWindowAttributesAux::new().event_mask(EventMask::PROPERTY_CHANGE))?;
    // A resolution or monitor change moves the centre; without RandR it is
    // simply not seen.
    let _ = conn.randr_select_input(root, x11rb::protocol::randr::NotifyMask::SCREEN_CHANGE);
    conn.flush()?;
    Ok(State { conn, atoms, root, overlay, input, active: None, last: None, events })
}

fn run(mut state: State, commands: Receiver<Command>) {
    state.refresh_foreground();
    loop {
        // Commands are drained on every wake-up, so a lost wake only delays one.
        loop {
            match commands.try_recv() {
                Ok(Command::Quit) | Err(TryRecvError::Disconnected) => return,
                Ok(command) => state.apply(command),
                Err(TryRecvError::Empty) => break,
            }
        }
        if state.conn.flush().is_err() {
            return;
        }
        match state.conn.wait_for_event() {
            Ok(event) => state.handle(event),
            Err(e) => {
                log::error!("X connection lost: {e}");
                return;
            }
        }
    }
}

impl State {
    fn apply(&mut self, command: Command) {
        let result = match command {
            Command::Show { bitmap, x, y, unlocked, button } => self.overlay.show(&self.conn, &bitmap, x, y, unlocked, button),
            Command::Hide => self.overlay.hide(&self.conn),
            Command::ListenTo { mouse, keys } => {
                self.input.listen(&self.conn, self.root, mouse, keys);
                Ok(())
            }
            Command::Raise => self.overlay.raise(&self.conn),
            // X11 has no way to keep a window out of captures.
            Command::ExcludeFromCapture(_) | Command::Quit => Ok(()),
        };
        if let Err(e) = result {
            log::warn!("overlay command failed: {e}");
        }
    }

    fn handle(&mut self, event: XEvent) {
        let reply = match &event {
            XEvent::PropertyNotify(e) if e.window == self.root && e.atom == self.atoms._NET_ACTIVE_WINDOW => {
                self.refresh_foreground();
                None
            }
            // Title and fullscreen changes of the window in front.
            XEvent::PropertyNotify(e)
                if Some(e.window) == self.active
                    && [self.atoms._NET_WM_NAME, self.atoms._NET_WM_STATE, u32::from(AtomEnum::WM_NAME)].contains(&e.atom) =>
            {
                self.refresh_foreground();
                None
            }
            XEvent::RandrScreenChangeNotify(_) => {
                self.last = None;
                self.refresh_foreground();
                None
            }
            XEvent::MappingNotify(_) => {
                self.input.load_keymap(&self.conn);
                None
            }
            XEvent::Error(e) => {
                // Mostly BadWindow from a window that closed while we read it.
                log::debug!("X error: {e:?}");
                None
            }
            _ => self.input.handle(&event).or_else(|| self.overlay.handle(&self.conn, &event).unwrap_or_else(|e| {
                log::warn!("overlay event failed: {e}");
                None
            })),
        };
        if let Some(reply) = reply {
            let _ = self.events.send(reply);
        }
    }

    fn refresh_foreground(&mut self) {
        // Some window managers name the root when the desktop is in front.
        let active = describe::active_window(&self.conn, &self.atoms, self.root).filter(|&w| w != self.root);
        if active != self.active {
            // Watch the window in front for its own property changes only.
            let watch = |w, mask| self.conn.change_window_attributes(w, &ChangeWindowAttributesAux::new().event_mask(mask)).map(|_| ());
            if let Some(old) = self.active {
                let _ = watch(old, EventMask::NO_EVENT);
            }
            if let Some(new) = active {
                let _ = watch(new, EventMask::PROPERTY_CHANGE);
            }
            self.active = active;
        }
        let info = active.and_then(|w| describe::describe(&self.conn, &self.atoms, w));
        if self.last.as_ref() != Some(&info) {
            self.last = Some(info.clone());
            let _ = self.events.send(Event::Foreground(info));
        }
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/src/platform/linux/overlay.rs`:

```rs
//! The crosshair window: override-redirect (so no window manager, taskbar or
//! focus), 32-bit ARGB, and an empty input shape while locked so every click
//! goes to the game underneath.

use super::super::Event;
use crate::core::raster::Bitmap;
use std::error::Error;
use x11rb::connection::Connection;
use x11rb::protocol::shape::{ConnectionExt as _, SK, SO};
use x11rb::protocol::xproto::*;
use x11rb::protocol::Event as XEvent;
use x11rb::wrapper::ConnectionExt as _;
use x11rb::NONE;

type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, Box<dyn Error>>;

/// Pointer travel (pixels) after which a press is a drag, not a click.
const DRAG_THRESHOLD: i32 = 3;

struct Drag {
    start: (i32, i32),
    origin: (i32, i32),
    moved: bool,
}

pub struct Overlay {
    pub window: Window,
    gc: Gcontext,
    compositor: Atom,
    swap_bytes: bool,
    /// Pixels as the server wants them, kept to repaint on Expose.
    pixels: Vec<u8>,
    width: u16,
    height: u16,
    x: i32,
    y: i32,
    mapped: bool,
    unlocked: bool,
    button: Option<(i32, i32, i32, i32)>,
    drag: Option<Drag>,
}

impl Overlay {
    pub fn create(conn: &impl Connection, screen_num: usize) -> Result<Overlay> {
        let screen = &conn.setup().roots[screen_num];
        let visual = screen
            .allowed_depths
            .iter()
            .filter(|d| d.depth == 32)
            .flat_map(|d| &d.visuals)
            .find(|v| v.class == VisualClass::TRUE_COLOR)
            .ok_or("the X server has no 32-bit TrueColor visual")?
            .visual_id;
        let colormap = conn.generate_id()?;
        conn.create_colormap(ColormapAlloc::NONE, colormap, screen.root, visual)?;
        let window = conn.generate_id()?;
        // A depth other than the root's needs an explicit border pixel and colormap.
        let aux = CreateWindowAux::new()
            .background_pixel(0)
            .border_pixel(0)
            .override_redirect(1)
            .colormap(colormap)
            .event_mask(EventMask::EXPOSURE | EventMask::BUTTON_PRESS | EventMask::BUTTON_RELEASE | EventMask::BUTTON1_MOTION);
        conn.create_window(32, window, screen.root, 0, 0, 1, 1, 0, WindowClass::INPUT_OUTPUT, visual, &aux)?;
        conn.change_property8(PropMode::REPLACE, window, AtomEnum::WM_NAME, AtomEnum::STRING, b"Scorpio overlay")?;
        conn.change_property8(PropMode::REPLACE, window, AtomEnum::WM_CLASS, AtomEnum::STRING, b"scorpio\0Scorpio\0")?;
        let gc = conn.generate_id()?;
        conn.create_gc(gc, window, &CreateGCAux::new())?;
        let compositor = conn.intern_atom(false, format!("_NET_WM_CM_S{screen_num}").as_bytes())?.reply()?.atom;
        Ok(Overlay {
            window,
            gc,
            compositor,
            swap_bytes: conn.setup().image_byte_order == ImageOrder::MSB_FIRST,
            pixels: Vec::new(),
            width: 0,
            height: 0,
            x: 0,
            y: 0,
            mapped: false,
            unlocked: false,
            button: None,
            drag: None,
        })
    }

    pub fn show(&mut self, conn: &impl Connection, bitmap: &Bitmap, x: i32, y: i32, unlocked: bool, button: Option<(i32, i32, i32, i32)>) -> Result<()> {
        let (Ok(width @ 1..), Ok(height @ 1..)) = (u16::try_from(bitmap.width), u16::try_from(bitmap.height)) else {
            return self.hide(conn);
        };
        (self.width, self.height, self.x, self.y) = (width, height, x, y);
        (self.unlocked, self.button) = (unlocked, button);
        if !unlocked {
            self.drag = None;
        }
        conn.configure_window(
            self.window,
            &ConfigureWindowAux::new().x(x).y(y).width(u32::from(width)).height(u32::from(height)).stack_mode(StackMode::ABOVE),
        )?;
        if unlocked {
            conn.shape_mask(SO::SET, SK::INPUT, self.window, 0, 0, NONE)?;
        } else {
            conn.shape_rectangles(SO::SET, SK::INPUT, ClipOrdering::UNSORTED, self.window, 0, 0, &[])?;
        }
        // Without a compositor alpha is ignored and the window would be a black
        // square: cut it to the painted pixels instead.
        if conn.get_selection_owner(self.compositor)?.reply()?.owner == NONE {
            conn.shape_rectangles(SO::SET, SK::BOUNDING, ClipOrdering::YX_BANDED, self.window, 0, 0, &painted(bitmap))?;
        } else {
            conn.shape_mask(SO::SET, SK::BOUNDING, self.window, 0, 0, NONE)?;
        }
        self.pixels.clone_from(&bitmap.data);
        if self.swap_bytes {
            self.pixels.chunks_exact_mut(4).for_each(|p| p.reverse());
        }
        if !self.mapped {
            conn.map_window(self.window)?;
            self.mapped = true;
        }
        self.paint(conn)
    }

    /// Back above every other window, without moving or repainting.
    pub fn raise(&mut self, conn: &impl Connection) -> Result<()> {
        if self.mapped {
            conn.configure_window(self.window, &ConfigureWindowAux::new().stack_mode(StackMode::ABOVE))?;
            conn.flush()?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn hide(&mut self, conn: &impl Connection) -> Result<()> {
        self.drag = None;
        if self.mapped {
            conn.unmap_window(self.window)?;
            self.mapped = false;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn paint(&self, conn: &impl Connection) -> Result<()> {
        let stride = usize::from(self.width) * 4;
        if !self.mapped || stride == 0 {
            return Ok(());
        }
        // Large bitmaps are sent in bands that fit the server's request limit.
        let rows = ((conn.maximum_request_bytes() - 64) / stride).max(1);
        for (band, data) in self.pixels.chunks(rows * stride).enumerate() {
            let y = i16::try_from(band * rows)?;
            let height = u16::try_from(data.len() / stride)?;
            conn.put_image(ImageFormat::Z_PIXMAP, self.window, self.gc, self.width, height, 0, y, 0, 32, data)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Handles events for the overlay window; returns what the app should hear.
    pub fn handle(&mut self, conn: &impl Connection, event: &XEvent) -> Result<Option<Event>> {
        match event {
            XEvent::Expose(e) if e.window == self.window && e.count == 0 => self.paint(conn)?,
            XEvent::ButtonPress(e) if e.event == self.window && e.detail == 1 && self.unlocked => {
                self.drag = Some(Drag { start: (e.root_x.into(), e.root_y.into()), origin: (self.x, self.y), moved: false });
            }
            XEvent::MotionNotify(e) if e.event == self.window => {
                if let Some(drag) = &mut self.drag {
                    let (dx, dy) = (i32::from(e.root_x) - drag.start.0, i32::from(e.root_y) - drag.start.1);
                    drag.moved |= dx.abs() + dy.abs() > DRAG_THRESHOLD;
                    if drag.moved {
                        (self.x, self.y) = (drag.origin.0 + dx, drag.origin.1 + dy);
                        conn.configure_window(self.window, &ConfigureWindowAux::new().x(self.x).y(self.y))?;
                    }
                }
            }
            XEvent::ButtonRelease(e) if e.event == self.window && e.detail == 1 => {
                if let Some(drag) = self.drag.take() {
                    if drag.moved {
                        return Ok(Some(Event::Moved { x: self.x, y: self.y }));
                    }
                    let (px, py) = (i32::from(e.event_x), i32::from(e.event_y));
                    if self.button.is_some_and(|(bx, by, bw, bh)| px >= bx && px < bx + bw && py >= by && py < by + bh) {
                        return Ok(Some(Event::CenterClicked));
                    }
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        Ok(None)
    }
}

/// Runs of non-transparent pixels, one rectangle per run, row by row.
fn painted(bitmap: &Bitmap) -> Vec<Rectangle> {
    let width = bitmap.width as usize;
    let mut rects = Vec::new();
    for (y, row) in bitmap.data.chunks_exact(width * 4).enumerate() {
        let mut x = 0;
        while x < width {
            if row[x * 4 + 3] == 0 {
                x += 1;
                continue;
            }
            let start = x;
            while x < width && row[x * 4 + 3] != 0 {
                x += 1;
            }
            rects.push(Rectangle { x: start as i16, y: y as i16, width: (x - start) as u16, height: 1 });
        }
    }
    rects
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn painted_runs() {
        let mut bitmap = Bitmap::transparent(4, 2);
        for i in [0, 1, 3, 6] {
            bitmap.data[i * 4 + 3] = 255;
        }
        let rects: Vec<_> = painted(&bitmap).iter().map(|r| (r.x, r.y, r.width)).collect();
        assert_eq!(rects, [(0, 0, 2), (3, 0, 1), (2, 1, 1)]);
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/src/platform/mod.rs`:

```rs
//! The only code that talks to the operating system. Everything here must pass
//! the anti-cheat review in docs/plans/scorpio.md: never open another process,
//! never hook or inject, never send input, nothing that polls.
//!
//! Each platform runs one background thread that owns the overlay window and
//! listens for system events; the app talks to it through `Platform`.

use crate::core::focus::WindowInfo;
use crate::core::raster::Bitmap;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::sync::mpsc::Sender;

#[cfg(windows)]
mod windows;
#[cfg(windows)]
pub use self::windows::start;

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
mod linux;
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub use self::linux::start;

#[cfg(not(any(windows, target_os = "linux")))]
mod stub;
#[cfg(not(any(windows, target_os = "linux")))]
pub use self::stub::start;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum MouseButton {
    Left,
    Right,
    Middle,
    Back,
    Forward,
}

/// What the platform thread tells the app.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Event {
    /// The window in front changed; None when nothing (or the desktop) is.
    Foreground(Option<WindowInfo>),
    /// A mouse button anywhere, read from raw input (never a hook).
    Mouse { button: MouseButton, down: bool },
    /// A key anywhere, read from raw input; `code` is a platform-neutral key
    /// name as in keybinds ("H", "F5", "Shift", "Mouse4"...).
    Key { code: String, down: bool },
    /// The unlocked overlay was dragged to this position (physical pixels).
    Moved { x: i32, y: i32 },
    /// The unlocked overlay's centre button was clicked.
    CenterClicked,
}

/// What the app tells the platform thread.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Command {
    /// Show this bitmap with its top-left at (x, y), physical pixels, topmost,
    /// click-through when `unlocked` is false. When `unlocked` is true the
    /// window takes the mouse: dragging moves it (reported as `Moved`), a click
    /// inside `button` (x, y, w, h relative to the bitmap) reports `CenterClicked`.
    Show { bitmap: Bitmap, x: i32, y: i32, unlocked: bool, button: Option<(i32, i32, i32, i32)> },
    /// Hide the overlay without destroying it.
    Hide,
    /// Put the shown overlay back above every other window; sent when the
    /// window in front changes, since a newly active window may go above it.
    Raise,
    /// Keep the overlay out of screenshots and recordings where the OS allows
    /// it (Windows 10 2004+). Off unless the player turns it on.
    ExcludeFromCapture(bool),
    /// Mouse buttons and keys to report; nothing is listened to otherwise.
    ListenTo { mouse: Vec<MouseButton>, keys: Vec<String> },
    Quit,
}

/// A handle to the running platform thread.
pub struct Platform {
    pub commands: Sender<Command>,
    /// Wakes the platform thread after a command was sent.
    pub wake: Box<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>,
    /// Top-level windows a game could be, for "add a running game". Reads only
    /// window-manager data and the system's process list.
    pub list_windows: Box<dyn Fn() -> Vec<WindowInfo> + Send + Sync>,
}

impl Platform {
    pub fn send(&self, command: Command) {
        if self.commands.send(command).is_ok() {
            (self.wake)();
        }
    }
}

/// Why the platform could not start (for example Wayland without X11).
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct Unsupported(pub String);

```

```rs
//! Other systems (development on macOS): nothing is shown or listened to, so
//! the core and the settings window can still be built and tested.

use super::{Command, Event, Platform, Unsupported};
use std::sync::mpsc::{channel, Sender};

pub fn start(_events: Sender<Event>) -> Result<Platform, Unsupported> {
    let (commands, _receiver) = channel::<Command>();
    std::mem::forget(_receiver);
    Ok(Platform { commands, wake: Box::new(|| {}), list_windows: Box::new(Vec::new) })
}
```

`src-tauri/src/platform/windows/input.rs`:

```rs
//! Mouse buttons and keys from Raw Input.
//!
//! Raw Input is the documented way for a process to read devices itself; games
//! and voice chat use it. Nothing is installed in any other process, unlike
//! the low-level hooks (`WH_KEYBOARD_LL`, `WH_MOUSE_LL`) anti-cheats look for,
//! which are banned here. `RIDEV_INPUTSINK` is what delivers the input while
//! the game, not Scorpio, has the focus. A device class is registered only
//! while an action needs it, so nothing is read otherwise.
//!
//! One process has one Raw Input target per device class. Tao (Tauri's window
//! library) registers its own when its event loop is created, so `ListenTo`
//! must be sent after that; Scorpio's registration then replaces Tao's.

use crate::platform::{Event, MouseButton};
use windows::Win32::Foundation::{HWND, LPARAM};
use windows::Win32::UI::Input::{
    GetRawInputData, RegisterRawInputDevices, HRAWINPUT, RAWINPUT, RAWINPUTDEVICE, RAWINPUTHEADER, RIDEV_INPUTSINK,
    RIDEV_REMOVE, RID_INPUT, RIM_TYPEKEYBOARD, RIM_TYPEMOUSE,
};

const USAGE_PAGE_GENERIC: u16 = 1;
const USAGE_MOUSE: u16 = 2;
const USAGE_KEYBOARD: u16 = 6;
const KEY_UP: u16 = 1; // RI_KEY_BREAK
const KEY_E0: u16 = 2; // RI_KEY_E0: the right-hand or navigation twin of a key

/// (button, RI_MOUSE_*_DOWN flag, RI_MOUSE_*_UP flag, name as a key)
const BUTTONS: [(MouseButton, u16, u16, &str); 5] = [
    (MouseButton::Left, 0x001, 0x002, "Mouse1"),
    (MouseButton::Right, 0x004, 0x008, "Mouse2"),
    (MouseButton::Middle, 0x010, 0x020, "Mouse3"),
    (MouseButton::Back, 0x040, 0x080, "Mouse4"),
    (MouseButton::Forward, 0x100, 0x200, "Mouse5"),
];

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Listener {
    mouse: Vec<MouseButton>,
    keys: Vec<String>,
    /// Physical keys now down, with their names: tells a repeat from a press,
    /// and keeps "Shift" down until both Shift keys are up.
    held: Vec<(u32, String)>,
    mouse_registered: bool,
    keyboard_registered: bool,
}

impl Listener {
    /// Replaces what is listened to; `sink` is the hidden window that receives WM_INPUT.
    pub fn listen(&mut self, sink: HWND, mouse: Vec<MouseButton>, keys: Vec<String>) {
        self.mouse = mouse;
        self.keys = keys;
        self.held.clear();
        // A keybind may be a mouse button ("Mouse4"); that needs the mouse too.
        let want_mouse = !self.mouse.is_empty() || BUTTONS.iter().any(|button| self.keys.iter().any(|key| key == button.3));
        let want_keyboard = self.keys.iter().any(|key| !key.starts_with("Mouse"));
        register(sink, USAGE_MOUSE, want_mouse, &mut self.mouse_registered);
        register(sink, USAGE_KEYBOARD, want_keyboard, &mut self.keyboard_registered);
    }

    /// Reads one WM_INPUT message and reports what was asked for.
    pub fn read(&mut self, input: LPARAM, mut report: impl FnMut(Event)) {
        let mut raw = RAWINPUT::default();
        let mut size = size_of::<RAWINPUT>() as u32;
        // SAFETY: `raw` is a writable RAWINPUT of `size` bytes; mouse and keyboard
        // packets always fit, and anything larger fails here and is dropped.
        let read = unsafe {
            GetRawInputData(
                HRAWINPUT(input.0 as _),
                RID_INPUT,
                Some((&mut raw as *mut RAWINPUT).cast()),
                &mut size,
                size_of::<RAWINPUTHEADER>() as u32,
            )
        };
        if read == u32::MAX {
            return;
        }
        if raw.header.dwType == RIM_TYPEMOUSE.0 {
            // SAFETY: the header says this packet is the mouse member of the union.
            let flags = unsafe { raw.data.mouse.Anonymous.Anonymous.usButtonFlags };
            for (button, down_flag, up_flag, name) in BUTTONS {
                if flags & (down_flag | up_flag) == 0 {
                    continue;
                }
                let down = flags & down_flag != 0;
                if self.mouse.contains(&button) {
                    report(Event::Mouse { button, down });
                }
                if self.keys.iter().any(|key| key == name) {
                    report(Event::Key { code: name.to_string(), down });
                }
            }
        } else if raw.header.dwType == RIM_TYPEKEYBOARD.0 {
            // SAFETY: the header says this packet is the keyboard member of the union.
            let key = unsafe { raw.data.keyboard };
            let Some(name) = key_name(key.VKey) else { return };
            if !self.keys.contains(&name) {
                return;
            }
            let physical = u32::from(key.MakeCode) | u32::from(key.Flags & KEY_E0) << 16 | u32::from(key.VKey) << 20;
            let was_down = self.held.iter().any(|(_, held)| *held == name);
            if key.Flags & KEY_UP == 0 {
                if self.held.iter().any(|(id, _)| *id == physical) {
                    return; // auto-repeat
                }
                self.held.push((physical, name.clone()));
                if !was_down {
                    report(Event::Key { code: name, down: true });
                }
            } else {
                self.held.retain(|(id, _)| *id != physical);
                if was_down && !self.held.iter().any(|(_, held)| *held == name) {
                    report(Event::Key { code: name, down: false });
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn register(sink: HWND, usage: u16, wanted: bool, registered: &mut bool) {
    if wanted == *registered {
        return;
    }
    let device = RAWINPUTDEVICE {
        usUsagePage: USAGE_PAGE_GENERIC,
        usUsage: usage,
        // RIDEV_REMOVE requires a null target window.
        dwFlags: if wanted { RIDEV_INPUTSINK } else { RIDEV_REMOVE },
        hwndTarget: if wanted { sink } else { HWND::default() },
    };
    // SAFETY: one valid RAWINPUTDEVICE and its true size.
    match unsafe { RegisterRawInputDevices(&[device], size_of::<RAWINPUTDEVICE>() as u32) } {
        Ok(()) => *registered = wanted,
        Err(error) => log::warn!("raw input registration (usage {usage}, on {wanted}) failed: {error}"),
    }
}

/// The platform-neutral name of a virtual key, as keybinds store it. Left and
/// right Shift, Control and Alt share one name. Punctuation is named by its
/// US-layout character.
pub fn key_name(vkey: u16) -> Option<String> {
    let fixed = match vkey {
        0x41..=0x5A | 0x30..=0x39 => return Some(char::from(vkey as u8).to_string()),
        0x70..=0x87 => return Some(format!("F{}", vkey - 0x6F)),
        0x60..=0x69 => return Some(format!("Numpad{}", vkey - 0x60)),
        0x10 | 0xA0 | 0xA1 => "Shift",
        0x11 | 0xA2 | 0xA3 => "Control",
        0x12 | 0xA4 | 0xA5 => "Alt",
        0x5B | 0x5C => "Super",
        0x08 => "Backspace",
        0x09 => "Tab",
        0x0D => "Enter",
        0x13 => "Pause",
        0x14 => "CapsLock",
        0x1B => "Escape",
        0x20 => "Space",
        0x21 => "PageUp",
        0x22 => "PageDown",
        0x23 => "End",
        0x24 => "Home",
        0x25 => "Left",
        0x26 => "Up",
        0x27 => "Right",
        0x28 => "Down",
        0x2C => "PrintScreen",
        0x2D => "Insert",
        0x2E => "Delete",
        0x6A => "NumpadMultiply",
        0x6B => "NumpadAdd",
        0x6D => "NumpadSubtract",
        0x6E => "NumpadDecimal",
        0x6F => "NumpadDivide",
        0x90 => "NumLock",
        0x91 => "ScrollLock",
        0xBA => ";",
        0xBB => "=",
        0xBC => ",",
        0xBD => "-",
        0xBE => ".",
        0xBF => "/",
        0xC0 => "`",
        0xDB => "[",
        0xDC => "\\",
        0xDD => "]",
        0xDE => "'",
        _ => return None, // includes 0xFF, the filler in multi-part scan codes
    };
    Some(fixed.to_string())
}

```

`src-tauri/src/platform/windows/mod.rs`:

```rs
//! Windows: one thread owns the overlay window, the foreground event hook and
//! Raw Input, and sleeps in `GetMessageW` until the system or the app has
//! something for it. Nothing polls.
//!
//! Anti-cheat rules (docs/plans/scorpio.md) as they apply here:
//! - no process is ever opened: the executable path comes from the kernel's
//!   process list by pid (`process.rs`), everything else from the window manager;
//! - no hooks that load into or intercept other processes: the foreground hook
//!   is `WINEVENT_OUTOFCONTEXT` (a notification posted to this thread's queue),
//!   input is Raw Input on our own hidden window (`input.rs`);
//! - no input is ever sent, nothing is injected, nothing is drawn into a game.

mod input;
mod overlay;
mod process;
mod window;

use super::{Command, Event, Platform, Unsupported};
use std::cell::{OnceCell, RefCell};
use std::sync::mpsc::{channel, Receiver, Sender, TryRecvError};
use windows::core::{w, PCWSTR};
use windows::Win32::Foundation::{HWND, LPARAM, LRESULT, WPARAM};
use windows::Win32::System::LibraryLoader::GetModuleHandleW;
use windows::Win32::UI::Accessibility::{SetWinEventHook, UnhookWinEvent, HWINEVENTHOOK};
use windows::Win32::UI::HiDpi::{SetThreadDpiAwarenessContext, DPI_AWARENESS_CONTEXT_PER_MONITOR_AWARE_V2};
use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
    CreateWindowExW, DefWindowProcW, DestroyWindow, DispatchMessageW, GetForegroundWindow, GetMessageW, LoadCursorW,
    PostMessageW, PostQuitMessage, RegisterClassW, EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND, HWND_MESSAGE, IDC_ARROW, MSG,
    WINDOW_EX_STYLE, WINDOW_STYLE, WINEVENT_OUTOFCONTEXT, WM_APP, WM_INPUT, WNDCLASSW, WNDPROC,
};

/// Posted to the hidden window by `Platform::wake`: commands are waiting.
const WM_WAKE: u32 = WM_APP + 1;

struct State {
    events: Sender<Event>,
    commands: Receiver<Command>,
    /// Hidden message-only window: the target of wake-ups and of Raw Input.
    sink: HWND,
    listener: RefCell<input::Listener>,
}

thread_local! {
    static STATE: OnceCell<State> = const { OnceCell::new() };
}

pub fn start(events: Sender<Event>) -> Result<Platform, Unsupported> {
    let (commands, receiver) = channel::<Command>();
    let (ready, started) = channel::<Result<isize, String>>();
    std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("scorpio-platform".into())
        .spawn(move || run(events, receiver, ready))
        .map_err(|error| Unsupported(format!("the platform thread could not start: {error}")))?;
    let sink = started
        .recv()
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| Err("the platform thread stopped while starting".into()))
        .map_err(Unsupported)?;
    Ok(Platform {
        commands,
        wake: Box::new(move || {
            // SAFETY: posting to a window handle is allowed from any thread; if
            // the platform thread has ended the handle is dead and this fails.
            let _ = unsafe { PostMessageW(Some(HWND(sink as *mut _)), WM_WAKE, WPARAM(0), LPARAM(0)) };
        }),
        list_windows: Box::new(window::list),
    })
}

fn run(events: Sender<Event>, commands: Receiver<Command>, ready: Sender<Result<isize, String>>) {
    // SAFETY: affects this thread only. Per-monitor v2 makes every coordinate
    // here a physical pixel, whatever the rest of the process chose.
    unsafe { SetThreadDpiAwarenessContext(DPI_AWARENESS_CONTEXT_PER_MONITOR_AWARE_V2) };

    let created = create_window(w!("ScorpioEvents"), sink_procedure, WINDOW_EX_STYLE(0), WINDOW_STYLE(0), Some(HWND_MESSAGE))
        .and_then(|sink| overlay::create(events.clone()).map(|()| sink));
    let sink = match created {
        Ok(sink) => sink,
        Err(error) => {
            let _ = ready.send(Err(error));
            return;
        }
    };
    let state = State { events, commands, sink, listener: RefCell::default() };
    STATE.with(|cell| cell.set(state).ok());

    // Out of context: the system posts the event to this thread's queue. No
    // module is loaded into the game or any other process, unlike an
    // in-context hook or SetWindowsHookEx. Own-process windows are not
    // skipped: the app needs to know when its settings window is in front.
    // SAFETY: the callback is a plain function that lives as long as the program.
    let hook = unsafe {
        SetWinEventHook(EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND, EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND, None, Some(foreground_changed), 0, 0, WINEVENT_OUTOFCONTEXT)
    };
    if hook.is_invalid() {
        log::warn!("the foreground event hook could not be set; focus changes will not be seen");
    }
    let _ = ready.send(Ok(sink.0 as isize));

    // SAFETY: no arguments; reads which window is in front right now.
    report_foreground(unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() });

    let mut message = MSG::default();
    // SAFETY: `message` is a valid out-structure; the loop ends on WM_QUIT (0) or an error (-1).
    while unsafe { GetMessageW(&mut message, None, 0, 0) }.0 > 0 {
        // SAFETY: `message` was just filled in by GetMessageW.
        unsafe { DispatchMessageW(&message) };
    }

    STATE.with(|state| state.get().map(|state| state.listener.borrow_mut().listen(sink, Vec::new(), Vec::new())));
    overlay::destroy();
    // SAFETY: the hook and the window belong to this thread and are not used again.
    unsafe {
        if !hook.is_invalid() {
            let _ = UnhookWinEvent(hook);
        }
        let _ = DestroyWindow(sink);
    }
}

/// Registers a window class (once per process) and creates one window of it on this thread.
fn create_window(
    class: PCWSTR,
    procedure: unsafe extern "system" fn(HWND, u32, WPARAM, LPARAM) -> LRESULT,
    ex_style: WINDOW_EX_STYLE,
    style: WINDOW_STYLE,
    parent: Option<HWND>,
) -> Result<HWND, String> {
    let procedure: WNDPROC = Some(procedure);
    // SAFETY: `class` is a static NUL-terminated string and the class structure
    // is valid for the call; registering twice fails harmlessly.
    unsafe {
        let instance = GetModuleHandleW(None).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?.into();
        let cursor = LoadCursorW(None, IDC_ARROW).unwrap_or_default();
        RegisterClassW(&WNDCLASSW { lpfnWndProc: procedure, hInstance: instance, hCursor: cursor, lpszClassName: class, ..Default::default() });
        CreateWindowExW(ex_style, class, w!("Scorpio overlay"), style, 0, 0, 0, 0, parent, None, Some(instance), None)
            .map_err(|error| format!("a window could not be created: {error}"))
    }
}

fn report_foreground(hwnd: HWND) {
    if let Some(event) = window::foreground_event(hwnd) {
        STATE.with(|state| {
            if let Some(state) = state.get() {
                let _ = state.events.send(event);
            }
        });
    }
}

unsafe extern "system" fn foreground_changed(
    _hook: HWINEVENTHOOK,
    _event: u32,
    hwnd: HWND,
    object: i32,
    child: i32,
    _thread: u32,
    _time: u32,
) {
    // Only the window itself (OBJID_WINDOW, CHILDID_SELF), not a part of it.
    if object == 0 && child == 0 {
        report_foreground(hwnd);
    }
}

unsafe extern "system" fn sink_procedure(hwnd: HWND, message: u32, wparam: WPARAM, lparam: LPARAM) -> LRESULT {
    STATE.with(|state| {
        let Some(state) = state.get() else { return };
        match message {
            WM_WAKE => drain_commands(state),
            WM_INPUT => state.listener.borrow_mut().read(lparam, |event| {
                let _ = state.events.send(event);
            }),
            _ => {}
        }
    });
    // WM_INPUT must reach the default procedure too, which frees the packet.
    // SAFETY: forwards the untouched arguments of this window procedure.
    unsafe { DefWindowProcW(hwnd, message, wparam, lparam) }
}

fn drain_commands(state: &State) {
    loop {
        match state.commands.try_recv() {
            Ok(Command::Show { bitmap, x, y, unlocked, button }) => overlay::show(&bitmap, x, y, unlocked, button),
            Ok(Command::Hide) => overlay::hide(),
            Ok(Command::Raise) => overlay::raise(),
            Ok(Command::ExcludeFromCapture(exclude)) => overlay::exclude_from_capture(exclude),
            Ok(Command::ListenTo { mouse, keys }) => state.listener.borrow_mut().listen(state.sink, mouse, keys),
            // SAFETY: no pointers; ends this thread's message loop.
            Ok(Command::Quit) | Err(TryRecvError::Disconnected) => return unsafe { PostQuitMessage(0) },
            Err(TryRecvError::Empty) => return,
        }
    }
}

```

`src-tauri/src/platform/windows/overlay.rs`:

```rs
//! The crosshair window: a separate layered window above the game, the same
//! kind Discord's and Steam's overlays-as-windows use. Nothing is ever drawn
//! into the game or its swap chain. It never takes the focus (`NOACTIVATE`),
//! so the game stays the foreground window, and while locked it is
//! click-through (`TRANSPARENT`), so every click reaches the game.

use super::create_window;
use crate::core::raster::Bitmap;
use crate::platform::Event;
use std::cell::{Cell, OnceCell};
use std::sync::mpsc::Sender;
use windows::core::w;
use windows::Win32::Foundation::{COLORREF, HWND, LPARAM, LRESULT, POINT, RECT, SIZE, WPARAM};
use windows::Win32::Graphics::Gdi::{
    CreateCompatibleDC, CreateDIBSection, DeleteDC, DeleteObject, SelectObject, AC_SRC_ALPHA, AC_SRC_OVER, BITMAPINFO,
    BITMAPINFOHEADER, BLENDFUNCTION, DIB_RGB_COLORS,
};
use windows::Win32::UI::Input::KeyboardAndMouse::{ReleaseCapture, SetCapture};
use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
    DefWindowProcW, DestroyWindow, GetCursorPos, GetWindowLongPtrW, GetWindowRect, IsWindowVisible, SetWindowDisplayAffinity,
    SetWindowLongPtrW, SetWindowPos, ShowWindow, UpdateLayeredWindow, GWL_EXSTYLE, HWND_TOPMOST, MA_NOACTIVATE,
    SWP_NOACTIVATE, SWP_NOMOVE, SWP_NOSIZE, SWP_NOZORDER, SWP_SHOWWINDOW, SW_HIDE, ULW_ALPHA, WDA_EXCLUDEFROMCAPTURE, WDA_NONE,
    GetForegroundWindow, WM_DISPLAYCHANGE, WM_CAPTURECHANGED, WM_LBUTTONDOWN, WM_LBUTTONUP, WM_MOUSEACTIVATE, WM_MOUSEMOVE, WS_EX_LAYERED,
    WS_EX_NOACTIVATE, WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW, WS_EX_TOPMOST, WS_EX_TRANSPARENT, WS_POPUP,
};

struct Overlay {
    hwnd: HWND,
    events: Sender<Event>,
    unlocked: Cell<bool>,
    /// The centre button, (x, y, w, h) inside the window; only while unlocked.
    button: Cell<Option<(i32, i32, i32, i32)>>,
    drag: Cell<Option<Drag>>,
}

/// What the left button is doing between press and release.
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
enum Drag {
    /// Pressed on the centre button: a click if released there too.
    Button,
    /// Moving the window: where the cursor holds it, and where it started.
    Window { grip: (i32, i32), from: (i32, i32) },
}

// The window procedure is re-entered while this thread is inside calls such as
// SetWindowPos, so the state is in `Cell`s and no borrow is ever held.
thread_local! {
    static OVERLAY: OnceCell<Overlay> = const { OnceCell::new() };
}

fn with(action: impl FnOnce(&Overlay)) {
    OVERLAY.with(|overlay| overlay.get().map(action));
}

/// Creates the hidden overlay window on this thread.
pub fn create(events: Sender<Event>) -> Result<(), String> {
    let style = WS_EX_LAYERED | WS_EX_TOPMOST | WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW | WS_EX_NOACTIVATE | WS_EX_TRANSPARENT;
    let hwnd = create_window(w!("ScorpioOverlay"), procedure, style, WS_POPUP, None)?;
    let overlay = Overlay { hwnd, events, unlocked: Cell::new(false), button: Cell::new(None), drag: Cell::new(None) };
    OVERLAY.with(|cell| cell.set(overlay).map_err(|_| "the overlay already exists on this thread".to_string()))
}

pub fn show(bitmap: &Bitmap, x: i32, y: i32, unlocked: bool, button: Option<(i32, i32, i32, i32)>) {
    with(|overlay| {
        let expected = bitmap.width as usize * bitmap.height as usize * 4;
        if expected == 0 || bitmap.data.len() != expected {
            log::warn!("overlay bitmap {}x{} with {} bytes was not shown", bitmap.width, bitmap.height, bitmap.data.len());
            return;
        }
        overlay.set_unlocked(unlocked);
        overlay.button.set(button.filter(|_| unlocked));
        // SAFETY: `hwnd` is this thread's window; `paint` documents its own pointers.
        unsafe {
            if let Err(error) = paint(overlay.hwnd, bitmap, x, y) {
                log::warn!("overlay could not be painted: {error}");
            }
            // Again on every show: other topmost windows may have gone above since.
            let flags = SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOSIZE | SWP_NOACTIVATE | SWP_SHOWWINDOW;
            if let Err(error) = SetWindowPos(overlay.hwnd, Some(HWND_TOPMOST), 0, 0, 0, 0, flags) {
                log::warn!("overlay could not be shown: {error}");
            }
        }
    });
}

pub fn hide() {
    with(|overlay| {
        overlay.stop_drag();
        // SAFETY: `hwnd` is this thread's window.
        let _ = unsafe { ShowWindow(overlay.hwnd, SW_HIDE) };
    });
}

/// The owner's exception to the anti-cheat rules (docs/plans/scorpio.md).
pub fn exclude_from_capture(exclude: bool) {
    with(|overlay| {
        let affinity = if exclude { WDA_EXCLUDEFROMCAPTURE } else { WDA_NONE };
        // SAFETY: `hwnd` is this thread's window.
        if let Err(error) = unsafe { SetWindowDisplayAffinity(overlay.hwnd, affinity) } {
            log::warn!("capture exclusion ({exclude}) failed: {error}");
        }
    });
}

pub fn raise() {
    with(|overlay| {
        // SAFETY: `hwnd` is this thread's window.
        unsafe {
            if !IsWindowVisible(overlay.hwnd).as_bool() {
                return;
            }
            let flags = SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOSIZE | SWP_NOACTIVATE;
            if let Err(error) = SetWindowPos(overlay.hwnd, Some(HWND_TOPMOST), 0, 0, 0, 0, flags) {
                log::warn!("overlay could not be raised: {error}");
            }
        }
    });
}

pub fn destroy() {
    with(|overlay| {
        overlay.stop_drag();
        // SAFETY: `hwnd` is this thread's window and is not used afterwards (the thread is ending).
        if let Err(error) = unsafe { DestroyWindow(overlay.hwnd) } {
            log::warn!("overlay could not be destroyed: {error}");
        }
    });
}

/// Hands the premultiplied BGRA pixels to the window manager, which composes
/// them with per-pixel alpha, and places the window at (x, y).
///
/// SAFETY (caller): `hwnd` is a layered window of this thread and `bitmap.data`
/// holds exactly `width * height * 4` bytes.
unsafe fn paint(hwnd: HWND, bitmap: &Bitmap, x: i32, y: i32) -> windows::core::Result<()> {
    let (width, height) = (bitmap.width as i32, bitmap.height as i32);
    let header = BITMAPINFOHEADER {
        biSize: size_of::<BITMAPINFOHEADER>() as u32,
        biWidth: width,
        biHeight: -height, // negative: top row first, as `Bitmap` is
        biPlanes: 1,
        biBitCount: 32,
        ..Default::default()
    };
    let info = BITMAPINFO { bmiHeader: header, ..Default::default() };
    let dc = CreateCompatibleDC(None);
    let mut pixels = std::ptr::null_mut();
    let result = CreateDIBSection(Some(dc), &info, DIB_RGB_COLORS, &mut pixels, None, 0).and_then(|dib| {
        // The section is `width * height * 4` bytes, the length checked by the caller.
        std::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(bitmap.data.as_ptr(), pixels.cast::<u8>(), bitmap.data.len());
        let previous = SelectObject(dc, dib.into());
        let blend = BLENDFUNCTION {
            BlendOp: AC_SRC_OVER as u8,
            BlendFlags: 0,
            SourceConstantAlpha: 255,
            AlphaFormat: AC_SRC_ALPHA as u8,
        };
        let updated = UpdateLayeredWindow(
            hwnd,
            None,
            Some(&POINT { x, y }),
            Some(&SIZE { cx: width, cy: height }),
            Some(dc),
            Some(&POINT::default()),
            COLORREF(0),
            Some(&blend),
            ULW_ALPHA,
        );
        // The window manager keeps its own copy, so nothing stays allocated here.
        SelectObject(dc, previous);
        let _ = DeleteObject(dib.into());
        updated
    });
    let _ = DeleteDC(dc);
    result
}

impl Overlay {
    fn set_unlocked(&self, unlocked: bool) {
        if !unlocked {
            self.stop_drag();
        }
        self.unlocked.set(unlocked);
        // SAFETY: `hwnd` is this thread's window; only its own style bits change.
        unsafe {
            let current = GetWindowLongPtrW(self.hwnd, GWL_EXSTYLE);
            let click_through = WS_EX_TRANSPARENT.0 as isize;
            let wanted = if unlocked { current & !click_through } else { current | click_through };
            if wanted != current {
                SetWindowLongPtrW(self.hwnd, GWL_EXSTYLE, wanted);
            }
        }
    }

    /// Ends a drag in progress; WM_CAPTURECHANGED then reports where it ended.
    fn stop_drag(&self) {
        if self.drag.get().is_some() {
            // SAFETY: no arguments; releases this thread's own mouse capture.
            let _ = unsafe { ReleaseCapture() };
        }
    }

    fn origin(&self) -> (i32, i32) {
        let mut rect = RECT::default();
        // SAFETY: `rect` is a valid out-structure.
        let _ = unsafe { GetWindowRect(self.hwnd, &mut rect) };
        (rect.left, rect.top)
    }

    fn on_button(&self, (x, y): (i32, i32)) -> bool {
        self.button.get().is_some_and(|(left, top, width, height)| {
            x >= left && x < left + width && y >= top && y < top + height
        })
    }

    /// Handles a message of the overlay window; None leaves it to the default.
    fn message(&self, message: u32, lparam: LPARAM) -> Option<LRESULT> {
        // Client coordinates of mouse messages: signed 16-bit halves of lparam.
        let point = ((lparam.0 & 0xFFFF) as i16 as i32, ((lparam.0 >> 16) & 0xFFFF) as i16 as i32);
        match message {
            // A click on the unlocked overlay must not pull the focus from the game.
            WM_MOUSEACTIVATE => return Some(LRESULT(MA_NOACTIVATE as isize)),
            WM_LBUTTONDOWN if self.unlocked.get() => {
                let drag = if self.on_button(point) {
                    Drag::Button
                } else {
                    let mut cursor = POINT::default();
                    // SAFETY: `cursor` is a valid out-structure.
                    let _ = unsafe { GetCursorPos(&mut cursor) };
                    let from = self.origin();
                    Drag::Window { grip: (cursor.x - from.0, cursor.y - from.1), from }
                };
                // SAFETY: `hwnd` is this thread's window. Capture keeps the
                // moves coming when the cursor outruns the window.
                unsafe { SetCapture(self.hwnd) };
                self.drag.set(Some(drag));
            }
            WM_MOUSEMOVE => {
                if let Some(Drag::Window { grip, .. }) = self.drag.get() {
                    let mut cursor = POINT::default();
                    let flags = SWP_NOSIZE | SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE;
                    // SAFETY: `cursor` is a valid out-structure and `hwnd` is this thread's window.
                    unsafe {
                        if GetCursorPos(&mut cursor).is_ok() {
                            let _ = SetWindowPos(self.hwnd, None, cursor.x - grip.0, cursor.y - grip.1, 0, 0, flags);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            WM_LBUTTONUP => {
                if matches!(self.drag.get(), Some(Drag::Button)) && self.on_button(point) {
                    let _ = self.events.send(Event::CenterClicked);
                }
                self.stop_drag();
            }
            // Sent on release and when anything else ends the drag (locking, hiding, Alt+Tab).
            WM_CAPTURECHANGED => {
                if let Some(Drag::Window { from, .. }) = self.drag.take() {
                    let (x, y) = self.origin();
                    if (x, y) != from {
                        let _ = self.events.send(Event::Moved { x, y });
                    }
                }
            }
            _ => return None,
        }
        Some(LRESULT(0))
    }
}

unsafe extern "system" fn procedure(hwnd: HWND, message: u32, wparam: WPARAM, lparam: LPARAM) -> LRESULT {
    // A resolution change (a game going exclusive fullscreen) moves the
    // centre: describe the window in front again. Top-level windows get it.
    if message == WM_DISPLAYCHANGE {
        // SAFETY: no arguments; reads which window is in front right now.
        super::report_foreground(unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() });
    }
    let handled = OVERLAY.with(|overlay| overlay.get().and_then(|overlay| overlay.message(message, lparam)));
    // SAFETY: forwards the untouched arguments of this window procedure.
    handled.unwrap_or_else(|| unsafe { DefWindowProcW(hwnd, message, wparam, lparam) })
}
```

`src-tauri/src/platform/windows/process.rs`:

```rs
//! A process's executable path from its pid, without ever opening the process.
//!
//! The usual route (`OpenProcess` + `QueryFullProcessImageNameW`) takes a handle
//! to the game, which is exactly what anti-cheats watch for, so it is banned
//! here. `NtQuerySystemInformation(SystemProcessIdInformation)` asks the kernel
//! for the image name of a pid instead: no handle to the process ever exists.

use std::ffi::c_void;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use windows::core::PCWSTR;
use windows::Win32::Storage::FileSystem::{GetLogicalDrives, QueryDosDeviceW};

const SYSTEM_PROCESS_ID_INFORMATION: u32 = 0x58;
const STATUS_INFO_LENGTH_MISMATCH: i32 = 0xC000_0004_u32 as i32;

#[repr(C)]
struct UnicodeString {
    length: u16,         // bytes in use
    maximum_length: u16, // bytes available; the kernel writes the needed size here
    buffer: *mut u16,
}

#[repr(C)]
struct ProcessIdInformation {
    process_id: *mut c_void, // a HANDLE-sized pid, not a handle
    image_name: UnicodeString,
}

#[link(name = "ntdll")]
extern "system" {
    fn NtQuerySystemInformation(class: u32, info: *mut c_void, length: u32, returned: *mut u32) -> i32;
}

/// `(name, path)` of a process: the DOS path of its executable and that path's
/// file stem. Either may be empty when the system won't say; that is never
/// worked around by opening the process.
pub fn image(pid: u32) -> (String, String) {
    let Some(nt_path) = nt_image_path(pid) else {
        return (String::new(), String::new());
    };
    let name = nt_path.rsplit('\\').next().unwrap_or("");
    let name = name.rsplit_once('.').map_or(name, |(stem, _)| stem).to_string();
    (name, dos_path(&nt_path).unwrap_or_default())
}

/// The kernel's name for the executable, like `\Device\HarddiskVolume3\Games\cs2.exe`.
fn nt_image_path(pid: u32) -> Option<String> {
    let mut buffer = vec![0u16; 512];
    for _ in 0..2 {
        let mut info = ProcessIdInformation {
            process_id: pid as usize as *mut c_void,
            image_name: UnicodeString {
                length: 0,
                maximum_length: (buffer.len() * 2) as u16,
                buffer: buffer.as_mut_ptr(),
            },
        };
        // SAFETY: `info` is the structure this class expects and its string
        // points at `buffer`, which is `maximum_length` bytes and outlives the call.
        let status = unsafe {
            NtQuerySystemInformation(
                SYSTEM_PROCESS_ID_INFORMATION,
                (&mut info as *mut ProcessIdInformation).cast(),
                size_of::<ProcessIdInformation>() as u32,
                std::ptr::null_mut(),
            )
        };
        if status == STATUS_INFO_LENGTH_MISMATCH {
            let needed = usize::from(info.image_name.maximum_length) / 2;
            if needed <= buffer.len() {
                return None;
            }
            buffer = vec![0u16; needed];
            continue;
        }
        let length = usize::from(info.image_name.length) / 2;
        if status < 0 || length == 0 || length > buffer.len() {
            return None;
        }
        return Some(String::from_utf16_lossy(&buffer[..length]));
    }
    None
}

/// Device name -> drive, like `\Device\HarddiskVolume3` -> `C:`. Filled on
/// first use and again when a path names a device not seen yet (a new drive).
static DRIVES: Mutex<Vec<(String, String)>> = Mutex::new(Vec::new());

fn dos_path(nt_path: &str) -> Option<String> {
    const NETWORK: &str = "\\Device\\Mup\\";
    if let Some(share) = nt_path.strip_prefix(NETWORK) {
        return Some(format!("\\\\{share}"));
    }
    let mut drives = DRIVES.lock().unwrap_or_else(|poisoned| poisoned.into_inner());
    if let Some(path) = with_drive(&drives, nt_path) {
        return Some(path);
    }
    *drives = read_drives();
    with_drive(&drives, nt_path)
}

fn with_drive(drives: &[(String, String)], nt_path: &str) -> Option<String> {
    drives.iter().find_map(|(device, drive)| {
        // The `\` after the device keeps HarddiskVolume1 from matching HarddiskVolume10.
        let rest = nt_path.strip_prefix(device.as_str())?;
        rest.starts_with('\\').then(|| format!("{drive}{rest}"))
    })
}

fn read_drives() -> Vec<(String, String)> {
    // SAFETY: no arguments; returns a bit per existing drive letter.
    let mask = unsafe { GetLogicalDrives() };
    let mut drives = Vec::new();
    for letter in (0..26u8).filter(|bit| mask & (1 << bit) != 0).map(|bit| (b'A' + bit) as char) {
        let drive = format!("{letter}:");
        let wide: Vec<u16> = drive.encode_utf16().chain([0]).collect();
        let mut device = [0u16; 512];
        // SAFETY: `wide` is NUL-terminated and `device` is a writable buffer of the given length.
        let written = unsafe { QueryDosDeviceW(PCWSTR(wide.as_ptr()), Some(&mut device)) } as usize;
        if written == 0 {
            continue;
        }
        // The answer is a list of NUL-separated names; the first is the current mapping.
        let end = device.iter().position(|&unit| unit == 0).unwrap_or(written);
        drives.push((String::from_utf16_lossy(&device[..end]), drive));
    }
    drives
}

```

`src-tauri/src/platform/windows/window.rs`:

```rs
//! What the window manager says about a top-level window. Everything here is
//! window-manager data (`user32`/`dwmapi`); the owning process is never opened.

use super::process;
use crate::core::focus::WindowInfo;
use crate::platform::Event;
use std::ffi::c_void;
use windows::core::BOOL;
use windows::Win32::Foundation::{HWND, LPARAM, RECT};
use windows::Win32::Graphics::Dwm::{DwmGetWindowAttribute, DWMWA_CLOAKED};
use windows::Win32::Graphics::Gdi::{GetMonitorInfoW, MonitorFromWindow, MONITORINFO, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST};
use windows::Win32::UI::HiDpi::{SetThreadDpiAwarenessContext, DPI_AWARENESS_CONTEXT_PER_MONITOR_AWARE_V2};
use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
    EnumWindows, GetAncestor, GetClassNameW, GetWindow, GetWindowLongPtrW, GetWindowRect, GetWindowTextW,
    GetWindowThreadProcessId, IsWindowVisible, IsZoomed, GA_ROOT, GWL_STYLE, GW_OWNER, WS_CAPTION,
};

/// The event for a window that came to the front, or None when the event is
/// not about a real top-level window and should be ignored.
pub fn foreground_event(hwnd: HWND) -> Option<Event> {
    if hwnd.is_invalid() {
        return Some(Event::Foreground(None));
    }
    // SAFETY: both calls only read window-manager state and accept stale handles.
    let top_level = unsafe { IsWindowVisible(hwnd).as_bool() && GetAncestor(hwnd, GA_ROOT) == hwnd };
    if !top_level {
        return None;
    }
    let info = describe(hwnd);
    Some(Event::Foreground((!is_desktop(&info)).then_some(info)))
}

/// The shell's desktop window: in front when nothing else is.
fn is_desktop(info: &WindowInfo) -> bool {
    matches!(info.window_class.as_str(), "Progman" | "WorkerW")
}

pub fn describe(hwnd: HWND) -> WindowInfo {
    let mut pid = 0u32;
    let mut title = [0u16; 512];
    let mut class = [0u16; 256];
    // SAFETY: the out-pointer and both buffers are valid for the duration of the calls.
    let (title_length, class_length) = unsafe {
        GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, Some(&mut pid));
        (GetWindowTextW(hwnd, &mut title), GetClassNameW(hwnd, &mut class))
    };
    let (name, path) = process::image(pid);
    let (fullscreen, monitor) = placement(hwnd);
    WindowInfo {
        pid,
        name,
        path,
        title: String::from_utf16_lossy(&title[..title_length.max(0) as usize]),
        window_class: String::from_utf16_lossy(&class[..class_length.max(0) as usize]),
        fullscreen,
        monitor,
    }
}

/// Whether the window is fullscreen, and the monitor it is on. Borderless or
/// exclusive fullscreen: the window covers its whole monitor without being a
/// maximised or captioned window (a maximised browser is not a game).
fn placement(hwnd: HWND) -> (bool, Option<[i32; 4]>) {
    let mut window = RECT::default();
    let mut monitor = MONITORINFO { cbSize: size_of::<MONITORINFO>() as u32, ..Default::default() };
    // SAFETY: the out-structures are valid and `cbSize` is set as GetMonitorInfoW requires.
    let (measured, zoomed, style) = unsafe {
        let measured = GetWindowRect(hwnd, &mut window).is_ok()
            && GetMonitorInfoW(MonitorFromWindow(hwnd, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST), &mut monitor).as_bool();
        (measured, IsZoomed(hwnd).as_bool(), GetWindowLongPtrW(hwnd, GWL_STYLE) as u32)
    };
    let screen = monitor.rcMonitor;
    let covers = window.left <= screen.left
        && window.top <= screen.top
        && window.right >= screen.right
        && window.bottom >= screen.bottom;
    let rect = measured.then_some([screen.left, screen.top, screen.right - screen.left, screen.bottom - screen.top]);
    (measured && covers && !zoomed && style & WS_CAPTION.0 != WS_CAPTION.0, rect)
}

/// Visible, unowned, titled top-level windows of other processes, without the desktop.
pub fn list() -> Vec<WindowInfo> {
    let mut windows: Vec<WindowInfo> = Vec::new();
    // SAFETY: the callback runs only during this call, while `windows` is alive
    // and not otherwise borrowed. The thread's DPI context is switched so the
    // window and monitor rectangles are both in physical pixels, then restored.
    unsafe {
        let previous = SetThreadDpiAwarenessContext(DPI_AWARENESS_CONTEXT_PER_MONITOR_AWARE_V2);
        let _ = EnumWindows(Some(collect), LPARAM(&mut windows as *mut Vec<WindowInfo> as isize));
        if !previous.0.is_null() {
            SetThreadDpiAwarenessContext(previous);
        }
    }
    windows
}

unsafe extern "system" fn collect(hwnd: HWND, list: LPARAM) -> BOOL {
    // SAFETY: `list` is the pointer `list()` passed in, valid for the whole enumeration.
    let windows = unsafe { &mut *(list.0 as *mut Vec<WindowInfo>) };
    let mut cloaked = 0u32;
    // SAFETY: read-only window-manager queries; `cloaked` is a valid 4-byte out-value.
    let candidate = unsafe {
        let _ = DwmGetWindowAttribute(hwnd, DWMWA_CLOAKED, (&mut cloaked as *mut u32).cast::<c_void>(), 4);
        IsWindowVisible(hwnd).as_bool() && GetWindow(hwnd, GW_OWNER).map_or(true, |owner| owner.is_invalid())
    };
    // Cloaked windows are "visible" but not on screen: suspended store apps,
    // windows on another virtual desktop.
    if candidate && cloaked == 0 {
        let info = describe(hwnd);
        if !info.title.is_empty() && info.pid != std::process::id() && !is_desktop(&info) {
            windows.push(info);
        }
    }
    true.into()
}
```
