`src-tauri/src/store.rs`:

```rs
//! The settings file: what the player chose, with defaults for everything and
//! validation on the way in, so the rest of the app trusts these types.

use crate::core::crosshair::Crosshair;
use crate::core::focus::Fingerprint;
use crate::platform::MouseButton;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;

pub const NAME_LIMIT: usize = 48;
pub const SAVED_LIMIT: usize = 100;
pub const GAMES_LIMIT: usize = 200;

/// One of the player's own crosshairs.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default, rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Saved {
    pub name: String,
    pub crosshair: Crosshair,
}

impl Default for Saved {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Saved { name: "Crosshair".into(), crosshair: Crosshair::default() }
    }
}

/// A game the player added. Its own crosshair replaces the main one while it
/// is in front; without one the main crosshair shows.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default, rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Game {
    pub name: String,
    pub fingerprint: Fingerprint,
    pub crosshair: Option<Crosshair>,
}

/// Which windows count as a game without being added.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default, rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Detect {
    /// A fullscreen window (not the desktop or the shell).
    pub fullscreen: bool,
    /// A program inside a game folder that Steam, Epic, GOG and others installed.
    pub launchers: bool,
}

impl Default for Detect {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Detect { fullscreen: true, launchers: true }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub enum AimMode {
    /// Hidden while the button is held.
    Hold,
    /// Each press hides or shows it.
    Toggle,
}

/// Hiding the crosshair while aiming down sights.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default, rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Aim {
    pub hide: bool,
    pub button: MouseButton,
    pub mode: AimMode,
}

impl Default for Aim {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Aim { hide: false, button: MouseButton::Right, mode: AimMode::Hold }
    }
}

/// Global shortcuts as accelerators ("Ctrl+Shift+Alt+X"); empty means none.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default, rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Keys {
    pub lock: String,
    pub center: String,
    pub hide: String,
    pub next: String,
    pub previous: String,
    pub add_game: String,
}

impl Default for Keys {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Keys {
            lock: "Ctrl+Shift+Alt+X".into(),
            center: "Ctrl+Shift+Alt+C".into(),
            hide: "Ctrl+Shift+Alt+H".into(),
            next: "Ctrl+Shift+Alt+Period".into(),
            previous: "Ctrl+Shift+Alt+Comma".into(),
            add_game: "Ctrl+Shift+Alt+G".into(),
        }
    }
}

impl Keys {
    pub fn all(&self) -> [(&'static str, &str); 6] {
        [
            ("lock", &self.lock),
            ("center", &self.center),
            ("hide", &self.hide),
            ("next", &self.next),
            ("previous", &self.previous),
            ("addGame", &self.add_game),
        ]
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default, rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Settings {
    pub crosshair: Crosshair,
    pub saved: Vec<Saved>,
    pub games: Vec<Game>,
    pub detect: Detect,
    pub aim: Aim,
    pub keys: Keys,
    /// Offset of the crosshair's centre from the display's centre, physical
    /// pixels. (0, 0) is exactly centred.
    pub offset: (i32, i32),
    pub start_with_system: bool,
    /// Windows only; off by default (see the plan's anti-cheat table).
    pub hide_from_capture: bool,
    /// Serve the crosshair to OBS as a Browser source (127.0.0.1 only).
    pub obs: bool,
    /// Off by default: updates install by themselves, between games.
    pub update_manually: bool,
}

fn clean_name(name: &str, fallback: &str) -> String {
    let name: String = name.trim().chars().filter(|c| !c.is_control()).take(NAME_LIMIT).collect();
    if name.is_empty() { fallback.to_string() } else { name }
}

impl Settings {
    /// Every value in range, names tidy, lists bounded. Idempotent.
    pub fn validated(mut self) -> Settings {
        self.crosshair = self.crosshair.normalized();
        self.saved.truncate(SAVED_LIMIT);
        for saved in &mut self.saved {
            saved.name = clean_name(&saved.name, "Crosshair");
            saved.crosshair = saved.crosshair.normalized();
        }
        self.games.retain(|game| game.fingerprint != Fingerprint::default());
        self.games.truncate(GAMES_LIMIT);
        for game in &mut self.games {
            let fallback = if game.fingerprint.name.is_empty() { "Game" } else { &game.fingerprint.name };
            game.name = clean_name(&game.name, fallback);
            game.crosshair = game.crosshair.as_ref().map(Crosshair::normalized);
        }
        const LIMIT: i32 = 4000;
        self.offset = (self.offset.0.clamp(-LIMIT, LIMIT), self.offset.1.clamp(-LIMIT, LIMIT));
        self
    }
}

/// Reads the settings; a missing file gives defaults, an unreadable one is
/// kept beside as `.broken` so nothing the player made is lost silently.
pub fn load(path: &Path) -> Settings {
    let text = match fs::read_to_string(path) {
        Ok(text) => text,
        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Settings::default(),
        Err(error) => {
            // Unreadable but present: keep a copy before defaults replace it.
            log::warn!("settings could not be read ({error}); starting from defaults");
            let _ = fs::copy(path, broken_name(path));
            return Settings::default();
        }
    };
    match serde_json::from_str::<Settings>(&text) {
        Ok(settings) => settings.validated(),
        Err(error) => {
            log::warn!("settings unreadable ({error}); starting from defaults");
            let _ = fs::rename(path, broken_name(path));
            Settings::default()
        }
    }
}

/// `settings.broken-<seconds>.json`, so an earlier broken copy is never lost.
fn broken_name(path: &Path) -> std::path::PathBuf {
    let seconds = std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).map_or(0, |d| d.as_secs());
    path.with_extension(format!("broken-{seconds}.json"))
}

/// Writes through a temporary file and a rename, so a crash mid-write never
/// leaves half a file.
pub fn save(path: &Path, settings: &Settings) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    if let Some(folder) = path.parent() {
        fs::create_dir_all(folder)?;
    }
    let temporary = path.with_extension("tmp");
    fs::write(&temporary, serde_json::to_vec_pretty(settings).expect("settings serialize"))?;
    fs::rename(&temporary, path)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn folder() -> std::path::PathBuf {
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("scorpio-store-{}-{:?}", std::process::id(), std::thread::current().id()));
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
        fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
        dir
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_file_gives_defaults() {
        assert_eq!(load(&folder().join("none.json")), Settings::default());
    }

    #[test]
    fn round_trip_keeps_everything() {
        let path = folder().join("settings.json");
        let mut settings = Settings::default();
        settings.saved.push(Saved { name: "Mine".into(), crosshair: Crosshair { gap: -2, ..Crosshair::default() } });
        settings.games.push(Game {
            name: "CS2".into(),
            fingerprint: Fingerprint { name: "cs2".into(), path: "c:\\games\\cs2.exe".into(), ..Fingerprint::default() },
            crosshair: Some(Crosshair { color: [255, 0, 0], ..Crosshair::default() }),
        });
        settings.aim = Aim { hide: true, button: MouseButton::Back, mode: AimMode::Toggle };
        settings.offset = (3, -1);
        save(&path, &settings).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(load(&path), settings.validated());
    }

    #[test]
    fn partial_file_fills_in_defaults() {
        let path = folder().join("settings.json");
        fs::write(&path, r#"{"aim":{"hide":true},"crosshair":{"gap":-3}}"#).unwrap();
        let settings = load(&path);
        assert!(settings.aim.hide);
        assert_eq!(settings.aim.button, MouseButton::Right);
        assert_eq!(settings.crosshair.gap, -3);
        assert_eq!(settings.keys, Keys::default());
    }

    #[test]
    fn broken_file_is_kept_aside() {
        let dir = folder();
        let path = dir.join("settings.json");
        fs::write(&path, "{ not json").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(load(&path), Settings::default());
        let kept = fs::read_dir(&dir).unwrap().filter_map(Result::ok).any(|e| e.file_name().to_string_lossy().starts_with("settings.broken-"));
        assert!(kept);
    }

    #[test]
    fn validation_cleans_and_is_idempotent() {
        let mut settings = Settings::default();
        settings.saved.push(Saved { name: "  \u{7}".into(), crosshair: Crosshair::default() });
        settings.saved.push(Saved { name: "x".repeat(200), crosshair: Crosshair::default() });
        settings.games.push(Game::default());
        settings.games.push(Game { name: String::new(), fingerprint: Fingerprint { name: "cs2".into(), ..Fingerprint::default() }, crosshair: None });
        settings.offset = (99_999, -99_999);
        let once = settings.validated();
        assert_eq!(once.saved[0].name, "Crosshair");
        assert_eq!(once.saved[1].name.chars().count(), NAME_LIMIT);
        assert_eq!(once.games.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(once.games[0].name, "cs2");
        assert_eq!(once.offset, (4000, -4000));
        assert_eq!(once.clone().validated(), once);
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/src/update.rs`:

```rs
//! Updates: signed packages from the releases page, checked at start and
//! every few hours. An automatic update is downloaded and installed only
//! after no game has been in front for a few minutes, so a restart never
//! lands in a match and the package never waits in memory. Only Scorpio's
//! own HTTPS request is involved; nothing touches a game.

use serde::Serialize;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::Duration;
use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter, Manager};
use tauri_plugin_updater::{Update, UpdaterExt};

const FIRST_CHECK: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);
const EVERY: Duration = Duration::from_secs(6 * 60 * 60);
/// How long no game must be in front before an automatic install.
const QUIET: Duration = Duration::from_secs(3 * 60);

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize)]
#[serde(tag = "state", rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub enum UpdateState {
    Idle,
    Checking,
    UpToDate,
    Available { version: String, notes: Option<String> },
    Downloading { version: String, percent: u8 },
    Installing { version: String },
    Failed { message: String },
    /// This install is updated by something else (a Linux package).
    Unsupported,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Updates {
    state: Mutex<Option<UpdateState>>,
    found: Mutex<Option<Update>>,
    busy: AtomicBool,
    /// Counts the times a game came to the front; a quiet wait that sees it
    /// change was interrupted by play.
    games_seen: AtomicU64,
    waiting: AtomicBool,
}

/// The updater replaces an AppImage or runs the Windows installer; a .deb
/// install belongs to the package manager.
pub fn supported() -> bool {
    !cfg!(target_os = "linux") || std::env::var_os("APPIMAGE").is_some()
}

pub fn state(handle: &AppHandle) -> UpdateState {
    let current = handle.state::<Updates>().state.lock().unwrap().clone();
    current.unwrap_or(if supported() { UpdateState::Idle } else { UpdateState::Unsupported })
}

fn set(handle: &AppHandle, state: UpdateState) {
    *handle.state::<Updates>().state.lock().unwrap() = Some(state.clone());
    let _ = handle.emit_to(crate::SETTINGS_WINDOW, "update", &state);
}

fn plain(error: &str) -> String {
    if error.contains("404") || error.to_lowercase().contains("could not fetch") {
        "The update server could not be reached. Try again later.".into()
    } else {
        format!("The update could not be checked: {error}")
    }
}

/// Looks for a newer version and remembers it.
pub async fn check(handle: &AppHandle) -> UpdateState {
    if !supported() {
        return UpdateState::Unsupported;
    }
    set(handle, UpdateState::Checking);
    let result = match handle.updater() {
        Ok(updater) => updater.check().await,
        Err(error) => Err(error),
    };
    let state = match result {
        Ok(Some(update)) => {
            let state = UpdateState::Available { version: update.version.clone(), notes: update.body.clone() };
            *handle.state::<Updates>().found.lock().unwrap() = Some(update);
            state
        }
        Ok(None) => UpdateState::UpToDate,
        Err(error) => UpdateState::Failed { message: plain(&error.to_string()) },
    };
    set(handle, state.clone());
    state
}

/// Downloads the update found by `check` and installs it; Scorpio then
/// restarts (on Windows the installer closes it and starts the new one).
async fn download_and_install(handle: &AppHandle) {
    let update = handle.state::<Updates>().found.lock().unwrap().clone();
    let Some(update) = update else { return };
    let version = update.version.clone();
    let progress = handle.clone();
    let progress_version = version.clone();
    let mut received = 0usize;
    let bytes = update
        .download(
            move |chunk, total| {
                received += chunk;
                if let Some(total) = total.filter(|t| *t > 0) {
                    let percent = ((received as u64 * 100) / total).min(100) as u8;
                    set(&progress, UpdateState::Downloading { version: progress_version.clone(), percent });
                }
            },
            || {},
        )
        .await;
    let bytes = match bytes {
        Ok(bytes) => bytes,
        Err(error) => return set(handle, UpdateState::Failed { message: format!("The download failed: {error}") }),
    };
    set(handle, UpdateState::Installing { version });
    crate::before_exit(handle);
    match update.install(bytes) {
        Ok(()) => handle.restart(),
        Err(error) => set(handle, UpdateState::Failed { message: format!("The update could not be installed: {error}") }),
    }
}

/// Runs `work` unless an update is already being checked or installed.
async fn exclusive(handle: &AppHandle, work: impl std::future::Future<Output = ()>) {
    let updates = handle.state::<Updates>();
    if updates.busy.swap(true, Ordering::SeqCst) {
        return;
    }
    work.await;
    updates.busy.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
}

/// The button: the player is in the settings window, not in a game.
pub async fn install_now(handle: AppHandle) {
    let work = async {
        let found = handle.state::<Updates>().found.lock().unwrap().is_some();
        if found || matches!(check(&handle).await, UpdateState::Available { .. }) {
            download_and_install(&handle).await;
        }
    };
    exclusive(&handle, work).await;
}

/// Called after every change of the window in front. Once no game has been
/// in front for `QUIET`, a waiting automatic update installs.
pub fn on_focus(handle: &AppHandle, game_in_front: bool, manual: bool) {
    let updates = handle.state::<Updates>();
    if game_in_front {
        updates.games_seen.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
        return;
    }
    let found = updates.found.lock().unwrap().is_some();
    if manual || !found || updates.waiting.swap(true, Ordering::SeqCst) {
        return;
    }
    let seen = updates.games_seen.load(Ordering::SeqCst);
    let handle = handle.clone();
    let spawned = std::thread::Builder::new().name("scorpio-update-wait".into()).spawn(move || {
        std::thread::sleep(QUIET);
        let updates = handle.state::<Updates>();
        updates.waiting.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
        let quiet = updates.games_seen.load(Ordering::SeqCst) == seen && !crate::game_in_front(&handle);
        if quiet && !crate::settings_snapshot(&handle).update_manually {
            tauri::async_runtime::block_on(exclusive(&handle, download_and_install(&handle)));
        }
    });
    if spawned.is_err() {
        updates.waiting.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    }
}

/// Starts the background checks on a thread of their own that sleeps
/// between them.
pub fn start(handle: &AppHandle) {
    if !supported() {
        return;
    }
    let handle = handle.clone();
    let spawned = std::thread::Builder::new().name("scorpio-updates".into()).spawn(move || {
        std::thread::sleep(FIRST_CHECK);
        loop {
            let manual = crate::settings_snapshot(&handle).update_manually;
            let already = handle.state::<Updates>().found.lock().unwrap().is_some();
            if !manual && !already {
                tauri::async_runtime::block_on(exclusive(&handle, async {
                    check(&handle).await;
                }));
                on_focus(&handle, crate::game_in_front(&handle), manual);
            }
            std::thread::sleep(EVERY);
        }
    });
    if let Err(error) = spawned {
        log::warn!("update checks not started: {error}");
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/tests/crosshair.rs`:

```rs
//! Share codes and drawing, checked against published reference codes.

use scorpio_lib::core::crosshair::{draw, pixels, read_code, resting_style, to_svg, write_code, Crosshair, Decoded, Piece, Pixels};
use serde_json::Value;

fn vectors() -> Vec<(String, Value)> {
    let fixture: Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!("fixtures/cs2-sharecodes.json")).unwrap();
    fixture["vectors"].as_array().unwrap().iter().map(|v| (v["code"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned(), v["expected"].clone())).collect()
}

fn format_of(expected: &Value) -> &str {
    expected["format"].as_str().unwrap()
}

fn code_starting(prefix: &str) -> (String, Value) {
    vectors().into_iter().find(|(code, _)| code.starts_with(prefix)).unwrap()
}

fn int(v: &Value) -> i64 {
    v.as_i64().unwrap()
}

/// Scorpio's fields as the fixture names them (akiver/csgo-sharecode's names).
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
struct View {
    format: String,
    style: u8,
    dot: bool,
    t_shape: bool,
    color: [u8; 3],
    alpha: u8,
    gap: i64,
    length: i64,
    thickness: f64,
    made_for: i64,
    outline: u8,
}

fn view(d: &Decoded) -> View {
    let s = &d.crosshair;
    View {
        format: d.format.to_owned(),
        style: s.style,
        dot: s.dot,
        t_shape: s.t_shape,
        color: s.color,
        alpha: s.alpha,
        gap: i64::from(s.gap),
        length: i64::from(s.length),
        thickness: f64::from(s.thickness),
        made_for: i64::from(s.made_for),
        outline: s.outline,
    }
}

fn expected_view(e: &Value) -> View {
    let outline = e.get("outlineMode").map_or_else(|| u8::from(e["outlineEnabled"].as_bool().unwrap()), |mode| int(mode) as u8);
    View {
        format: format_of(e).to_owned(),
        style: resting_style(int(&e["style"]) as u8),
        dot: e["centerDotEnabled"].as_bool().unwrap(),
        t_shape: e["tStyleEnabled"].as_bool().unwrap(),
        color: [int(&e["red"]) as u8, int(&e["green"]) as u8, int(&e["blue"]) as u8],
        alpha: int(&e["alpha"]) as u8,
        gap: int(&e["gap"]),
        length: int(&e["length"]),
        thickness: e["thickness"].as_f64().unwrap(),
        made_for: int(&e["screenHeight"]),
        outline,
    }
}

#[test]
fn every_pixel_era_reference_code_reads_to_its_published_values() {
    let pixel_era: Vec<_> = vectors().into_iter().filter(|(_, e)| format_of(e) != "legacy-v1").collect();
    assert_eq!(pixel_era.len(), 12);
    for (code, expected) in pixel_era {
        assert_eq!(view(&read_code(&code, 1080).unwrap()), expected_view(&expected), "{code}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_cs2_code_comes_back_character_for_character() {
    let statics: Vec<_> = vectors()
        .into_iter()
        .filter(|(_, e)| format_of(e) == "cs2-v1" && [3, 4, 6, 8, 9].contains(&int(&e["style"])))
        .collect();
    assert!(!statics.is_empty());
    for (code, _) in statics {
        assert_eq!(write_code(&read_code(&code, 1080).unwrap().crosshair), code);
    }

    let (dynamic, _) = vectors().into_iter().find(|(_, e)| format_of(e) == "cs2-v1" && int(&e["style"]) == 2).unwrap();
    let decoded = read_code(&dynamic, 1080).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((decoded.code_style, decoded.crosshair.style), (2, 4), "a dynamic style comes in at rest");
}

#[test]
fn the_split_recoil_and_scope_values_survive_a_round_trip() {
    let (code, e) = code_starting("CSAB6");
    let again = read_code(&write_code(&read_code(&code, 1080).unwrap().crosshair), 1080).unwrap();
    let x = &again.crosshair.extra;
    let f = |key: &str| e[key].as_f64().unwrap() as f32;
    assert_eq!(x.follow_recoil, e["followRecoil"].as_bool().unwrap());
    assert_eq!(i64::from(x.spread_limit), int(&e["dynamicSpreadLimit"]));
    assert_eq!(i64::from(x.split_distance), int(&e["splitDistance"]));
    assert_eq!(x.inner_split_alpha, f("innerSplitAlpha"));
    assert_eq!(x.outer_split_alpha, f("outerSplitAlpha"));
    assert_eq!(x.split_size_ratio, f("splitSizeRatio"));
    assert_eq!(x.scope_dot_scale, f("scopeDotScale"));
    assert_eq!(x.scope_dot_uses_color, e["scopeDotUseCrosshairColor"].as_bool().unwrap());
}

#[test]
fn an_old_csgo_code_is_placed_at_the_players_screen_as_the_old_renderer_drew_it() {
    let code = "CSGO-WsnnD-eHaMw-QNDf9-oxuDh-ydOUD";
    assert!(vectors().iter().any(|(c, _)| c == code));
    let at480 = read_code(code, 480).unwrap();
    let s = &at480.crosshair;
    assert_eq!(at480.format, "legacy-v1");
    assert_eq!((s.length, s.thickness, s.gap, s.alpha, s.outline), (10, 1.0, 1, 200, 1));
    assert_eq!(read_code(code, 1080).unwrap().crosshair.length, 22, "size 10 at 1080p is 22.5, which rounds to even");
}

#[test]
fn every_legacy_v1_reference_code_reads() {
    for (code, e) in vectors().into_iter().filter(|(_, e)| format_of(e) == "legacy-v1") {
        let decoded = read_code(&code, 1080).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(decoded.format, "legacy-v1", "{code}");
        assert_eq!(decoded.code_style, int(&e["style"]) as u8, "{code}");
        assert_eq!(decoded.crosshair.color, [int(&e["red"]) as u8, int(&e["green"]) as u8, int(&e["blue"]) as u8], "{code}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn anything_else_is_refused() {
    let first = &vectors()[0].0;
    let bad_sum = format!("{}A", &first[..first.len() - 1]);
    for bad in ["", "hello", "CSGO-12345-12345-12345-12345-12345", &bad_sum, &format!("CS{}", "A".repeat(44))] {
        assert!(read_code(bad, 1080).is_err(), "{bad}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_code_with_spaces_around_it_reads() {
    let (code, _) = code_starting("CSAB6");
    assert!(read_code(&format!("  {code}\n"), 1080).is_ok());
}

#[test]
fn settings_are_kept_in_range() {
    // The reference also feeds length 999 and colour [300, -4, "x"]; Rust's
    // u8 fields cannot hold those, so the type system does that part.
    let s = Crosshair { thickness: 1.3, gap: -9999, outline: 7, outline_width: 0, made_for: 10, style: 2, ..Crosshair::default() }.normalized();
    assert_eq!((s.thickness, s.gap, s.outline, s.outline_width, s.made_for, s.style), (1.5, -3840, 2, 1, 240, 4));
    let wild = Crosshair { thickness: f32::NAN, style: 31, ..Crosshair::default() }.normalized();
    assert_eq!((wild.thickness, wild.style), (2.0, 4));
}

#[test]
fn sizes_scale_with_the_screen_thickness_keeps_quarter_pixels() {
    let at720 = Crosshair { length: 8, thickness: 2.0, gap: 4, made_for: 720, ..Crosshair::default() };
    assert_eq!(pixels(&at720, 1440), Pixels { length: 16, thickness: 4.0, gap: 8, outline: 2 });
    let thin = Crosshair { length: 8, thickness: 1.0, gap: 4, made_for: 1080, ..Crosshair::default() };
    assert_eq!(pixels(&thin, 1440), Pixels { length: 11, thickness: 1.25, gap: 5, outline: 1 });
}

fn bars(crosshair: &Crosshair) -> (Vec<[f32; 4]>, f32, u32) {
    let drawing = draw(crosshair, 1080);
    let rects = drawing
        .pieces
        .iter()
        .map(|piece| match *piece {
            Piece::Rect { x, y, w, h, .. } => [x, y, w, h],
            Piece::Ring { .. } => panic!("no rings in these shapes"),
        })
        .collect();
    (rects, (drawing.size / 2) as f32, drawing.size)
}

fn cross(length: u8, thickness: f32, gap: i16) -> Crosshair {
    Crosshair { style: 4, length, thickness, gap, made_for: 1080, ..Crosshair::default() }
}

#[test]
fn a_static_cross_four_bars_beyond_the_gap_all_starting_on_whole_pixels() {
    let (bars, c, size) = bars(&Crosshair { dot: true, ..cross(8, 2.0, 4) });
    assert_eq!(size % 2, 0);
    assert_eq!(bars[0], [c - 1.0 + 2.0 + 4.0, c - 1.0, 8.0, 2.0]);
    assert_eq!(*bars.last().unwrap(), [c - 1.0, c - 1.0, 2.0, 2.0], "the dot is as wide as the lines");
    assert_eq!(bars.len(), 5);

    let (half, _, _) = self::bars(&cross(8, 1.5, 4));
    assert!(half.iter().all(|b| b[0].fract() == 0.0 && b[1].fract() == 0.0), "a half step only softens the far edge");
    assert_eq!(half[0][3], 1.5);
}

#[test]
fn the_t_shape_drops_the_top_bar_not_another_one() {
    let (bars, c, _) = bars(&Crosshair { t_shape: true, ..cross(6, 2.0, 3) });
    assert_eq!(bars.len(), 3);
    assert!(bars.iter().all(|b| b[1] + b[3] > c - 1.0), "nothing above the centre block");
}

#[test]
fn outline_width_grows_the_full_outline_and_the_half_outlines_two_edges() {
    let dot = |outline, outline_width| Crosshair { style: 6, thickness: 4.0, outline, outline_width, made_for: 1080, ..Crosshair::default() };
    let full = draw(&dot(1, 3), 1080);
    match full.pieces[0] {
        Piece::Rect { x, y, w, h, .. } => assert_eq!((x == y, w, h), (true, 10.0, 10.0), "a 4 px dot with 3 px all round"),
        Piece::Ring { .. } => panic!("expected a rect"),
    }
    assert!(to_svg(&full).contains(r#"width="10" height="10"/>"#));
    assert!(to_svg(&draw(&dot(2, 2), 1080)).contains(r#"height="2"/>"#), "a 2 px top edge");
    assert_eq!(write_code(&Crosshair { outline_width: 5, ..Crosshair::default() }), write_code(&Crosshair::default()), "not part of a share code");
}

#[test]
fn the_svg_draws_the_outline_first_and_rings_as_strokes() {
    let outlined = Crosshair { outline: 1, outline_color: [1, 2, 3], color: [200, 100, 50], ..Crosshair::default() };
    let svg = to_svg(&draw(&outlined, 1080));
    let outline_at = svg.find("rgba(1,2,3,1.000)").unwrap();
    assert!(outline_at < svg.find("rgba(200,100,50,1.000)").unwrap());
    assert!(svg.starts_with("<svg") && svg.ends_with("</g></svg>"));

    let circle = to_svg(&draw(&Crosshair { style: 3, ..Crosshair::default() }, 1080));
    assert!(circle.contains(r#"<circle cx="#) && circle.contains(r#"fill="none""#), "{circle}");
}

fn frame_centre(crosshair: &Crosshair) -> (f32, f32) {
    let d = draw(crosshair, 1080);
    let (mut x0, mut y0, mut x1, mut y1) = (f32::MAX, f32::MAX, f32::MIN, f32::MIN);
    for piece in &d.pieces {
        if let Piece::Rect { x, y, w, h, .. } = piece {
            (x0, y0, x1, y1) = (x0.min(*x), y0.min(*y), x1.max(x + w), y1.max(y + h));
        }
    }
    ((x0 + x1) / 2.0 - d.size as f32 / 2.0, (y0 + y1) / 2.0 - d.size as f32 / 2.0)
}

#[test]
fn square_and_quad_sit_where_a_cross_of_their_width_sits() {
    for style in [8, 9] {
        for (thickness, width) in [(1.0, 1.0), (1.5, 2.0), (2.0, 2.0), (2.5, 3.0), (3.0, 3.0)] {
            let c = Crosshair { style, thickness, length: 6, gap: 4, ..Crosshair::default() };
            let cross = Crosshair { style: 4, thickness: width, length: 6, gap: 4, ..Crosshair::default() };
            assert_eq!(frame_centre(&c), frame_centre(&cross), "style {style} thickness {thickness}");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_circle_has_an_outline_ring_under_it_as_wide_as_the_outline_asks() {
    for mode in [1, 2] {
        let c = Crosshair { style: 3, outline: mode, outline_width: 3, outline_color: [0, 0, 0], ..Crosshair::default() };
        let d = draw(&c, 1080);
        let rings: Vec<_> = d.pieces.iter().filter_map(|p| match p { Piece::Ring { width, rgba, .. } => Some((*width, *rgba)), _ => None }).collect();
        assert_eq!(rings.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(rings[0].1[..3], [0, 0, 0]);
        assert_eq!(rings[0].0, rings[1].0 + 6.0);
    }
    let plain = draw(&Crosshair { style: 3, outline: 0, ..Crosshair::default() }, 1080);
    assert_eq!(plain.pieces.iter().filter(|p| matches!(p, Piece::Ring { .. })).count(), 1);
}
```

`tools/icon.js`:

```js
// Renders Scorpio's icons from the vector sources in assets/brand.
//
//   node tools/icon.js           writes tools/icon.png (1024 px) and the two tray PNGs
//   node tools/icon.js --icons   the same, then runs `npx tauri icon tools/icon.png`,
//                                deletes the outputs Scorpio does not ship, and puts the
//                                pixel-drawn tray icon into the small sizes
//
// Why the small sizes are replaced: Tauri gives the tray the 32 px icon (48 px at
// 150 % scaling) and the shell shrinks it to half. A 1024 px drawing shrunk that far
// is a blur. The tray drawings sit on whole pixels and are stored at exactly double
// size, so they halve cleanly.
//
// The renderer is a headless Chromium. Set CHROME to its path, or let the script
// look in the Playwright cache and the usual install places.
const fs = require( 'fs' )
const os = require( 'os' )
const path = require( 'path' )
const { execFileSync, execSync } = require( 'child_process' )
const { pathToFileURL } = require( 'url' )

const ROOT = path.join( __dirname, '..' )
const BRAND = path.join( ROOT, 'assets', 'brand' )
const ICONS = path.join( ROOT, 'src-tauri', 'icons' )

// size in src-tauri/icons -> the tray drawing it is rendered from
const SMALL = {
	16: 'scorpio-tray.svg',
	24: 'scorpio-tray-24.svg',
	32: 'scorpio-tray.svg',
	48: 'scorpio-tray-24.svg',
}

const findChrome = () => {

	const names = [ 'chrome-headless-shell', 'chrome-headless-shell.exe', 'chrome', 'chrome.exe', path.join( 'Chromium.app', 'Contents', 'MacOS', 'Chromium' ) ]
	const caches = [
		path.join( os.homedir(), 'Library', 'Caches', 'ms-playwright' ),
		path.join( os.homedir(), '.cache', 'ms-playwright' ),
		path.join( process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '', 'ms-playwright' ),
	]
	const found = []

	if ( process.env.CHROME ) found.push( process.env.CHROME )

	for ( const cache of caches ) {

		if ( ! fs.existsSync( cache ) ) continue

		for ( const build of fs.readdirSync( cache ).filter( name => name.startsWith( 'chromium' ) ).sort().reverse() ) {

			for ( const inner of fs.readdirSync( path.join( cache, build ) ) ) {

				for ( const name of names ) found.push( path.join( cache, build, inner, name ) )

			}

		}

	}

	found.push(
		'/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome',
		'/usr/bin/chromium',
		'/usr/bin/chromium-browser',
		'/usr/bin/google-chrome',
		'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
		'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Microsoft\\Edge\\Application\\msedge.exe',
	)

	const chrome = found.find( file => fs.existsSync( file ) && fs.statSync( file ).isFile() )

	if ( ! chrome ) throw new Error( 'No Chromium found. Set CHROME to a Chrome, Chromium or Edge binary.' )

	return chrome

}

const chrome = findChrome()

// Draws one SVG file into a square PNG with a transparent ground.
const render = ( svg, png, size ) => {

	execFileSync( chrome, [
		'--headless',
		'--hide-scrollbars',
		'--default-background-color=00000000',
		`--screenshot=${ png }`,
		`--window-size=${ size },${ size }`,
		pathToFileURL( svg ).href,
	], { stdio: 'ignore' } )

	const head = fs.readFileSync( png )

	if ( head.readUInt32BE( 16 ) !== size || head.readUInt32BE( 20 ) !== size ) throw new Error( `${ png } is not ${ size } px square` )

	if ( png.startsWith( ROOT ) ) console.log( path.relative( ROOT, png ), `${ size } px` )

}

// Swaps the PNG entries of the given sizes in an .ico file. Entry order stays as Tauri wrote it.
const patchIco = ( file, layers ) => {

	const ico = fs.readFileSync( file )
	const count = ico.readUInt16LE( 4 )
	const entries = []

	for ( let i = 0; i < count; i++ ) {

		const at = 6 + ( i * 16 )
		const size = ico[at] || 256
		const data = layers[size] || ico.subarray( ico.readUInt32LE( at + 12 ), ico.readUInt32LE( at + 12 ) + ico.readUInt32LE( at + 8 ) )

		entries.push( { head: Buffer.from( ico.subarray( at, at + 16 ) ), data } )

	}

	const missing = Object.keys( layers ).filter( size => ! entries.some( entry => ( entry.head[0] || 256 ) === Number( size ) ) )

	if ( missing.length ) throw new Error( `${ file } has no ${ missing.join( ', ' ) } px entry` )

	let offset = 6 + ( count * 16 )

	for ( const entry of entries ) {

		entry.head.writeUInt32LE( entry.data.length, 8 )
		entry.head.writeUInt32LE( offset, 12 )
		offset += entry.data.length

	}

	fs.writeFileSync( file, Buffer.concat( [ ico.subarray( 0, 6 ), ...entries.map( entry => entry.head ), ...entries.map( entry => entry.data ) ] ) )

}

render( path.join( BRAND, 'scorpio-icon.svg' ), path.join( __dirname, 'icon.png' ), 1024 )
render( path.join( BRAND, 'scorpio-tray.svg' ), path.join( BRAND, 'scorpio-tray-16.png' ), 16 )
render( path.join( BRAND, 'scorpio-tray.svg' ), path.join( BRAND, 'scorpio-tray-32.png' ), 32 )

if ( process.argv.includes( '--icons' ) ) {

	execSync( 'npx tauri icon tools/icon.png', { cwd: ROOT, stdio: 'inherit' } )

	// Scorpio ships for Windows and Linux only
	for ( const name of fs.readdirSync( ICONS ) ) {

		if ( name === 'android' || name === 'ios' || name === 'icon.icns' || name === 'StoreLogo.png' || name.startsWith( 'Square' ) ) fs.rmSync( path.join( ICONS, name ), { recursive: true } )

	}

	const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync( path.join( os.tmpdir(), 'scorpio-icon-' ) )
	const layers = {}

	for ( const [ size, drawing ] of Object.entries( SMALL ) ) {

		const png = path.join( tmp, `${ size }.png` )

		render( path.join( BRAND, drawing ), png, Number( size ) )
		layers[size] = fs.readFileSync( png )

	}

	fs.writeFileSync( path.join( ICONS, '32x32.png' ), layers[32] )
	patchIco( path.join( ICONS, 'icon.ico' ), layers )
	fs.rmSync( tmp, { recursive: true } )
	console.log( 'src-tauri/icons: 16, 24, 32 and 48 px are the pixel-drawn tray icon' )

}

```

`tools/measure-windows.ps1`:

```ps1
# Checks a running Scorpio against docs/budget.md. Run on Windows while
# Scorpio is running with its settings window closed:
#   powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\measure-windows.ps1 [-AfterSettings]
# -AfterSettings uses the budget for after the settings window was opened
# and closed once (the WebView2 runtime keeps a few threads loaded).
param([switch]$AfterSettings)
$budgetPrivateMB = if ($AfterSettings) { 12.8 } else { 8.6 }   # measured 10.2 / 6.9, +25 %
$budgetCpuMs = 50                                               # measured 0 ms over 10 s idle
$p = Get-Process scorpio -ErrorAction Stop
$c1 = $p.TotalProcessorTime.TotalMilliseconds; Start-Sleep 10; $p.Refresh()
$cpu = $p.TotalProcessorTime.TotalMilliseconds - $c1
$mb = $p.PrivateMemorySize64 / 1MB
$web = @(Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter "Name='msedgewebview2.exe'" | Where-Object { $_.CommandLine -like '*com.lxxvii.scorpio*' }).Count
"private {0:N1} MB (budget {1}), cpu {2} ms over 10 s (budget {3}), web view processes {4} (budget 0)" -f $mb, $budgetPrivateMB, $cpu, $budgetCpuMs, $web
if ($mb -gt $budgetPrivateMB -or $cpu -gt $budgetCpuMs -or $web -gt 0) { Write-Error 'over budget'; exit 1 }

```

`tools/site/build.js`:

```js
// Generator for docs/site/index.html. Run: node tools/site/build.js
// The page follows the lxxvii style (~/.claude/skills/lxxvii-style, rules R1 to R17).
// The FAQ is written once here and printed twice: as visible HTML and as FAQPage JSON-LD, so the two always match.
const fs = require( 'fs' )
const OUT = require( 'path' ).join( __dirname, '../../docs/site/index.html' )
const SITE = 'https://scorpio.lxxvll.com/'
const REPO = 'https://github.com/Deikus-LXXVII/Scorpio-Crosshair'
const DOWNLOAD = REPO + '/releases/latest'
const VERSION = '0.1.1'
const DATE = '2026-11-21'

const TITLE = 'Scorpio: crosshair overlay for Windows and Linux'
// The definition sentence. It lives in the meta description and the JSON-LD; the hero carries only the short line.
const DESC = 'Free crosshair overlay for Windows and Linux. Scorpio draws a CS2-based crosshair over your game only while you play, and never touches the game or its anti-cheat.'
const LINE = 'A crosshair over your game, only while you play'

const BANDS = 'M647.8 403.4L896 512L512 680L484.2 667.8L453.5 597.7L512 623.2L766.3 512L678.5 473.6ZM403.4 376.2L512 128L680 512L667.8 539.8L597.7 570.5L623.2 512L512 257.7L473.6 345.5ZM376.2 620.6L128 512L512 344L539.8 356.2L570.5 426.3L512 400.8L257.7 512L345.5 550.4ZM620.6 647.8L512 896L344 512L356.2 484.2L426.3 453.5L400.8 512L512 766.3L550.4 678.5Z'
const mark = label => `<svg viewBox="128 128 768 768" role="img" aria-label="${label}"><path fill="#ebe5da" d="${BANDS}"/><path fill="#c8435a" d="M468 512L512 468L556 512L512 556Z"/></svg>`

// [question, answer as HTML]. Three sentences at most; links and <b> only.
const FAQ = [
	[ 'Is a crosshair overlay bannable?',
		`No overlay can promise that you will never be banned. Scorpio never reads or changes the game, but a game's own rules can forbid external crosshairs.` ],
	[ 'Does it work with Vanguard, Easy Anti-Cheat, BattlEye or VAC?',
		`None of them has approved it directly. Scorpio crosses none of their lines, since it never touches the game or the anti-cheat. The exception is a game whose own rules forbid crosshair overlays.` ],
	[ 'Does Scorpio lower my frame rate?',
		`It does not touch the game or its input. Its effect on the frame rate has not been measured yet.` ],
	[ 'Does Scorpio collect data?',
		`No. It has no telemetry and no account, and goes online only for updates from GitHub.` ],
]
const plain = html => html.replace( /<[^>]+>/g, '' )

// Every operating system call Scorpio makes: [need, Windows, Linux (X11), why it stays outside the game].
// On a phone the rows stack; only the two system columns keep a label there (data-h), the reason reads as one.
const HEAD = [ 'Need', 'Windows', 'Linux (X11)', 'Why it stays outside the game' ]
const CALLS = [
	[ 'Window in front', '<code>SetWinEventHook</code>, out of context', '<code>_NET_ACTIVE_WINDOW</code> events', 'Nothing is loaded into another process' ],
	[ 'Title, class and size', '<code>GetWindowTextW</code>, <code>GetClassNameW</code>, <code>GetWindowRect</code>', 'Window manager properties', 'No process is opened' ],
	[ 'Program path', '<code>NtQuerySystemInformation</code>', '<code>/proc/&lt;pid&gt;/exe</code>', 'No process handle' ],
	[ 'Aim button, hide key', 'Raw Input on its own window', 'XInput2 raw events', 'No hooks' ],
	[ 'Shortcuts', '<code>RegisterHotKey</code>', '<code>XGrabKey</code>', 'The standard shortcut API' ],
	[ 'The crosshair', 'A layered, topmost, click-through window', 'An override-redirect ARGB window', 'Nothing is drawn into the game' ],
	[ 'OBS, opt-in', 'HTTP on <code>127.0.0.1:47117</code>', 'The same', 'Your own computer only' ],
]

const SHOTS = {
	games: [ 'scorpio-game-detection.webp', 'Scorpio Games tab: two added games, one with its own crosshair, and switches for fullscreen windows and games from launchers' ],
	editor: [ 'scorpio-saved-and-pro-crosshairs.webp', 'Scorpio Crosshair tab: a green crosshair in the preview at 4x, its share code, four saved crosshairs and pro player crosshairs' ],
	obs: [ 'scorpio-obs-browser-source.webp', 'Scorpio System tab: Show the crosshair in OBS is on, with three setup steps and the Browser source address' ],
	controls: [ 'scorpio-hide-while-aiming-and-shortcuts.webp', 'Scorpio Controls tab: the hide while aiming switch, the aim button, hold or toggle mode, and the six global shortcuts' ],
}
// The hero. Three concepts; switch with this one line.
//   'a' Signum: the Nodus mark cut into the tablet, large, beside the name; its porphyry point is the page's one colour.
//   'b' In front: the tablet is a screen with a drawn hall in it, and the crosshair stands at its vanishing point.
//   'c' Lineae: the crosshair's arms are cut across the tablet; the name and the action sit in its quadrants, on the page's own keylines.
const HERO = 'a'

// Hall for concept b: a vaulted colonnade in one-point perspective, drawn around its vanishing point (0, 0), in px.
const hall = () => {
	const F = 560, W = 1.15, FLOOR = .62, TOP = -.95, NEAR = .55, FAR = 11
	const Z = [ 1, 1.45, 2.1, 3.05, 4.4, 6.4 ]
	const p = ( x, y, z ) => [ +( x * F / z ).toFixed( 1 ), +( y * F / z ).toFixed( 1 ) ]
	const ln = ( a, b, o ) => `<path d="M${a[ 0 ]} ${a[ 1 ]}L${b[ 0 ]} ${b[ 1 ]}" stroke-opacity="${o}"/>`
	const out = []
	const op = z => Math.max( .05, .2 - z * .022 ).toFixed( 3 )
	// the floor's edges, the architrave and the floor's joints run to the vanishing point
	for ( const s of [ -1, 1 ] ) {
		out.push( ln( p( s * W, FLOOR, NEAR ), p( s * W, FLOOR, FAR ), .16 ) )
		out.push( ln( p( s * W, TOP, NEAR ), p( s * W, TOP, FAR ), .16 ) )
	}
	for ( let x = -W + .23; x < W - .1; x += .23 ) if ( Math.abs( x ) > .01 ) out.push( ln( p( x, FLOOR, NEAR ), p( x, FLOOR, FAR ), .07 ) )
	for ( const z of Z ) {
		const o = op( z )
		out.push( ln( p( -W, FLOOR, z ), p( W, FLOOR, z ), o * .6 ) )
		// a column on each side: two edges, a capital and a base
		for ( const s of [ -1, 1 ] ) {
			const xo = s * W, xi = s * ( W - .12 )
			out.push( ln( p( xo, FLOOR, z ), p( xo, TOP, z ), o ), ln( p( xi, FLOOR, z ), p( xi, TOP, z ), o ) )
			out.push( ln( p( s * ( W + .03 ), TOP + .05, z ), p( s * ( W - .15 ), TOP + .05, z ), o ) )
			out.push( ln( p( s * ( W + .03 ), FLOOR - .04, z ), p( s * ( W - .15 ), FLOOR - .04, z ), o ) )
		}
		// the vault: a half circle from column to column
		const [ cx, cy ] = p( 0, TOP, z ), r = +( W * F / z ).toFixed( 1 )
		out.push( `<path d="M${cx - r} ${cy}A${r} ${r} 0 0 1 ${cx + r} ${cy}" stroke-opacity="${o}"/>` )
	}
	return `<svg class="hb-hall" viewBox="-1200 -900 2400 1800" aria-hidden="true"><g fill="none" stroke="#ebe5da" stroke-width="1" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke">${out.join( '' )}</g></svg>`
}

// The crosshair at the vanishing point: four porphyry arms with a cut outline, CS2's classic shape.
const crosshair = `<svg class="hb-x" viewBox="-16 -16 32 32" aria-hidden="true"><g fill="#c8435a" stroke="#060605" stroke-width="1"><rect x="-1.5" y="-13.5" width="3" height="9"/><rect x="-1.5" y="4.5" width="3" height="9"/><rect x="-13.5" y="-1.5" width="9" height="3"/><rect x="4.5" y="-1.5" width="9" height="3"/></g></svg>`

const cue = cls => `<a class="cue${cls ? ' ' + cls : ''}" href="#games" aria-label="Go to the first section"><i></i></a>`
const download = `<a class="btn" href="${DOWNLOAD}">Download Scorpio</a>`

const HEROES = {
	a: `
	<header class="tablet hero-a">
		<div class="ha-copy">
			<h1 class="ha-name">Scorpio</h1>
			<p class="caps">${LINE}</p>

		</div>

			<defs>

				<radialGradient id="ha-l" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse" cx="330" cy="300" r="520"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#f0e4ce" stop-opacity=".34"/><stop offset=".45" stop-color="#f0e4ce" stop-opacity=".07"/><stop offset=".72" stop-color="#f0e4ce" stop-opacity="0"/></radialGradient>
			</defs>
			<use href="#ha-b" transform="translate(0 2.5)" fill="#ebe1cd" fill-opacity=".05"/>
			<use href="#ha-b" transform="translate(0 -3)" fill="#000" fill-opacity=".7"/>
			<use href="#ha-b" fill="#060605"/>
			<use href="#ha-b" fill="url(#ha-l)"/>
			<path d="M468 512L512 468L556 512L512 556Z" fill="#c8435a"/>
			<path d="M468 512L512 468L556 512" fill="none" stroke="#e8899a" stroke-opacity=".5" stroke-width="2"/>
		</svg>
		${cue()}
	</header>`,
	b: `
	<header class="tablet hero-b">

// Per-concept styles. Shared parts (wall, tablet, edge, cramps, caps, button, cue) stay in the main sheet.

// reveal once, and light the name of the block in the middle of the screen. Both start a change; neither follows the scroll.
const js = `
// FAQ answers open and close with their height, on the guide's one curve.
// Reduced motion leaves the native, instant toggle alone.
for ( const d of document.querySelectorAll( '.qa' ) ) {
	const s = d.querySelector( 'summary' ), a = d.querySelector( '.a' )
	let run = null
	s.addEventListener( 'click', e => {
		if ( matchMedia( '(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)' ).matches ) return
		e.preventDefault()
		const from = run ? a.offsetHeight : ( d.open ? a.scrollHeight : 0 )
		run?.cancel()
		const opening = !d.open || ( run && run.closing )
		d.open = true
		const to = opening ? a.scrollHeight : 0
		run = a.animate( [ { height: from + 'px', opacity: opening ? 0 : 1 }, { height: to + 'px', opacity: opening ? 1 : 0 } ],
			{ duration: opening ? 400 : 300, easing: 'cubic-bezier(.2,.7,.15,1)' } )
		run.closing = !opening
		run.onfinish = () => { if ( !opening ) d.open = false; run = null }
	} )
}
(() => {
	if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) { document.querySelectorAll('.rv').forEach((el) => el.classList.add('in')); return }
	const rv = new IntersectionObserver((es) => es.forEach((e) => { if (e.isIntersecting) { e.target.classList.add('in'); rv.unobserve(e.target) } }), { threshold: .15 })
	document.querySelectorAll('.rv').forEach((el) => rv.observe(el))
	const lit = new IntersectionObserver((es) => es.forEach((e) => e.target.classList.toggle('current', e.isIntersecting)), { rootMargin: '-40% 0px -40% 0px' })
	document.querySelectorAll('.block').forEach((el) => lit.observe(el))
})()
`

const block = ( id, name, copy, figure, flip ) => `
<section class="block${flip ? ' flip' : ''}" id="${id}" aria-labelledby="${id}-h">
	<div class="copy rv">
		<h2 class="name" id="${id}-h">${name}</h2>
		${copy}
	</div>
	${figure}
</section>`

		<p>Game Capture records only the game, so add Scorpio as a <b>Browser source</b>.</p>
		<ol class="steps">
			<li>In Scorpio, on the System tab, switch on <b>Show the crosshair in OBS</b>.</li>
			<li>In OBS, add a Browser source above your game capture.</li>
			<li>Set its URL to <code>http://127.0.0.1:47117/obs</code>.</li>
			<li>Make it the size of your canvas, with no transform.</li>
		</ol>`, shot( 'obs' ) )}

<section class="block wide" id="safety" aria-labelledby="safety-h">
	<h2 class="name" id="safety-h">Outside the game</h2>
	<p class="lead">Scorpio never opens another process, reads or writes its memory, injects code, installs a hook, sends synthetic input, loads a driver, or draws into the game's frames.</p>
	<table class="tbl obj">
		<thead><tr>${HEAD.map( h => `<th scope="col">${h}</th>` ).join( '' )}</tr></thead>
		<tbody>
${CALLS.map( r => `\t\t\t<tr>${r.map( ( c, i ) => `<td${i === 1 || i === 2 ? ` data-h="${HEAD[ i ]}"` : ''}>${c}</td>` ).join( '' )}</tr>` ).join( '\n' )}
		</tbody>
	</table>
	<div class="faq obj" id="faq">
${FAQ.map( ( [ q, a ] ) => `\t\t<details class="qa"><summary>${q}<i aria-hidden="true"></i></summary><div class="a"><p>${a}</p></div></details>` ).join( '\n' )}
	</div>
</section>
${block( 'download', 'Download', `
		<ol class="steps">
			<li>On Windows 10 or 11, run the <code>-setup.exe</code>. It needs no administrator rights; it is unsigned, so SmartScreen may warn you the first time.</li>
			<li>On Linux with X11 or XWayland, run the <code>.AppImage</code>, or <code>sudo apt install ./Scorpio_*.deb</code>.</li>
			<li>Click the tray icon, paste a share code, and switch to your game.</li>
		</ol>
		<p>Free for non-commercial use under the <b>PolyForm Noncommercial 1.0.0</b> licence. The source is on GitHub.</p>
		<a class="go" href="${DOWNLOAD}">Open the latest release</a>`, shot( 'controls' ), true )}
</main>

<footer>
	<div class="foot">
		<span class="sig">${mark( 'Scorpio' )}Scorpio</span>
		<nav aria-label="Links">
			<a class="go" href="${REPO}">GitHub</a>
			<a class="go" href="${REPO}/blob/main/LICENSE">Licence</a>
			<a class="go" href="https://lxxvll.com">LXXVII</a>
		</nav>
		<span class="year" title="2026">MMXXVI</span>
	</div>
</footer>
<script>${js}</script>
</body>
</html>
`
fs.writeFileSync( OUT, html )
console.log( OUT, html.length )
```

`tools/site/deploy.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Builds the website and copies it to the web server.
# Usage: SCORPIO_SITE_HOST=<ssh host> tools/site/deploy.sh
# The server serves /var/www/scorpio as https://scorpio.lxxvll.com (Caddy).
set -eu
: "${SCORPIO_SITE_HOST:?set SCORPIO_SITE_HOST to the ssh host of the web server}"
cd "$(dirname "$0")/../.."
node tools/site/build.js
# tar over ssh: works with any rsync (or none) on either side.
COPYFILE_DISABLE=1 tar --no-xattrs -C docs/site --exclude '._*' --exclude '.DS_Store' -czf - . |
	ssh "$SCORPIO_SITE_HOST" 'set -e; new=$(mktemp -d /var/www/.scorpio-XXXXXX); tar -xzf - -C "$new"; chmod -R u=rwX,go=rX "$new"; rm -rf /var/www/scorpio.old; mv /var/www/scorpio /var/www/scorpio.old; mv "$new" /var/www/scorpio; rm -rf /var/www/scorpio.old'
echo "deployed to https://scorpio.lxxvll.com/"

```

```css
/* Scorpio's settings window: layout only. Every colour, size, shape and
```

`ui/app.js`:

```js
// The settings window. Local state is the truth while the window is open;
// every change is saved through `save_settings`, which returns the
// validated settings. Changes made elsewhere (shortcuts) arrive as events.
'use strict'

const { h, setting, toggle, segmented, select, slider, colorButton, keybind, dialog, toast } = window.ui
const tauri = window.__TAURI__
const invoke = ( name, args ) => tauri.core.invoke( name, args )

const S = {
	tab: 'crosshair',
	view: null,
	settings: null,
	status: null,
	refused: [],
	target: 'main',
	zoom: 1,
	savedPick: null,
	pros: [],
}

const clone = value => JSON.parse( JSON.stringify( value ) )

/* Saving */

let saveTimer = 0
let lastSaved = ''

const save = ( { now = false } = {} ) => {

	clearTimeout( saveTimer )
	const run = async () => {

		const { settings, refused, obsError } = await invoke( 'save_settings', { settings: S.settings } )

		lastSaved = JSON.stringify( settings )
		S.refused = refused
		if ( obsError ) {

			S.settings.obs = false
			toast( `The OBS source could not start. ${obsError}`, { alert: true } )
			if ( S.tab === 'system' ) render()

		}
		if ( refused.length ) {

			toast( 'Those keys are taken, by another program or another shortcut. Choose different keys.', { alert: true } )
			if ( S.tab === 'controls' ) render()

		}

	}

	if ( now ) return run()
	saveTimer = setTimeout( run, 150 )

}

/* The crosshair being edited: the main one or a game's own */

const targetGame = () => ( S.target === 'main' ? null : S.settings.games[S.target] || null )

const editing = () => {

	const game = targetGame()

	return game?.crosshair || S.settings.crosshair

}

const change = mutate => {

	mutate( editing() )
	paintPreview()
	save()

}

/* Preview */

let previewFrame = 0
let previewAsked = 0

const paintPreview = () => {

	cancelAnimationFrame( previewFrame )
	previewFrame = requestAnimationFrame( async () => {

		const well = document.getElementById( 'preview' )

		if ( !well ) return
		// Answers can arrive out of order; only the newest is drawn.
		const asked = ++previewAsked
		const svg = await invoke( 'preview', { crosshair: editing() } )

		if ( asked !== previewAsked ) return
		well.innerHTML = svg
		sizeSvg( well.firstElementChild, S.zoom )
		const code = document.getElementById( 'code' )

		if ( code && document.activeElement !== code ) code.value = await invoke( 'write_code', { crosshair: editing() } )

	} )

}

// SVG units are physical pixels: at 1x the preview is exactly what the
// overlay draws on this screen.
const sizeSvg = ( svg, zoom ) => {

	if ( !svg ) return
	const box = svg.viewBox?.baseVal
	const width = box?.width || Number( svg.getAttribute( 'width' ) ) || 0
	const height = box?.height || Number( svg.getAttribute( 'height' ) ) || 0
	const scale = zoom / window.devicePixelRatio

	svg.setAttribute( 'width', String( width * scale ) )
	svg.setAttribute( 'height', String( height * scale ) )
	svg.style.shapeRendering = zoom === 1 ? 'crispEdges' : 'auto'

}

const thumb = async ( crosshair, node ) => {

	node.innerHTML = await invoke( 'preview', { crosshair } )
	const svg = node.firstElementChild

	if ( !svg ) return
	const box = svg.viewBox?.baseVal
	const size = Math.max( box?.width || 1, box?.height || 1 )
	const fit = Math.min( 1, 64 / size )

	svg.setAttribute( 'width', String( ( box?.width || size ) * fit / window.devicePixelRatio * window.devicePixelRatio ) )
	svg.setAttribute( 'height', String( ( box?.height || size ) * fit ) )

}

/* Status in the title bar */

const paintStatus = () => {

	const node = document.getElementById( 'status' )
	const s = S.status

	if ( !node || !s ) return
	// The words say whether the crosshair is shown, so there is no dot beside them (rules.md R16).
	let text = 'Hidden until a game is in front'

	if ( !s.locked ) text = 'Position unlocked: drag the crosshair, then lock it'
	else if ( s.hidden ) text = 'Hidden by shortcut'
	else if ( s.visible ) text = s.game ? `Shown over ${s.game}` : 'Shown over a detected game'

	node.textContent = text

}

/* Tabs */

const crosshairTab = () => {

	const c = editing()
	const styles = S.view.styles.map( ( [ id, name ] ) => [ id, name ] )
	const ownGames = S.settings.games.map( ( g, i ) => [ i, g ] ).filter( ( [ , g ] ) => g.crosshair )
	const targets = [ [ 'main', 'Main crosshair' ], ...ownGames.map( ( [ i, g ] ) => [ i, `${g.name}'s own crosshair` ] ) ]
	const outlineOff = c.outline === 0

	const code = h( 'input.input#code', { spellcheck: 'false', 'aria-label': 'Share code', placeholder: 'Paste a CS2 share code' } )

	code.addEventListener( 'change', async () => {

		const read = await invoke( 'read_code', { code: code.value } )

		if ( !read ) {

			code.setAttribute( 'aria-invalid', 'true' )
			toast( 'That is not a crosshair share code. They start with CSGO- or CS.', { alert: true } )

			return

		}

		code.setAttribute( 'aria-invalid', 'false' )
		const keep = editing().outlineWidth

		change( target => Object.assign( target, read.crosshair, { outlineWidth: keep } ) )
		render()
		toast( read.codeStyle !== read.crosshair.style ? 'Code imported. Its dynamic style is shown at rest.' : 'Code imported.' )

	} )

	const copy = h( 'button.btn', { type: 'button', text: 'Copy', onclick: async () => {

		await navigator.clipboard.writeText( await invoke( 'write_code', { crosshair: editing() } ) )
		toast( 'Share code copied. Paste it in CS2 or send it to a friend.' )

	} } )

	const zoom = segmented( [ [ 1, 'Actual size' ], [ 4, '4×' ] ], S.zoom, z => {

		S.zoom = z
		sizeSvg( document.getElementById( 'preview' ).firstElementChild, z )

	}, { label: 'Preview size' } )

	const left = h( 'section.stage', {},
		targets.length > 1 ? select( targets, S.target, t => {

			S.target = t
			render()

		}, { label: 'Crosshair to edit' } ) : null,
		h( 'div.well.cramps#preview', { 'aria-label': 'Crosshair preview' } ),
		h( 'div.stage-row', {}, zoom ),
		h( 'div.field', {}, h( 'label.field-label', { for: 'code', text: 'Share code' } ), h( 'div.code-row', {}, code, copy ),
			h( 'div.help', { text: 'Paste a code to import it. Your outline width stays, CS2 codes do not carry it.' } ) ),
	)

	const shape = setting( 'Style', null, select( styles, c.style, v => {

		change( t => {

			t.style = v

		} )
		render()

	}, { label: 'Style' } ) )

	const sizes = h( 'div.slab.flush', {},
		shape,
		setting( 'Length', null, slider( { min: 0, max: 40, value: c.length, label: 'Length', onInput: v => change( t => {

			t.length = v

		} ) } ), { stack: true } ),
		setting( 'Thickness', 'In half steps, finer than CS2.', slider( { min: 0, max: 10, step: 0.5, value: c.thickness, label: 'Thickness', format: v => v.toFixed( 1 ), onInput: v => change( t => {

			t.thickness = v

		} ) } ), { stack: true } ),
		setting( 'Gap', null, slider( { min: -10, max: 20, value: c.gap, label: 'Gap', onInput: v => change( t => {

			t.gap = v

		} ) } ), { stack: true } ),
		setting( 'Centre dot', null, toggle( c.dot, v => change( t => {

			t.dot = v

		} ), { label: 'Centre dot' } ) ),
		setting( 'T shape', 'Leaves out the top line.', toggle( c.tShape, v => change( t => {

			t.tShape = v

		} ), { label: 'T shape' } ) ),
	)

	const colour = h( 'div.slab.flush', {},
		setting( 'Colour', null, colorButton( c.color, v => change( t => {

			t.color = v

		} ), { label: 'Crosshair colour' } ) ),
		setting( 'Opacity', null, slider( { min: 0, max: 255, value: c.alpha, label: 'Opacity', format: v => `${Math.round( v / 2.55 )}%`, onInput: v => change( t => {

			t.alpha = v

		} ) } ), { stack: true } ),
	)

	const outline = h( 'div.slab.flush', {},
		setting( 'Outline', null, segmented( [ [ 0, 'None' ], [ 1, 'Full' ], [ 2, 'Top and left' ] ], c.outline, v => {

			change( t => {

				t.outline = v

			} )
			render()

		}, { label: 'Outline' } ) ),
		setting( 'Outline width', 'CS2 draws 1 pixel. Wider outlines stay in Scorpio.', slider( { min: 1, max: 6, value: c.outlineWidth, label: 'Outline width', format: v => `${v} px`, onInput: v => change( t => {

			t.outlineWidth = v

		} ) } ), { stack: true, off: outlineOff } ),
		setting( 'Outline colour', null, colorButton( c.outlineColor, v => change( t => {

			t.outlineColor = v

		} ), { label: 'Outline colour' } ), { off: outlineOff } ),
		setting( 'Outline opacity', null, slider( { min: 0, max: 255, value: c.outlineAlpha, label: 'Outline opacity', format: v => `${Math.round( v / 2.55 )}%`, onInput: v => change( t => {

			t.outlineAlpha = v

		} ) } ), { stack: true, off: outlineOff } ),
	)

	const right = h( 'section.controls', {},
		h( 'h2.t-h4', { text: 'Shape' } ), sizes,
		h( 'h2.t-h4', { text: 'Colour' } ), colour,
		h( 'h2.t-h4', { text: 'Outline' } ), outline,
		savedSection(),
		proSection(),
	)

	return h( 'div.crosshair-tab', {}, left, right )

}

const savedSection = () => {

	const saved = S.settings.saved
	const tiles = h( 'div.tiles' )

	saved.forEach( ( entry, index ) => {

		const preview = h( 'span.thumb' )

		thumb( entry.crosshair, preview )
		tiles.append( h( 'button.tile', {
			type: 'button',
			'aria-pressed': String( S.savedPick === index ),
			title: 'Use this crosshair',
			onclick: () => {

				S.savedPick = index
				change( t => Object.assign( t, clone( entry.crosshair ) ) )
				render()

			},
		}, preview, h( 'span', { text: entry.name } ) ) )

	} )

	const add = h( 'button.btn', { type: 'button', text: 'Save this crosshair', onclick: () => {

		const name = `Crosshair ${saved.length + 1}`

		saved.push( { name, crosshair: clone( editing() ) } )
		S.savedPick = saved.length - 1
		save()
		render()

	} } )

	const picked = saved[S.savedPick]
	const edit = picked ? h( 'div.saved-edit', {},
		h( 'input.input', { value: picked.name, 'aria-label': 'Name', maxlength: '48', oninput: event => {

			picked.name = event.target.value
			save()

		}, onchange: render } ),
		h( 'button.btn', { type: 'button', text: 'Update', title: 'Replace it with the crosshair shown now', onclick: () => {

			picked.crosshair = clone( editing() )
			save()
			render()
			toast( `${picked.name} updated.` )

		} } ),
		h( 'button.btn.danger', { type: 'button', text: 'Delete', onclick: () => {

			saved.splice( S.savedPick, 1 )
			S.savedPick = null
			save()
			render()

		} } ),
	) : null

	return h( 'div.saved', {},
		h( 'div.section-head', {}, h( 'h2.t-h4', { text: 'Your crosshairs' } ), add ),
		saved.length ? tiles : h( 'p.help', { text: 'Save a crosshair to switch back to it in one click, or with the next and previous shortcuts.' } ),
		edit,
	)

}

const proSection = () => {

	if ( !S.pros.length ) return null
	const tiles = h( 'div.tiles' )

	for ( const pro of S.pros ) {

		const preview = h( 'span.thumb' )

		invoke( 'read_code', { code: pro.code } ).then( read => {

			if ( read ) thumb( read.crosshair, preview )

		} )
		tiles.append( h( 'button.tile', { type: 'button', title: pro.team ? `${pro.name}, ${pro.team}` : pro.name, onclick: async () => {

			const read = await invoke( 'read_code', { code: pro.code } )

			if ( !read ) return
			const keep = editing().outlineWidth

			change( t => Object.assign( t, read.crosshair, { outlineWidth: keep } ) )
			render()
			toast( `${pro.name}'s crosshair applied.` )

		} }, preview, h( 'span', { text: pro.name } ) ) )

	}

	return h( 'div.saved', {}, h( 'div.section-head', {}, h( 'h2.t-h4', { text: 'Pro players' } ) ), tiles )

}

const gamesTab = () => {

	const games = S.settings.games
	const shortcut = S.settings.keys.addGame

	const rows = games.map( ( game, index ) => {

		const where = game.fingerprint.path || game.fingerprint.name || game.fingerprint.windowClass

		return h( 'div.game-row', {},
			h( 'div.game-name', {},
				h( 'input.input.inline', { value: game.name, 'aria-label': 'Game name', maxlength: '48', oninput: event => {

					game.name = event.target.value
					save()

				} } ),
				h( 'div.help.path', { text: where, title: where } ),
			),
			h( 'div.game-own', {},
				h( 'span.setting-name', { text: 'Own crosshair' } ),
				toggle( Boolean( game.crosshair ), on => {

					game.crosshair = on ? clone( S.settings.crosshair ) : null
					if ( !on && S.target === index ) S.target = 'main'
					save()
					render()

				}, { label: `${game.name} has its own crosshair` } ),
			),
			h( 'div.game-actions', {},
				game.crosshair ? h( 'button.btn', { type: 'button', text: 'Edit crosshair', onclick: () => {

					S.target = index
					go( 'crosshair' )

				} } ) : null,
				h( 'button.btn.quiet', { type: 'button', text: 'Remove', onclick: () => {

					games.splice( index, 1 )
					if ( S.target === index ) S.target = 'main'
					else if ( typeof S.target === 'number' && S.target > index ) S.target -= 1
					save()
					render()

				} } ),
			),
		)

	} )

	const add = h( 'button.btn.primary', { type: 'button', text: 'Add a game', onclick: pickWindow } )
	const list = games.length
		? h( 'div.slab.flush.games', {}, rows )
		: h( 'div.empty', {}, h( 'h3', { text: 'No games added yet' } ), h( 'p', { text: 'Add a game so the crosshair shows only while it is in front, with its own crosshair if you like.' } ) )

	return h( 'div.page', {},
		h( 'div.section-head', {}, h( 'h2.t-h1-app', { text: 'Games' } ), add ),
		shortcut ? h( 'p.help.lede', { text: `Or press ${shortcut.replaceAll( '+', ' + ' )} while the game is in front.` } ) : null,
		list,
		h( 'h2.t-h4', { text: 'Without adding' } ),
		h( 'div.slab.flush', {},
			setting( 'Fullscreen windows', 'Show the crosshair over any fullscreen or borderless window, except the desktop.', toggle( S.settings.detect.fullscreen, v => {

				S.settings.detect.fullscreen = v
				save()

			}, { label: 'Fullscreen windows' } ) ),
			setting( 'Games from launchers', S.view.installed
				? `Show the crosshair over the ${S.view.installed} games Steam, Epic, GOG and other launchers installed.`
				: 'Show the crosshair over games Steam, Epic, GOG and other launchers installed.', toggle( S.settings.detect.launchers, v => {

				S.settings.detect.launchers = v
				save()

			}, { label: 'Games from launchers' } ) ),
		),
	)

}

const pickWindow = async () => {

	const windows = await invoke( 'running_windows' )
	const list = h( 'div.window-list' )
	let close = () => {}

	if ( !windows.length ) list.append( h( 'p.help', { text: 'No windows found. Start the game, then try again.' } ) )
	for ( const w of windows ) {

		list.append( h( 'button.window-item', { type: 'button', onclick: async () => {

			const added = await invoke( 'add_window', { window: w } )

			close()
			if ( added ) {

				const fresh = await invoke( 'view' )

				S.settings = fresh.settings
				lastSaved = JSON.stringify( fresh.settings )
				render()
				toast( `${w.name || w.title} added.` )

			} else {

				toast( 'That game is already added.' )

			}

		} }, h( 'b', { text: w.name || w.windowClass } ), h( 'span', { text: w.title } ) ) )

	}

	close = dialog( 'Add a game', list, [ h( 'button.btn', { type: 'button', text: 'Cancel', onclick: () => close() } ) ] )

}

const BUTTONS = [ [ 'Right', 'Right button' ], [ 'Middle', 'Middle button' ], [ 'Back', 'Back side button' ], [ 'Forward', 'Forward side button' ], [ 'Left', 'Left button' ] ]
const SHORTCUTS = [
	[ 'lock', 'Unlock or lock position', 'Unlocked, drag the crosshair anywhere.' ],
	[ 'center', 'Centre exactly', 'Puts the crosshair back on the exact centre of the screen.' ],
	[ 'hide', 'Hide or show', null ],
	[ 'next', 'Next saved crosshair', null ],
	[ 'previous', 'Previous saved crosshair', null ],
	[ 'addGame', 'Add the game in front', null ],
]

const controlsTab = () => {

	const aim = S.settings.aim
	const off = !aim.hide

	return h( 'div.page', {},
		h( 'h2.t-h1-app', { text: 'Controls' } ),
		h( 'h2.t-h4', { text: 'Aiming' } ),
		h( 'div.slab.flush', {},
			setting( 'Hide while aiming', 'For scoped weapons. Scorpio reads the button the way every game does and sends nothing to the game.', toggle( aim.hide, v => {

				aim.hide = v
				save()
				render()

			}, { label: 'Hide while aiming' } ) ),
			setting( 'Aim button', null, select( BUTTONS, aim.button, v => {

				aim.button = v
				save()

			}, { label: 'Aim button' } ), { off } ),
			setting( 'When pressed', null, segmented( [ [ 'hold', 'Hide while held' ], [ 'toggle', 'Each press hides or shows' ] ], aim.mode, v => {

				aim.mode = v
				save()

			}, { label: 'Aim mode' } ), { off } ),
		),
		h( 'h2.t-h4', { text: 'Shortcuts' } ),
		h( 'p.help.lede', { text: 'Click a shortcut, then press the keys. Escape cancels, Backspace clears.' } ),
		h( 'div.slab.flush', {}, SHORTCUTS.map( ( [ id, name, help ] ) => {

			const refused = S.refused.includes( id )

			return setting( name, refused ? 'Taken, by another program or another shortcut.' : help, keybind( S.settings.keys[id], v => {

				S.settings.keys[id] = v
				S.refused = S.refused.filter( r => r !== id )
				save()

			}, { label: name, error: refused } ) )

		} ) ),
	)

}

const systemTab = () => {

	const s = S.settings
	const windows = S.view.platform === 'windows'
	const [ dx, dy ] = s.offset
	const centred = dx === 0 && dy === 0

	return h( 'div.page', {},
		h( 'h2.t-h1-app', { text: 'System' } ),
		h( 'h2.t-h4', { text: 'Position' } ),
		h( 'div.slab.flush', {},
			setting( 'Position', centred ? 'Exactly centred.' : `${Math.abs( dx )} px ${dx < 0 ? 'left' : 'right'} and ${Math.abs( dy )} px ${dy < 0 ? 'up' : 'down'} from the centre.`, h( 'div.inline-actions', {},
				h( 'button.btn', { type: 'button', text: S.status?.locked === false ? 'Lock' : 'Unlock', onclick: async () => {

					await invoke( 'set_locked', { locked: S.status?.locked === false } )

				} } ),
				h( 'button.btn', { type: 'button', text: 'Centre exactly', disabled: centred, onclick: () => {

					s.offset = [ 0, 0 ]
					save()
					render()

				} } ),
			) ),
		),
		h( 'h2.t-h4', { text: 'Scorpio' } ),
		h( 'div.slab.flush', {},
			setting( 'Start with the system', 'Scorpio waits in the tray and costs nothing until a game is in front.', toggle( s.startWithSystem, v => {

				s.startWithSystem = v
				save()

			}, { label: 'Start with the system' } ) ),
			setting( 'Hide from screen capture', windows
				? 'Screenshots, recordings and streams show the game without the crosshair. Cheat overlays hide from anti-cheat screenshots the same way, so an anti-cheat could take a closer look at Scorpio. Leave it off unless you need it.'
				: 'Not available on Linux: X11 has no way to keep a window out of captures.', toggle( s.hideFromCapture, v => {

				s.hideFromCapture = v
				save()

			}, { label: 'Hide from screen capture', disabled: !windows } ), { off: !windows } ),
		),
		h( 'h2.t-h4', { text: 'Stream' } ),
		obsBlock(),
		h( 'h2.t-h4', { text: 'Updates' } ),
		updatesBlock(),
		h( 'div.about', {},
			h( 'img.lockup', { src: 'brand/lockup.svg', alt: 'Scorpio' } ),
			h( 'p.t-h3', { text: `Version ${S.view.version}` } ),
			h( 'p.help', { text: 'A crosshair overlay. It draws its own window over the game and never opens, reads or changes the game or its anti-cheat.' } ),
			h( 'p.help', { text: 'Free for non-commercial use under the PolyForm Noncommercial licence 1.0.0. Made by LXXVII.' } ),
			h( 'button.btn.danger', { type: 'button', text: 'Quit Scorpio', onclick: () => invoke( 'quit_app' ) } ),
		),
	)

}

// OBS's game capture never contains the overlay window, so the crosshair
// reaches the stream as a Browser source served by Scorpio itself.
const obsBlock = () => {

	const on = S.settings.obs
	const address = S.view.obsAddress
	const rows = [
		setting( 'Show the crosshair in OBS', 'For Game Capture, which never includes the crosshair window. Scorpio serves the crosshair to OBS on this computer only; it shows and hides with yours.', toggle( on, v => {

			S.settings.obs = v
			save( { now: true } ).then( render )

		}, { label: 'Show the crosshair in OBS' } ) ),
	]

	if ( on ) {

		rows.push( h( 'div.setting.stack', {},
			h( 'div.setting-name', { text: 'In OBS' } ),
			h( 'ol.obs-steps', {},
				h( 'li', { text: 'Add a Browser source above your game capture.' } ),
				h( 'li', {}, 'Set its URL to the address below, and its width and height to your canvas size (Settings, Video, Base resolution).' ),
				h( 'li', { text: 'Keep it filling the whole canvas, untransformed.' } ),
			),
			h( 'div.code-row', {},
				h( 'input.input', { value: address, readonly: true, 'aria-label': 'Address for OBS', onfocus: event => event.target.select() } ),
				h( 'button.btn', { type: 'button', text: 'Copy', onclick: async () => {

					await navigator.clipboard.writeText( address )
					toast( 'Address copied. Paste it into the Browser source in OBS.' )

				} } ),
			),
		) )

	}

	return h( 'div.slab.flush', {}, rows )

}

const UPDATE_TEXT = {
	idle: () => 'Not checked yet.',
	checking: () => 'Checking for a new version.',
	upToDate: () => `Scorpio ${S.view.version} is the newest version.`,
	available: u => `Scorpio ${u.version} is out.`,
	downloading: u => `Downloading Scorpio ${u.version}: ${u.percent}%.`,
	installing: u => `Installing Scorpio ${u.version}. Scorpio restarts in a moment.`,
	failed: u => u.message,
	unsupported: () => 'This copy is updated by your package manager.',
}

const updatesBlock = () => {

	const u = S.update || { state: 'idle' }
	const unsupported = u.state === 'unsupported'
	const busy = [ 'checking', 'downloading', 'installing' ].includes( u.state )
	const offer = u.state === 'available'
	const button = h( `button.btn${offer ? '.primary' : ''}`, {
		type: 'button',
		text: offer ? 'Install and restart' : 'Check for updates',
		disabled: busy || unsupported,
		onclick: async () => {

			if ( offer ) return invoke( 'install_update' )
			S.update = await invoke( 'check_updates' )
			render()

		},
	} )

	return h( 'div.slab.flush', {},
		setting( 'Install updates automatically', unsupported ? null : 'Scorpio checks every few hours. An update installs after no game has been in front for three minutes, then Scorpio restarts.', toggle( !S.settings.updateManually, v => {

			S.settings.updateManually = !v
			save()

		}, { label: 'Install updates automatically', disabled: unsupported } ), { off: unsupported } ),
		setting( 'Version', ( UPDATE_TEXT[u.state] || UPDATE_TEXT.idle )( u ), button ),
	)

}

const TABS = { crosshair: crosshairTab, games: gamesTab, controls: controlsTab, system: systemTab }

const render = () => {

	const view = document.getElementById( 'view' )
	const scroll = view.scrollTop

	view.replaceChildren( TABS[S.tab]() )
	view.scrollTop = scroll
	if ( S.tab === 'crosshair' ) paintPreview()
	paintStatus()

}

const go = tab => {

	S.tab = tab
	for ( const t of document.querySelectorAll( '.tabs [role="tab"]' ) ) t.setAttribute( 'aria-selected', String( t.dataset.tab === tab ) )
	document.getElementById( 'view' ).scrollTop = 0
	render()

}

const start = async () => {

	const win = tauri.window.getCurrentWindow()

	document.getElementById( 'minimize' ).onclick = () => win.minimize()
	document.getElementById( 'maximize' ).onclick = () => win.toggleMaximize()
	document.getElementById( 'close' ).onclick = () => win.close()

	const tabs = Array.from( document.querySelectorAll( '.tabs [role="tab"]' ) )

	for ( const t of tabs ) t.addEventListener( 'click', () => go( t.dataset.tab ) )
	document.querySelector( '.tabs' ).addEventListener( 'keydown', event => {

		const step = { ArrowRight: 1, ArrowLeft: -1 }[event.key]

		if ( !step ) return
		const next = tabs[( tabs.indexOf( document.activeElement ) + step + tabs.length ) % tabs.length]

		next.focus()
		go( next.dataset.tab )

	} )

	S.view = await invoke( 'view' )
	S.settings = S.view.settings
	S.status = S.view.status
	S.update = S.view.update
	lastSaved = JSON.stringify( S.settings )
	S.pros = ( await invoke( 'pro_crosshairs' ) )?.players || []

	await tauri.event.listen( 'settings', ( { payload } ) => {

		const text = JSON.stringify( payload )

		if ( text === lastSaved ) return
		lastSaved = text
		S.settings = payload
		render()

	} )
	await tauri.event.listen( 'update', ( { payload } ) => {

		S.update = payload
		if ( S.tab === 'system' ) render()

	} )
	await tauri.event.listen( 'status', ( { payload } ) => {

		const lockChanged = S.status?.locked !== payload.locked

		S.status = payload
		paintStatus()
		if ( lockChanged && S.tab === 'system' ) render()

	} )

	render()

}

start()
```
